### [1] [Behavioral and Performance Indicators of Depression and Anxiety in Electronic Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04254)
*Arya VarastehNezhad,Fattaneh Taghiyareh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过Moodle LMS日志数据提取行为与表现指标，发现大学生抑郁与焦虑状态与时间活动模式、会话时长、作业提交提前量等行为特征显著相关，表明LMS数据可作为非诊断性的心理健康情境标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索学习管理系统中的行为与表现数据是否能够反映大学本科生的抑郁和焦虑状态，以期为早期识别学生心理健康问题提供非侵入性的数据支持。

Method: 采用定量观察设计，整合97名学生的LMS事件日志、学业记录及Beck抑郁量表-II和Beck焦虑量表自评得分；提取时间投入、会话结构、截止日期行为、页面刷新模式及导航等行为指标，采用描述性统计、独立样本t检验（Benjamini-Hochberg FDR校正）、效应量和Spearman相关分析。

Result: 多项行为指标与抑郁和焦虑显著相关：抑郁程度较高者表现出时间活动模式偏移、会话时长延长、作业提交提前量缩短；焦虑程度较高者则表现为时间投入集中和会话间差异增大。

Conclusion: 常规LMS数据可以提供与学生心理健康相关的有意义行为信号，有助于早期教育意识提升；但这些指标应作为情境性、非诊断性标记，而非临床评估的替代品。

Abstract: This study investigates whether behavioral and performance indicators derived from a Moodle-based learning management system are associated with university students' depression and anxiety in two undergraduate Computer Engineering courses. Using a quantitative observational design, LMS event logs, academic records, and self-reported Beck Depression Inventory-II and Beck Anxiety Inventory scores from 97 students were integrated. A broad set of behavioral and performance indicators spanning temporal engagement, session structure, deadline-related behavior, page-refresh patterns, and LMS navigation was extracted from raw event logs and analyzed using descriptive statistics, independent-samples t-tests with Benjamini-Hochberg FDR correction, effect sizes, and Spearman correlations; inventory scores were confirmed invariant by sex and academic year. Several indicators were significantly associated with depression and anxiety. Higher depression was associated with shifted temporal activity patterns, longer session durations, and shorter homework submission lead times, while higher anxiety was associated with concentrated temporal engagement and session-based differences. These findings suggest that routine LMS data can provide meaningful behavioral signals related to student well-being and may support earlier educational awareness of students who experience mental-health-related strain. At the same time, such indicators should be interpreted as contextual and non-diagnostic markers rather than as substitutes for clinical assessment.

</details>


### [2] [Creative Reading: Scaffolding Reading for Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04308)
*Sophia Liu,Sarah Abowitz,Yijun Liu,Sarah Sterman,Shm Garanganao Almeda,Max Kreminski*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文批评了当前阅读增强系统将阅读简化为信息传输的倾向，提出了"创意阅读"作为替代目标，通过融合文学叙事理论与学术意义建构，设计以问题为导向的设计空间，重视阅读过程中的多元解读和读者自我转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阅读增强系统虽解决了时间和认知负荷问题，但将阅读视为"可丢弃的信息传输"，将解释权委托给机器，改变了阅读的本质和结果。学术阅读中，判断文本含义及其重要性是学术生产的核心工作，现有系统未能支持这一过程。

Method: 将文学叙事理论与学术意义建构及创造力支持相结合，提出以问题为导向（provocation-oriented）的设计空间，旨在支持读者在阅读中创造多元解读并塑造自身作为读者的身份。

Result: 提出了"创意阅读"这一替代目标框架，以及相应的设计空间概念，强调阅读过程的价值在于保留解读的多元性和读者的时间性转变。

Conclusion: 阅读增强系统应将阅读视为主动的、创造性的意义建构过程，而非被动的信息接收；系统的设计应以激发读者的思考与多元解读为目标，支持读者在阅读过程中的自我转变。

Abstract: Reading augmentation systems increasingly help readers process text at scale. While these tools address real constraints of time and cognitive load, they often implicitly frame reading as information transmission, or "reading to discard," delegating interpretation and effort to the machine. Yet this delegation changes the outcome of reading. For example, in scholarly reading, deciding what a research text implies and why it matters is central to the work of scholarly production. We propose creative reading as an alternative goal: reading augmentation that supports readers in creating both readings and themselves as readers. By putting literary and narrative theories into conversation with scholarly sensemaking and creativity support, we present a provocation-oriented design space for valuing the process of reading as a way of preserving a plurality of readings and transforming readers over time.

</details>


### [3] [Speculating the Impacts of Mediated Social Touch Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04489)
*Russian,Wu,Tim Moesgen,Myung Jin,Kim,Xinyan Yu,Naoki Kameyama,Anusha Withana,Marius Hoggenmueller,Luke Hespanhol*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文使用“未来涟漪”方法，通过三个工作坊与24名参与者（潜在用户、领域专家、触觉研究人员）集体探讨了中介社交触觉（MST）技术的更广泛社会技术影响，识别出四个主题和三个独特挑战，为触觉研究人员在RSR流程中提供了干预点和设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦MST的直接益处（如减少孤独感、提供情感支持），但对其更广泛的社会技术影响关注不足，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用“未来涟漪”（Future Ripples）方法，组织三个工作坊，邀请24名参与者（包括潜在用户、领域专家和触觉研究人员），共同设想可能的未来场景、机遇与威胁，并提出应对措施，随后对结果进行定性分析。

Result: 定性分析将见解归纳为四个主题和三个独特挑战，为触觉研究人员提供了跨越RSR流程的干预点，以及将“未来涟漪”方法应用于MST技术的方法论见解。

Conclusion: 本文为MST技术的负责任设计提供了前瞻性框架，帮助研究人员在技术开发早期识别并应对潜在的社会技术风险，同时也展示了“未来涟漪”方法在触觉交互领域的适用性。

Abstract: With growing research on haptic interfaces, Mediated Social Touch (MST) technologies offer the potential to record, synthesise, and reproduce (RSR) touch experiences across space and time, enabling, for instance, a hug from afar and from the past. Although much of the existing research highlights the direct benefits of these systems, such as reducing loneliness and providing emotional support, little attention has been paid to their broader sociotechnical impacts. To address this gap, we used the Future Ripples method to speculate on possible effects of MST. We conducted three workshops with 24 participants, including potential users, domain experts, and haptics researchers. Throughout these sessions, participants collectively envisioned possible future scenarios, alongside opportunities and threats, and proposed actionable responses. Our qualitative analysis organised these insights into four themes and three distinctive challenges. These findings offer haptics researchers intervention points across the RSR pipeline to inform MST design, alongside methodological insights from applying Future Ripples to MST technology.

</details>


### [4] [Addressing Negative Commons Governance with Positive Commons Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04563)
*Boyang Zhou,Oleg Ianchenko*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过分析全球电子废物贸易和Linux内核社区两个案例，评估了奥斯特罗姆的八项公共池资源治理设计原则是否也适用于负公共池资源（NCPR）治理。研究发现，这八项原则在成功的负公共品治理系统中同样存在，并建议未来的NCPR治理设计应优先考虑这些原则，特别是明确界定的边界和运作良好的嵌套结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验奥斯特罗姆的公共池资源治理原则能否从传统正公共品（如清洁水源）扩展到负公共品（如电子废物、有缺陷的代码贡献），探索负公共品治理的有效框架。

Method: 对全球电子废物贸易和Linux内核社区两个领域进行定性分析，基于第一手资料，对照奥斯特罗姆八项设计原则进行案例评估。

Result: 奥斯特罗姆的八项设计原则同样出现在成功的负公共品治理系统中，与正公共品治理中的表现一致。

Conclusion: 未来负公共池资源治理设计应优先采用奥斯特罗姆的原则，尤其是明确界定的边界和运作良好的嵌套结构。

Abstract: Computing is accompanied by both positive and negative commons throughout its lifecycle of creation, execution, and disposal. We examine two governance systems situated within this lifecycle -- global e-waste trade and the Linux kernel community -- to evaluate whether Elinor Ostrom's eight design principles for common-pool resource (CPR) governance extend to the management of negative common-pool resources (NCPRs). Unlike traditional CPRs where communities work to preserve a finite resource (i.e. clean water), NCPR governance seeks to collectively reduce a negative shared stock. In our two cases, e-waste governance aims to reduce the volume of mismanaged waste and illicit trade, while the Linux community aims to reduce the number of error-prone or malicious contributions that reach the main branch and, in turn, extend the life of existing hardware. Through qualitative analysis of primary sources from each domain, we find that the same eight principles by Ostrom that aid positive commons governance tend to appear in successful negative commons governance systems. We argue that future NCPR governance design should prioritize Ostrom's principles, particularly clearly defined boundaries and well-functioning nested structures.

</details>

### [5] [GraftDB: Dynamic Folding of Concurrent Analytical Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04303)
*Genki Kimura,Kazuo Goda*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GraftDB提出了一种动态多查询执行引擎，通过状态中心化执行和查询嫁接机制，将后续到达的查询动态折叠到正在运行的执行中，实现工作复用和共享，从而减少分析型数据库中的冗余执行，在TPC-H负载下吞吐量提升至2.17倍，P95响应时间降至0.17倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析型数据库系统在处理随时间到达的并发分析查询时，不同查询之间往往存在重叠计算，但传统独立执行方式会导致大量冗余执行工作。如何利用这种重叠来优化整体查询处理，是一个关键的 technical challenge。

Method: GraftDB包含三个核心机制：(1) 状态中心化执行（state-centric execution），将算子执行过程中积累的状态视为共享状态而非单一查询私有；(2) 每查询状态透镜（per-query state lens），让每个查询只能观察到在其语义下有效的那部分状态；(3) 查询嫁接（query grafting），为新到达查询识别已有的可复用状态以及仍可共享的后续工作。

Result: 基于TPC-H衍生的动态并发工作负载实验表明：GraftDB相比同引擎的独立执行基线，吞吐量最高提升2.17倍；在过载开放到达场景下，P95响应时间降至基线的0.17倍。

Conclusion: GraftDB通过动态折叠查询和共享执行状态，有效减少了分析查询中的冗余执行工作，显著提升了系统吞吐量和响应时间，验证了状态中心化执行方法在多查询优化场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Analytical database systems serve as foundational infrastructure for knowledge discovery across many domains. Day after day, researchers, practitioners, and increasingly AI-driven agents issue analytical queries, inspect their results, and refine their inquiries. An analytical database system thus receives and processes diverse analytical queries that arrive over time and execute concurrently. Such workloads can create redundant execution work across independently issued queries. Exploiting this overlap to optimize query processing as a whole is a critical technical challenge. This paper presents GraftDB, a multi-query execution engine that dynamically folds a later-arriving query into a running execution, reusing previously performed work and sharing subsequently performed work. GraftDB achieves dynamic folding with state-centric execution, which treats operator state accumulated during execution not as owned by a single query, but as shared state that any compatible query can observe or contribute to. Each query observes shared state through a per-query state lens, which lets the query observe that state only after the relevant input has been incorporated and receive only rows or state fragments valid under the query's semantics. For an arriving query, query grafting identifies operator state that already satisfies part of the query's requirements and work that can still be shared to satisfy the rest. Together, these mechanisms let GraftDB share work across overlapping analytical queries and reduce redundant execution work. Experiments using TPC-H-derived instances of dynamic concurrent workloads show that GraftDB achieves up to 2.17 times higher throughput than a same-engine isolated-execution baseline. Under overloaded open-loop arrivals, GraftDB reduces P95 response time to as low as 0.17 times the same baseline's P95 response time.

</details>


### [6] [Selectivity Estimation for Semantic Filters on Image Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04610)
*Matthias Urban,Vu Huy Nguyen,Gabriele Sanmartino,Paolo Papotti,Carsten Binnig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出Semantic Histograms，一种利用共享嵌入空间替代在线采样分析的语义查询选择性估计方法，将端到端查询优化与执行开销降低高达86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义数据系统依赖在线采样分析来优化查询，但该方法延迟开销大，且难以处理低选择性查询。

Method: 利用语义过滤器实质上是隐式范围查询这一洞察，提出两种估计查询特异性的方法，并以集成方式取得最佳效果。

Result: 端到端查询优化与执行运行时开销降低高达86%。

Conclusion: Semantic Histograms通过绕过传统采样分析，有效解决了语义查询优化中的选择性估计难题，显著提升效率。

Abstract: Semantic data systems integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) directly into database query execution, enabling expressive queries on multi-modal data. However, optimizing these queries requires accurate selectivity estimates to determine the most efficient operator execution order. Contemporary systems rely on online sample-based profiling, a process that incurs severe latency overheads and struggles with low-selectivity queries. In this paper, we introduce Semantic Histograms, a novel selectivity estimator for semantic filters on image data that leverages shared embedding spaces to bypass traditional profiling. We realize that all semantic filters are implicit range queries, as they match a range of different images. Some filter predicates are more general, yielding a wide range, while others are more specific, yielding a smaller range. To address the challenge of implicit ranges, we propose two approaches to estimate the queries' specificity, with an ensemble of the two performing best. The evaluation shows that Semantic Histograms can reduce the end-to-end runtime overhead of query optimization and execution by up to 86%.

</details>


### [7] [Indexicon: A Spatial Indexing Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04676)
*Panagiotis Simatis,Panagiotis Bouros,Nikos Mamoulis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Indexicon是一个统一、高度可移植、可扩展的开源空间索引库，提供基于树的纯内存空间访问方法（R-tree、Quad-tree变体、KD-tree），以单文件、仅头文件、零依赖的C++模板形式实现，具备统一接口和全面的性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源空间索引库普遍存在索引类型单一、依赖复杂、关键功能缺失、API不一致以及对空间数据类型支持受限等问题，严重阻碍了空间访问方法的研究、比较与基准测试。

Method: 设计并实现Indexicon库：采用单文件、仅头文件、零外部依赖的C++模板架构，每个索引独立封装；提供统一接口，原生支持批量加载、动态插入/删除、范围查询、k近邻搜索和结构统计追踪。

Result: 在6个真实地理数据集上，与Boost Geometry、PCL、Nanoflann等成熟实现对比，Indexicon的轻量设计在性能上匹配或超越现有最先进实现，同时具备更高的架构灵活性。

Conclusion: Indexicon通过统一、轻量、零依赖的设计有效降低了空间索引的研究门槛，促进可复现的空间研究；已将完整库、数据集和查询负载开源。

Abstract: Spatial indexing is foundational to Geographic Information Systems (GIS) and multi-dimensional data management, yet the current open-source landscape poses a significant barrier to research that employs or benchmarks spatial access methods. We observe that most of the existing open-source libraries include a single index. Some are hindered by complex dependencies, missing critical functionalities, inconsistent APIs, and rigid constraints regarding the support of spatial data types. To address this issue, we introduce Indexicon: a unified, highly portable, extendable, open-source spatial indexing library, designed specifically for rapid integration and ease of modification of main-memory spatial access methods. Indexicon provides a comprehensive suite of popular tree-based spatial access methods, including the R-tree, Quad-tree variants, and the KD-tree. Each structure is meticulously implemented as a self-contained, single-file, header-only C++ template with zero external dependencies beyond the standard library. Crucially, every index features a uniform interface, natively supporting bulk-loading, dynamic insertions/deletions, range queries, $k$-nearest neighbor ($k$NN) search, and structural statistics tracking. We also present an extensive performance evaluation of Indexicon against well-established and widely used implementations of these structures (including Boost Geometry, PCL, and Nanoflann) across six real-world geographic datasets. Our results demonstrate that Indexicon's lightweight design matches or outperforms existing state-of-the-art implementations while offering unmatched architectural flexibility. To foster reproducible spatial research, we open-source the complete library alongside our datasets and query workloads.

</details>


### [8] [GraphAlg Playground: An Online Platform for Learning and Experimenting with the GraphAlg Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04813)
*Daan de Graaf,Robert Brijder,Soham Chakraborty,George Fletcher,Bram van de Wall,Nikolay Yakovets*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GraphAlg语言的Web端交互式游乐场，支持用户在浏览器中编写和执行图算法程序，包含交互式教程，无需安装即可使用，面向新手和专家用户分别提供学习和原型验证功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在数据库中实现对用户自定义图分析工作负载的原生支持，需要一种专门的图算法语言及其易用的执行环境。

Method: 开发了一个基于Web浏览器的GraphAlg游乐场，包含交互式教程，支持在线编写和执行GraphAlg程序，并以可复用库的形式在宽松许可下免费提供。

Result: 实现了无需安装即可在浏览器中运行的图算法编程环境，并设计了面向新手用户的学习教程和面向专家用户的算法原型验证两种使用场景。

Conclusion: 该Web游乐场降低了GraphAlg语言的使用门槛，既便于新手学习，也便于专家快速验证图算法，促进了图分析工作负载在数据库中的落地。

Abstract: The GraphAlg language for graph algorithms enables native support for user-defined graph analytics workloads in databases. In this demonstration, we present a web-based playground for writing and executing GraphAlg programs in the web browser, including an interactive tutorial explaining its key concepts. The playground runs inside the user's web browser without any installation, and is freely available under a permissive license as a reusable library. We present two demonstration scenarios of the publicly available playground website, showing how new users can learn to program in GraphAlg using the tutorial, while expert users can use the playground to prototype and validate their algorithms.

</details>

### [9] [Functional Interface Blocks for Neuromorphic Hardware: A Junction-Centered Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04281)
*Wellington Avelino,Yann Beillard,Fabien Allibart,Dominique Drouin,Gilberto Medeiros-Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 针对异构神经形态硬件中器件间功能兼容性问题，提出了一种以连接点为中心的接口框架，通过定义驱动/感知角色并组织成规范功能块，采用第二代电流传输器（CCII）实现，并在结合忆阻突触与单结晶体管（UJT）后神经元的巴甫洛夫条件反射演示器上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构神经形态硬件集成了具有不同电气特性和动态特性的器件，其互连处的功能兼容性是主要设计挑战。直接耦合因负载线条件无法确保正确操作。

Method: 提出以连接点为中心的接口框架，为器件间连接分配驱动/感知角色，组织成规范功能接口块；采用第二代电流传输器（CCII）作为复合实现方案。

Result: 在结合忆阻突触与UJT后神经元的巴甫洛夫条件反射演示器上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 通过将局部连接条件与可重用接口功能关联，为异构神经形态系统的设计与分析提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: Heterogeneous neuromorphic hardware integrates devices with dissimilar electrical characteristics and dynamics, making functional compatibility at their interconnections a primary design challenge. Direct coupling alone is insufficient to ensure correct operation, because the load-line conditions established at each junction determine the effective operating regime. Here, we propose a junction-centered interface framework in which inter-device connections are described through assigned drive/sense roles and organized into canonical functional interface blocks. As a concrete hardware realization, a second-generation current conveyor (CCII)-based implementation is then adopted as a composite realization of these interface primitives. The framework is validated experimentally in a Pavlovian-conditioning demonstrator combining a memristive synapse with a unijunction-transistor (UJT) post-neuron. By linking local junction conditions to reusable interface functions, the proposed methodology provides a systematic basis for the design and analysis of heterogeneous neuromorphic systems.

</details>


### [10] [ThermoPix: A High-Spatial-Resolution ElectronicPhotonic Temperature Sensor Array With Microsecond Row Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04902)
*Md Rahatul Islam Udoy,Dharanidhar Dang,Wantong Li,Sumeet Kumar Gupta,Suman Datta,Ahmedullah Aziz*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出ThermoPix，一种CMOS兼容的电子-光子混合架构，用于高空间分辨率温度传感。系统通过谷光子晶体马赫-曾德尔干涉仪(VPCMZI)将温度引起的光谱偏移转换为时序信息，再由CMOS电路处理。电路级仿真显示温度灵敏度3.15 ns/K，行读出时间2 μs，传感功耗延迟积0.152 fJ，每光像元所需光功率150 nW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高空间分辨率、CMOS兼容、可扩展且节能的集成温度传感阵列，无需冷却或特殊环境安排，解决传统温度传感器在分辨率、功耗和集成度方面的局限性。

Method: 采用谷光子晶体MZI作为传感单元，集成波导光电探测器检测温度依赖的光谱响应，将其转换为随时间变化的光电流；CMOS读出电路利用相变材料器件进行阈值检测，生成与温度相关的时序信号。光功率分配架构采用两种方案：不同抽头比沿行分布，或相同抽头比结合双向激励。

Result: 温度灵敏度3.15 ns/K，行读出时间2 μs，传感PDP为0.152 fJ，每像元光功率150 nW；两种架构的平均光像元间距分别为23.26 μm和38.52 μm。

Conclusion: ThermoPix架构为集成温度传感阵列提供了可扩展的平台，将光子传感元件与CMOS兼容的基于时序的读出电路相结合，实现了高空间分辨率、低功耗的能量高效阵列操作。

Abstract: This paper presents ThermoPix, a CMOS-compatible electronic-photonic architecture for high-spatial-resolution temperature sensing. The proposed system converts temperature-induced wavelength shifts in a photonic interferometric sensor into timing information that can be processed by CMOS circuitry. We use a valley photonic crystal Mach-Zehnder interferometer (VPCMZI) as the sensing element, whose temperature-dependent spectral response is detected using an integrated waveguide photodetector and translated into a time-varying photocurrent. A CMOS readout circuit employing a phase-transition-material device performs threshold detection and generates a timing signal corresponding to the temperature-dependent crossing event. Circuit-level simulations demonstrate a temperature sensitivity of 3.15 ns/K, a row readout time of 2 us, and a sensing power-delay product (PDP) of 0.152 fJ. The required optical power per photonic cell is 150 nW, enabling energy-efficient array operation without requiring cooling or special environmental arrangements. We also present alternative photonic layer architectures for optical power distribution across the array. In one approach, we use different tap ratios along the row, while the other uses identical tap ratios with bidirectional excitation. The resulting average photonic cell pitches are 23.26 um and 38.52 um, respectively. The proposed ThermoPix architecture therefore provides a scalable platform for integrated temperature sensing arrays that combine photonic sensing elements with CMOS-compatible timing-based readout.

</details>

### [11] [Climbing Up the Semantic Tower -- at Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04034)
*François-René Rideau*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为"一等实现"（first-class implementations）的反射协议，通过形式化规范的计算内容提取，实现了在运行时沿语义塔向上导航的能力，从而调和了语义学与运行时反射这两个此前互斥的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时系统只能沿语义塔向下（从抽象到具体）移动，而无法在运行时向上回溯到更高抽象层级。作者希望弥补这一空白，使计算在运行时能够观察和改变其自身的高层实现。

Method: 作者从实现的形式化规范中提取计算内容，构建了"一等实现"反射协议。核心概念是广义化的"安全点"（safe points），它允许在比计算实际运行层级更高的抽象层级上观察计算，从而实现沿语义塔向上攀爬。

Result: 成功调和了形式语义学与运行时反射这两个此前被认为互斥的领域，证明了可以从语义规范中导出实用的反射机制。

Conclusion: 一等实现协议提供了一种在运行时动态切换计算底层实现的能力，通过形式化方法确保了语义的严谨性，为编程语言的可演化性和运行时自适应系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Software exists at multiple levels of abstraction, where each more concrete level is an implementation of the more abstract level above, in a semantic tower of compilers and/or interpreters. First-class implementations are a reflection protocol to navigate this tower *at runtime*: they enable changing the underlying implementation of a computation *while it is running*. Key is a generalized notion of *safe points* that enable observing a computation at a higher-level than that at which it runs, and therefore to climb up the semantic tower, when at runtime most existing systems only ever allow but to go further down. The protocol was obtained by extracting the computational content of a formal specification for implementations and some of their properties. This approach reconciles two heretofore mutually exclusive fields: Semantics and Runtime Reflection.

</details>

### [12] [MaskForge: Structure-Aware Adaptive Attacks for Jailbreaking Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04027)
*Yingzi Ma,Zhengyue Zhao,Xiaogeng Liu,Minhui Xue,Yue Zhao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种针对扩散大语言模型(dLLM)的黑盒自适应攻击方法MaskForge，通过可复用模式库和UCB bandit选择器实现高效越狱，在五个公开模型上平均攻击成功率达79.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型因其双向上下文迭代去噪的生成机制，与自回归LLM存在本质不同的安全暴露面。现有越狱攻击要么忽略了dLLM的原生文本填充能力，要么依赖低多样性的掩码模板，缺乏结构自适应性和攻击经验的积累，亟需一种专门针对dLLM特性的高效攻击方法。

Method: MaskForge将dLLM的红队测试建模为对增长结构模式库的优化搜索。核心组件包括：(1) 模式抽象：将成功的攻击尝试提炼为可复用的结构化模式；(2) UCB bandit选择器：从模式库中选取与目标最兼容的模式；(3) 评分器引导的fallback机制：当当前库攻击失败时生成候选模式；(4) 经验蒸馏：将成功攻击回馈至模式库，实现跨目标的经验积累。

Result: 在五个公开dLLM和三个基准上，MaskForge平均攻击成功率为79.3%，相对最强基线提升17.6%。成熟后的模式库无需更新直接迁移至AdvBench，实现88.2%的攻击成功率，相对最强基线提升67%。

Conclusion: MaskForge证明了结构化模式库+自适应选择的攻击范式能有效利用dLLM的文本填充特性和经验积累，实现高效且可迁移的黑盒越狱，揭示了dLLM安全防御的紧迫性。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising partially masked sequences under bidirectional context, exposing a safety surface distinct from autoregressive LLMs. Because mask tokens are native inputs and tokens are committed by confidence rather than position, harmful content can be induced through infilling and outside the monitored prefix. Existing jailbreaks either miss this native infill capability or rely on low-diversity mask-bearing templates applied uniformly across goals, with little structural adaptation or accumulated attack experience. We propose MaskForge, a fully black-box adaptive attack that casts dLLM red-teaming as optimized search over a growing library of structural patterns. MaskForge abstracts successful attempts into reusable schemas, selects goal-compatible patterns with a UCB bandit, and invokes a scorer-guided fallback when the current library fails. Successful attempts are distilled back into the pattern library, enabling experience to accumulate across goals. Across five public dLLMs and three benchmarks, MaskForge achieves an average attack success rate of 79.3%, a 17.6% relative improvement over the strongest competing dLLM baseline. The matured pattern library further transfers to AdvBench without any updates, achieving a 88.2% attack success rate and a 67% relative improvement over the strongest competing baseline.

</details>


### [13] [Need to Know: Contextual-Integrity-Grounded Query Rewriting for Privacy-Conscious LLM Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04067)
*Xinyue Huang,Xiaochun Cao,Wenyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DelegateCI-Bench基准，并基于情境完整性（Contextual Integrity）构建强化学习框架，训练查询重写器在保留任务关键信息的同时抑制不必要的敏感信息披露，实现隐私与效用的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于类型的PII脱敏方法是上下文无关的，可能导致两类问题：过度暴露未标记的敏感上下文，以及过度移除包含答案的关键片段。需要一种任务感知的隐私保护重写方法。

Method: 将隐私保护查询重写重新定义为情境完整性（Contextual Integrity）问题；构建DelegateCI-Bench基准（3167样本，含合成数据、真实用户查询和医疗挑战集）；提出CI引导的强化学习框架，将必要与非必要敏感片段转化为可验证的优化信号，训练查询重写器。

Result: 学习到的重写器在隐私-效用权衡中达到最优，相比端侧基线平均效用提升最高达+10.1。

Conclusion: 基于情境完整性的任务感知重写方法比传统的类型化PII脱敏更有效，能够在保护隐私的同时保留任务关键信息。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly woven into everyday workflows, user queries sent to cloud hosted LLMs routinely mix task-essential content with task non-essential sensitive disclosures, yet type based PII redaction is context agnostic and may raise two issues: over disclosing untyped sensitive context and over removing answer bearing spans. We recast privacy preserving query rewriting under Contextual Integrity: a span should be forwarded only if it is necessary for the task. We introduce DelegateCI-Bench, the first task based Contextual Integrity benchmark for privacy-conscious delegation, comprising 3,167 samples that combine high quality synthetic data spanning 11 tasks and 20 task types, WildChat based real user queries, and a medical challenge set with dense sensitive information. Building on this benchmark, we propose a CI-guided reinforcement learning framework that converts essential and non-essential sensitive spans into verifiable optimization signals, and train a query rewriter to preserve task critical information while suppressing unnecessary sensitive disclosure. Experiments show that our learned rewriter achieves the best privacy-utility tradeoff, achieving up to +10.1 average utility over on-device baselines.

</details>


### [14] [Bayesian Membership Privacy for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04069)
*Sinan Yıldırım,Megha Khosla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯成员隐私（BMP）——一种考虑图采样概率和节点先验的节点级成员隐私形式化方法，将成员推断建模为贝叶斯假设检验，并提出采样感知的审计机制来量化GNN中的隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN隐私分析继承了非图场景的假设，忽略了结构相关性以及训练图采样的随机性。特别是节点相关的先验使得仅凭I型和II型错误不足以刻画最优成员推断测试，需要一种新的隐私定义来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 将成员推断重新定义为贝叶斯假设检验问题，引入节点相关先验信息，将图采样概率作为攻击者知识的一部分，以后验成员概率量化成员隐私，并提出采样感知的审计机制来估计BMP参数。

Result: 在基准图数据集上的实验表明，BMP能够提供全局攻击精度无法捕捉的细粒度隐私洞察。

Conclusion: BMP为GNN节点级成员隐私提供了一种更准确、更细粒度的分析框架，填补了现有定义在处理图结构特性和采样随机性方面的空白。

Abstract: Existing privacy analyses for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) largely inherit assumptions from non-graph settings, overlooking structural correlations and stochastic training-graph sampling. In particular, node-dependent priors make type-I and type-II errors alone insufficient to characterize the best membership inference test. To address this, we introduce Bayesian Membership Privacy (BMP), a sampling-aware formulation of node-level membership privacy that incorporates node-dependent priors and treats graph sampling probabilities as part of the adversary's knowledge. BMP casts membership inference as a Bayesian hypothesis test and accordingly quantifies membership privacy in terms of posterior membership probability. We explore theoretical properties of BMP in relation to the existing definitions in the literature. We further propose a practical, sampling-aware auditing mechanism to estimate the parameters of BMP as a measure of node-level privacy leakage in GNNs. We conduct experiments on benchmark graph datasets and show that BMP yields fine-grained privacy insights that are not visible through global attack accuracy alone.

</details>


### [15] [Formal verification of the S-two AIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04311)
*Jeremy Avigad,Anat Ganor,Lior Goldberg,David Levit,Ohad Nir,Yoav Seginer,Alon Titelman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用Lean 4证明助手验证了StarkWare的S-two证明系统中AIR编码的正确性，证明AIR的可满足性蕴含着对应的计算声明的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保StarkWare的S-two证明系统中AIR编码的可靠性，即证明AIR的可满足性能够真实反映Cairo程序的执行结果，从而保证区块链上程序验证的数学严谨性。

Method: 使用Lean 4证明助手对AIR编码进行形式化验证，通过数学证明建立AIR可满足性与计算声明之间的蕴含关系。

Result: 成功使用Lean 4验证了AIR编码的可靠性，即AIR的可满足性蕴含了对应的计算声明的正确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为StarkWare的S-two证明系统提供了形式化验证层面的可靠性保障，增强了基于Cairo语言的区块链程序验证的可信度。

Abstract: StarkWare's S-two prover provides an efficient means for establishing, on blockchain, that a program written in the Cairo virtual machine language runs to completion. The latter claim is encoded by an algebraic intermediate representation (AIR) that captures the semantics of the Cairo language. The AIR asserts the existence of tables of values from a finite field satisfying certain algebraic constraints. A cryptographic interactive proof system, circle STARK, provides an efficiently-checked certificate that the AIR is satisfied. We describe our verification, using the Lean 4 proof assistant, that the AIR encoding is sound, which is to say, the satisfiability of the AIR implies the computational claim.

</details>


### [16] [Covert Influence Between Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04071)
*Avidan Shah,Jay Chooi,Jinghua Ou,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型间通过不可见痕迹实现隐蔽影响的风险，分析了三种接口（监督微调、在线蒸馏、上下文学习）下影响传递的规模差异，并提出使用逐样本归因评分方法来放大或检测这种影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型越来越多地消费彼此的生成输出，隐蔽影响（即发送者的载荷通过人类无法检测的载体传递给接收者）成为一种日益增长的风险，需要系统地刻画和评估。

Method: 在三种接口（监督微调、在线蒸馏、上下文学习）下，利用推理时逐样本归因评分选择能够放大训练时影响的载体，研究隐蔽影响的可实现规模。

Result: 不同接口在实现隐蔽影响的可达到规模上存在差异；使用逐样本归因评分能够实现先前工作无法达到的载荷传递；自然语言载体与数字载体在隐蔽影响上表现不同，前者更不易被人类检测且跨模型家族可迁移性更差。

Conclusion: 隐蔽影响的风险面比先前认知的更广，逐样本归因评分方法可作为研究和缓解该威胁的有效工具。

Abstract: As language models increasingly consume one another's outputs, covert influence -- a phenomenon where a sender's payload (the behavioral disposition it is conditioned to propagate) transfers to a receiver through carriers undetectable by humans -- becomes a growing risk. We characterize this risk across three interfaces: supervised fine-tuning, on-policy distillation, and in-context learning, and find that they vary in the scale of influence achievable without leaving behind human-visible traces. Using inference-time per-sample attribution scores, we study covert influence across all three interfaces with the ability to select carriers that amplify training-time influence, unlocking payload transfers that prior work could not achieve. We further provide evidence that covert influence with natural-language carriers is a distinct phenomenon from prior studies using number carriers, as the latter is more resistant to human detection and less portable across model families. Together, these results suggest that the risk surface for covert influence is broader than previously recognized, and we study pointwise attribution scoring methods as a tool to investigate and mitigate it.

</details>


### [17] [Caught in the Act(ivation): Toward Pre-Output and Multi-Turn Detection of Credential Exfiltration by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04141)
*Kargi Chauhan,Pratibha Revankar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代理在凭证泄露方面的安全漏洞，提出了三种互补防御方法：激活探针检测、蜜令牌检测和累积信息流追踪。实验表明这些方法可以有效检测凭证泄露攻击，但结果仍属初步阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理常将敏感凭证与不可信检索内容置于同一上下文窗口，导致间接提示注入可直接引发凭证泄露，现有输出级文本过滤器不足以防御此类攻击。

Method: 1) 基于激活探针在输出token生成前检测凭证访问；2) 构建格式特异性蜜令牌并通过分裂共形预测校准检测；3) 将多轮泄露视为累积信息流问题，跨对话轮次追踪估计的泄漏预算。

Result: 激活特征在开放权重模型上能以高准确率区分善意与凭证窃取提示（包括未见过的编码变换）；累积记账方法能检测到逐轮检测器遗漏的攻击。

Conclusion: 凭证泄露防御应结合输出前监控、校准的蜜令牌检测和时域泄漏记账，而非仅依赖文本级输出过滤器。

Abstract: LLM agents often place sensitive credentials in the same context window as untrusted retrieved content, creating a direct path for indirect prompt injection to induce credential exfiltration. We study this failure mode through three complementary defenses. First, we ask whether activation probes can detect credential access before output tokens are emitted. Second, we construct honeytokens from format-specific character models and calibrate detection with split conformal prediction. Third, we treat multi-turn exfiltration as a cumulative information-flow problem and track an estimated leakage budget across conversation turns. In controlled experiments on open-weight models, activation features separate benign and credential-seeking prompts with high accuracy, including under held-out encoding transformations. In a small synthetic multi-turn suite, cumulative accounting detects attacks that per-turn detectors miss. These results are preliminary: the multi-turn benchmark is in-house and small, the activation method requires white-box access, and the information estimator provides a practical signal rather than a formal upper bound. Still, the results suggest that credential-exfiltration defenses should combine pre-output monitoring, calibrated canary detection, and temporal leakage accounting rather than relying only on text-level output filters.

</details>


### [18] [MimeLens: Position-Agnostic Content-Type Detection for Binary Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04171)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MimeLens是一种基于BERT的小型编码器家族，通过对文件随机偏移位置进行二进制内容预训练，实现了无需文件头部的MIME类型分类。在完整文件头部数据上，其top-1准确率比Google Magika v1.1高出10.7个百分点；在无法访问文件头的场景（如UDP数据包、磁盘块）中，分类精度显著优于libmagic和Magika；代价是CPU推理速度慢1-2个数量级，但在GPU或批处理模式下性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的文件类型分类系统（如Google Magika）假设需要从已知偏移位置（文件头部）获取完整文件内容，因此在许多实际工作流中无法使用——如单个数据包负载、无头部的文件碎片、随机磁盘块或分块上传。这限制了文件类型分类在恶意软件分类、法医取证、包检测和存储索引等任务中的应用。

Method: 提出MimeLens，一组小型BERT风格编码器家族，包含标准和短上下文两种变体。使用二进制内容进行预训练，训练数据从每个文件的均匀随机偏移位置采样窗口，不依赖文件头位置。输入可以是来自文件任意位置的字节块，无需头部且无固定大小；输出为libmagic的125种MIME标签之一。

Result: 在完整文件的干净头部数据上，MimeLens的top-1准确率比Magika v1.1高出10.7个百分点。在无法使用文件头的场景中：从单次UDP数据包中仍能分类，精度远超Magika；在随机中间文件块上，分类准确率是libmagic和Magika的两倍以上。代价是CPU推理速度慢约1-2个数量级，但在消费级GPU或批处理模式下性能相当。

Conclusion: MimeLens证明了不依赖文件头位置、从任意偏移进行文件类型分类是可行的，且精度优于现有方法。尽管CPU推理速度较慢，但在GPU或批处理场景下代价可控。所有检查点已在Hugging Face开源。

Abstract: File-type classification underlies many workflows like malware triage, forensic carving, packet inspection, and storage indexing. Learned systems such as Google's Magika assume whole-file access at a known offset, so they break on the inputs many of these tasks actually produce, like a single packet payload, a header-less carved fragment, a random disk block, or a chunked upload. We introduce MimeLens, a family of small BERT-style encoders pretrained on binary content from windows sampled at a uniformly random offset within each file, with no privileged head-of-file position, in standard- and short-context variants. A byte chunk goes in from anywhere in a file, no header needed and no fixed size; out comes one of libmagic's 125 MIME labels. On the clean head of complete files, MimeLens beats Magika v1.1 by +10.7 pp top-1 on libmagic-labeled data, and it keeps classifying where Magika cannot: from a single mid-stream UDP packet, and more than twice as accurately as libmagic and Magika on random mid-file disk blocks. The cost is latency: MimeLens runs roughly one to two orders of magnitude slower per sample on CPU than Magika, though it matches on consumer GPUs or in batch. All trained checkpoints are released on Hugging Face (mjbommar/mimelens-001-*).

</details>


### [19] [Notarized Agents: Receiver-Attested Confidential Receipts for AI Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04193)
*Juan Figuera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sello协议，通过接收方签名、HPKE加密、公钥透明日志和令牌引用发现四个机制，解决AI代理行为日志中因代理与日志记录者为同一实体导致的可信性缺陷，使所有者能在不信任代理或其运营商的情况下重建防篡改审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理的可观测性存在结构性缺陷：生成活动日志的实体与被记录活动的实体是同一个。被攻陷或有缺陷的代理可以省略、篡改或伪造自身的跟踪记录，而运行代理的操作员无法独立检测篡改行为。

Method: 提出一类协议，通过反转信任边界实现：接收代理调用的服务使用自己的密钥签署其观察到的调用收据，将收据用HPKE加密给代理所有者，并发布到公开的透明度日志中。具体实例化为Sello协议，包含四个特性：P1接收方签名、P2通过JWS将HPKE加密与绑定到授权令牌的所有者公钥结合、P3发布到见证人联合签名的Merkle日志、P4所有者通过令牌引用发现。

Result: 描述了协议细节，分析了在对手控制代理及运营商情况下的安全性，给出了密码学操作的微基准测试结果，并将Sello与现有收据协议（Signet、AgentROA、Agent Passport System、draft-farley-acta、SCITT）进行了对比定位。

Conclusion: 讨论了已知局限性，包括压制攻击、服务合谋以及采用激励问题。

Abstract: Current AI agent observability is structurally compromised: the entity producing the activity log is the same entity whose activity is being logged. A compromised or buggy agent can omit, alter, or fabricate its own traces, and the operator running the agent has no independent way to detect tampering. We propose a class of protocols that resolves this by inverting the trust boundary: the service that receives an agent's call signs a receipt of what it observed using its own key, encrypts the receipt to the agent's owner, and publishes it to a public transparency log. The owner reconstructs a tamper-evident trail without trusting the agent or its operator. We instantiate the class as Sello, a protocol combining four properties absent in any current system: (P1) receiver-side signing, (P2) HPKE encryption to an owner public key bound to the authorization token via JWS, (P3) publication to a witness-cosigned Merkle log, and (P4) owner-side discovery by token reference. We describe the protocol, analyze its security under an adversary that controls the agent and its operator, present microbenchmarks of the cryptographic operations, and situate Sello among adjacent receipt-protocol work (Signet, AgentROA, Agent Passport System, draft-farley-acta, SCITT). We discuss known limitations including the suppression attack, service collusion, and the adoption-incentive problem.

</details>


### [20] [Toward a Generalized Defense Across Sparse, Continuous, and Structured Parameter Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04317)
*Bin Duan,Zeyu Bai,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ParDef是一种针对深度神经网络参数攻击的通用防御方法，通过密钥通道重参数化、QC-LDPC量化和自适应鲁棒推理三种技术结合，在不明显降低模型性能的情况下，有效防御多种类型的参数攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在异构和部分不可信环境中部署（如云存储、CI/CD流水线、容器化服务和边缘执行平台），模型参数面临多种完整性风险。现有防御方法要么需要重训练、要么显著降低精度、要么仅针对特定攻击类型，而在实际部署场景中参数攻击形式往往不可预测，因此需要一种通用防御方法。

Method: ParDef整合了三种核心技术：(1) 密钥通道重参数化——遮蔽敏感参数方向；(2) QC-LDPC量化——嵌入冗余并支持纠错；(3) 自适应鲁棒推理——在不确定性下稳定预测。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上使用ResNet和VGG模型的评估表明，ParDef在不同参数攻击下持续降低攻击成功率，同时保持高模型性能，仅引入适度的部署开销。

Conclusion: ParDef是一种实用且通用的DNN部署防御方案，能够在不可预测的参数攻击场景下提供有效的保护。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous and partially untrusted environments, where models are distributed through cloud storage, CI/CD pipelines, containerized services, and edge execution platforms. This broad deployment landscape exposes model parameters to various integrity risks. Unlike input-space adversarial attacks, parameter attacks directly tamper with the model's internal parameters and persist across all subsequent inferences. Existing defenses either require retraining, incur significant accuracy degradation, or are limited to specific attack classes. However, in real-world deployment scenarios, the forms of parameter attacks are often unpredictable. To address this challenge, we present ParDef, a generalized defense for deep neural networks against diverse types of parameter attacks. ParDef integrates keyed channel reparameterization, which obscures sensitive parameter directions, QC-LDPC quantization, which embeds redundancy and supports error correction, and adaptive robust inference, which stabilizes predictions under uncertainty. Our evaluation on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet using ResNet and VGG models demonstrates that ParDef consistently reduces attack success rates across different parameter attacks while maintaining high model performance and incurring only moderate deployment overhead. These results highlight that ParDef is a practical and generalized defense for DNN deployments.

</details>


### [21] [From Untrusted Input to Trusted Memory: A Systematic Study of Memory Poisoning Attacks in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04329)
*Pritam Dash,Tongyu Ge,Aditi Jain,Tanmay Shah,Zhiwei Shang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了基于LLM的AI智能体中的记忆中毒问题，识别了四种记忆写入通道和九种结构性漏洞，提出了六类记忆中毒攻击的分类法，并设计了评估基准MPBench，发现更激进记忆操作的智能体更容易被利用，现有提示注入防御无法覆盖记忆中毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的持久记忆机制虽然能积累知识提升性能，但也引入了一个关键风险：单次对抗性记忆写入就可能对智能体行为产生长期影响（记忆中毒）。当前缺乏对这一威胁的系统研究。

Method: 通过分析LLM智能体的系统架构，识别出四种记忆写入通道和九种结构性漏洞（涵盖模型能力、系统提示设计和智能体系统架构三个层面）。基于这些漏洞，构建了六类记忆中毒攻击的分类体系，并设计了专门的评估基准MPBench进行实验验证。

Result: 研究发现，设计得越能积极写入和检索记忆的智能体反而越容易被利用；现有的提示注入防御措施无法有效覆盖记忆中毒攻击场景。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和防御AI智能体的记忆中毒攻击奠定了基础，揭示了现有防御机制的不足，并指出了更激进记忆策略带来的安全风险。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate knowledge across interactions and improve performance. However, persistent memory introduces the risk of memory poisoning, where a single adversarial memory write can exert long-term influence over agent behavior. We present a systematic study of memory poisoning in LLM-based agents. We identify four memory write channels and nine structural vulnerabilities in model capabilities, system prompt design, and agent system architecture that make these channels exploitable. Based on these vulnerabilities, we develop a taxonomy of six classes of memory poisoning attacks. Furthermore, we design MPBench -- a benchmark for evaluating memory poisoning attacks, and show that agents designed to write and retrieve memory more aggressively are more exploitable. We also show that existing prompt injection defenses fail to cover memory poisoning attacks. Our findings provide a foundation for understanding and mitigating memory poisoning attacks against AI agents.

</details>


### [22] [TITAN-FedAnil+: Trust-Based Adaptive Blockchain Federated Learning for Resource-Constrained Intelligent Enterprises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04388)
*Muhammad Hadi,Muhammad Jahangir,Talha Shafique,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TITAN-FedAnil+框架，结合信任自适应网络与区块链，通过亲和传播聚类聚合、GPU加速向量化和轻量级区块链同步机制，在资源受限的企业边缘设备上实现安全高效的联邦学习，内存开销降低81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习面临数据异构（非IID分布）和去中心化安全威胁的双重挑战，特别是在资源受限的企业环境中，需要同时保证鲁棒性、可扩展性和资源效率。

Method: (1) 基于亲和传播的自适应聚类聚合，无需先验知识即可识别和过滤恶意更新；(2) GPU加速向量化提升计算效率；(3) 带符号状态跳跃机制实现轻量级区块链重同步。

Result: 在50轮通信的8GB受限边缘设备上，相比基线框架实现高达81%的内存开销节省。

Conclusion: TITAN-FedAnil+有效提升了智能企业环境中安全联邦学习部署的鲁棒性、可扩展性和资源效率。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as an effective paradigm for collaborative intelligence while preserving data privacy. However, data heterogeneity arising from non-IID distributions and decentralized security threats remain significant challenges, particularly in resource-constrained enterprise environments. This paper presents TITAN-FedAnil+, a Trust-Based Adaptive Network for blockchain-enabled federated learning in intelligent enterprises. The proposed framework introduces affinity propagation-based adaptive clustered aggregation to identify and filter malicious updates without requiring prior knowledge of the number of attackers. In addition, GPU-accelerated vectorization is employed to improve computational efficiency, while a signed state jump mechanism enables lightweight blockchain resynchronization. Experimental results demonstrate substantial reductions in memory overhead, achieving up to 81% savings across 50 communication rounds on constrained 8 GB edge devices compared with the baseline framework. The results indicate that TITAN-FedAnil+ effectively improves robustness, scalability, and resource efficiency for secure federated learning deployments in intelligent enterprise environments.

</details>


### [23] [Token Rankings are Unforgeable Language Model Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04459)
*Matthew Finlayson,Andreas Grivas,Xiang Ren,Swabha Swayamdipta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 语言模型的对数概率输出存在模型特定的几何约束，可作为模型签名；本文发现即使只暴露token排序（而非概率值），对于足够大的k，top-k排序同样构成唯一且不可伪造的签名（因NP难），但排序仍可近似窃取模型参数，通过限制k值可在提供不可伪造签名与防止参数泄露间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明语言模型的logits输出包含模型参数信息，但更严格的API仅暴露token排序（排名而非概率值）。本文探究token排序是否同样构成模型签名，以及其安全影响。

Method: 理论分析证明对于足够大的k，每个模型有唯一的可行top-k排序集合；证明寻找具有相同可行排序集合的模型是NP难的，从而构成首个多项式时间不可伪造签名。同时分析排序对模型参数窃取的可行性及限制k值的防御效果。

Result: Token排序构成唯一且多项式时间不可伪造的模型签名（NP难）；通过排序可近似窃取模型最终层参数，但近似程度不足以伪造签名；存在k值的临界区间，使得签名可被验证而参数无法被有效窃取。

Conclusion: API可以通过暴露top-k排序来提供不可伪造的模型身份认证，同时通过限制k值大小来防止模型参数泄露，实现安全与身份验证的平衡。

Abstract: Language model parameters are known to impose unique (to each model) geometric constraints on their logit outputs, which serves as a signature that identifies the model, but also leaks the model's final layer parameters when an API distributes logits. We investigate more restrictive APIs that expose token rankings (i.e., their ordering by probability, but not the probability values) and find that rankings also constitute a signature: every model has a unique set of feasible top-$k$ rankings for sufficiently large $k$. Furthermore, the ranking signature is the first known (polynomially) unforgeable signature, since finding a model with the same set of feasible rankings is NP-hard. On the security front, we find that token rankings are already sufficient to approximately steal the final layer of the model, similar to logits, though the approximation is too coarse to forge the signature, and can be effectively countered by restricting the API to top-$k$ tokens with sufficiently small $k$. Since the top-$k$ required to present the model signature is generally smaller than the $k$ required to prevent stealing, it is possible for an API to present an unforgeable signature without leaking model parameters.

</details>


### [24] [CyberGym-E2E: Scalable Real-World Benchmark for AI Agents' End-to-End Cybersecurity Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04460)
*Tianneng Shi,Robin Rheem,Dongwei Jiang,Mona Wang,Francisco De La Riega,Zhun Wang,Jingzhi Jiang,Alexander Cheung,Sean Tai,Jonah Cha,Jianhong Tu,Gabriel Han,Chenguang Wang,Jingxuan He,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CyberGym-E2E，一个大规模、真实的端到端网络安全基准测试，用于全面评估AI代理在漏洞发现、PoC生成和补丁生成全生命周期中的能力，包含920个真实世界漏洞和139个开源项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI网络安全评估在规模或范围上有限，未能捕捉真实软件漏洞发现与修复的端到端生命周期，因此需要更全面、可扩展的评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个自动化、代理增强的流水线，将开源漏洞数据转化为真实的评估环境，形成了包含920个真实漏洞和139个开源项目的基准测试集。

Result: 目前基准测试包含920个真实世界漏洞，涵盖139个不同开源项目。

Conclusion: CyberGym-E2E通过大规模、端到端的评估框架，弥补了现有AI网络安全评估在覆盖漏洞全生命周期方面的空白，为AI自主漏洞发现与修复能力提供了更全面的评测标准。

Abstract: AI has the potential to transform cybersecurity by enabling systems that can autonomously detect, analyze, and remediate software vulnerabilities. However, existing cybersecurity evaluations of AI systems are limited in scale or scope, and fail to capture the end-to-end lifecycle of real-world software vulnerability discovery and remediation. To address this gap, we propose CyberGym-E2E, a large-scale and realistic end-to-end cybersecurity benchmark that comprehensively evaluates AI agents' abilities across the full lifecycle of vulnerability discovery, PoC generation, and patch generation. CyberGym-E2E is comprehensive and scalable, as we build an automated, agent-enhanced pipeline for transforming open-source vulnerability data into realistic evaluation environments. Currently, the benchmark consists of 920 real-world vulnerabilities across 139 different open-source projects.

</details>


### [25] [CLIF: Cross-layer LEO-ISL Fingerprinting for Physical and Network Attack Detection in Dense LEO Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04901)
*Varun Kohli,Arijit Bhattacharjee,Samar Shailendra,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨层轻量级行为指纹框架，融合物理层与网络层数据，对LEO巨型星座（星链、Kuiper及多运营商联合星座）中的网络层与组合攻击进行异常检测，基于马氏距离的检测器在三个星座上分别达到99.5%、99.4%和94.8%的召回率，FPR低于0.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO巨型星座依赖星间激光链路进行自主网状路由，但现有安全方法仅聚焦物理层安全，缺乏对网络层及组合攻击的覆盖，且缺少真实世界数据；随着星座密度增加和多运营商结盟，ISL完整性对商业可用性和国家安全至关重要。

Method: 构建跨层轻量级行为指纹框架，融合星上物理层测量与网络层数据；基于星链第一壳层（1,584颗）、Kuiper（1,156颗）及联合多运营商场景（2,740颗）进行轨道仿真，注入十种攻击类型（涵盖欺骗、流量操纵、路由颠覆）；评估三种无监督每星检测器，其中马氏距离检测器表现最优。

Result: 马氏距离检测器在星链上召回率99.5%，Kuiper上99.4%，多运营商联合星座上94.8%，假正率均低于0.7%；跨层特征融合对LEO星座全面安全是必要的，且对大规模网络具有高成本效益，适合资源受限卫星的严格星上能量预算。

Conclusion: 跨层特征融合能够以低计算开销实现LEO星座网络层与组合攻击的有效检测，马氏距离方法在精度和效率上均表现优异，适合部署于资源受限的卫星平台。

Abstract: Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) mega-constellations such as Starlink by SpaceX and Kuiper by Amazon rely on optical Inter-Satellite Links (ISLs) for autonomous mesh routing to provide low-latency telecommunication, Internet of Things (IoT), and security services globally. As commercial operators and governments deploy increasingly dense constellations and form multi-operator peering coalitions, ISL integrity becomes critical to both commercial availability and national security. However, there is a lack of real-world data for LEO constellations and existing real-time security approaches focus strictly on physical layer security, leaving blind spots in the coverage of network-layer and composite attacks. In this paper, we present a cross-layer, lightweight behavioral fingerprinting framework that fuses onboard physical-layer measurements with network-layer data to detect anomalies at low computational overhead. We construct an orbital simulation covering the first shells of Starlink (1,584 satellites), Kuiper (1,156 satellites), and a joint multi-operator peering scenario (2,740 satellites), injecting ten attack types that span spoofing, traffic manipulation, and routing subversion at varying severity. We evaluate three unsupervised, per-satellite detectors among which our Mahalanobis-distance-based detector achieves 99.5% recall on Starlink, 99.4% on Kuiper, and 94.8\% on the multi-operator constellation, while maintaining False Positive Rates (FPR) below 0.7%. Our results demonstrate that cross-layer feature fusion is not only necessary for comprehensive security of LEO constellations but highly cost-effective for large-scale networks while fitting into the strict onboard energy budgets of resource-constrained satellites.

</details>


### [26] [From Agent Traces to Trust: Evidence Tracing and Execution Provenance in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04990)
*Yiqi Wang,Jiaqi Zhang,Taotao Cai,Zirui Liu,Qingqiang Sun,Zequn Sun,Zhangkai Wu,Mingkai Zhang,Yanming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了LLM代理中的证据追踪与执行溯源问题，提出了统一溯源视角的概念框架和分类体系，覆盖了从检索归因、工具使用安全到故障诊断等关键方法方向，并探讨了从最终答案正确性向过程级可问责性转变的评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理通过与外部工具、检索系统、记忆模块、环境和其他代理交互来解决复杂任务，这种能力扩展了代理自主性，但也使得代理行为更难验证、调试和审计。仅靠最终答案的准确性无法解释输出是如何产生的、每个主张由哪些证据支持、工具调用是否合理、记忆如何影响后续决策以及执行故障的根源。证据追踪与执行溯源通过建模检索证据、工具输出、记忆项、环境观察、中间主张、动作和最终答案之间的连接关系，填补了这一空白。

Method: 本文采用系统综述方法，围绕统一的溯源视角组织相关工作，建立了涵盖溯源来源、证据与执行单元、溯源关系、溯源粒度与时机、表示形式和信任函数的分类体系，并梳理了包括溯源表示、证据归因、工具使用溯源、运行时护栏、承载溯源的记忆、基于轨迹的可观测性和故障诊断等关键方法方向。

Result: 映射了现有基准、数据集和评估指标与溯源相关能力的关系，提出了从最终答案正确性向过程级可问责性转变的评估方向。

Conclusion: 指出了开放挑战，包括统一溯源模式、主张级和语义级溯源、溯源感知的安全机制、真实的执行轨迹基准、面向恢复的评估以及隐私感知的审计基础设施。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly solve complex tasks by interacting with external tools, retrieval systems, memory modules, environments, and other agents. These capabilities expand agent autonomy, but also make agent behavior harder to verify, debug, and audit. Final-answer accuracy alone cannot explain how an output was produced, which evidence supported each claim, whether tool calls were justified, how memory influenced later decisions, or where execution failures originated. Evidence tracing and execution provenance address this gap by modeling how retrieved evidence, tool outputs, memory items, environment observations, intermediate claims, actions, and final answers are connected throughout agent execution. This survey provides a systematic review and conceptual framework for evidence tracing and execution provenance in LLM agents. We organize related work around a unified provenance perspective that connects retrieval grounding, claim support, tool-use safety, memory lineage, observability, debugging, audit, and recovery. We introduce a taxonomy covering trace sources, evidence and execution units, provenance relations, tracing granularity and timing, representation forms, and trust functions. We review key methodological directions, including provenance representation, evidence attribution, tool-use provenance, runtime guardrails, provenance-bearing memory, trace-based observability, and failure diagnosis. We also map existing benchmarks, datasets, and evaluation metrics to provenance-related capabilities, and discuss how evaluation can move from final-answer correctness toward process-level accountability. Finally, we outline open challenges, including unified trace schemas, claim-level and semantic provenance, provenance-aware safety mechanisms, realistic execution-trace benchmarks, recovery-oriented evaluation, and privacy-aware audit infrastructure.

</details>


### [27] [Long-Term and Short-Term Transistor Aging in Deep Neural Networks: Impact and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04266)
*Alireza Sarmadi,Virinchi Roy Surabhi,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Hussam Amrouch,Ramesh Karri,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本章详细讨论了长期和短期晶体管老化对深度神经网络（DNN）推理精度的影响，并提出了一种老化感知重训练方法，即使在使用激进（小于所需）保护带的情况下也能生成鲁棒的DNN，从而提升存在老化退化时的推理精度。此外，还简要讨论了将短期老化作为硬件木马检测的激励机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN在集成电路硬件上实现时，晶体管老化（如时序违反）会导致推理精度下降。传统的防护带方法会带来性能损失，因此需要在不影响性能的前提下缓解老化对DNN精度的影响。

Method: 提出了一种老化感知重训练（aging-aware retraining）方法，使得DNN即使在使用激进（小于常规所需）保护带的情况下也能保持较高的推理精度。

Result: 该方法能提升DNN在老化诱导退化下的推理精度，有效缓解长期和短期老化带来的精度损失。

Conclusion: 老化感知重训练可以有效缓解晶体管老化对DNN推理精度的负面影响，允许使用更小的防护带从而避免性能损失。短期老化还可作为硬件木马检测的激励手段。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are used in a variety of real-world applications including, for example, image classification and speech recognition. The inference accuracy of DNN implemented on hardware in integrated circuits (ICs) degrades under phenomena such as transistor aging. Aging slows down the switching speed of transistors, resulting in system-level timing violations due to unsustainable clocks. To maintain reliability for the entire projected lifetime, designers add guardbands to prevent timing violations; however, adding large timing guardbands causes losses in performance (speed or throughput). This chapter provides a detailed discussion of the effects of long-term and short-term transistor aging on DNN inference accuracy. Furthermore, to mitigate aging effects on DNN's accuracy and keep them at bay, a methodology for aging-aware retraining is presented in order to generate a resilient DNN even when aggressive (i.e., smaller than required) guardbands are used. This improves the inference accuracy of the DNNs even in the presence of aging-induced degradation. These effects are discussed in this chapter along with mitigation strategies on a hardware implementation of a DNN for image classification on an off-the-shelf image dataset. The application of short-term aging as an excitation mechanism for the detection of hardware Trojans in integrated circuits is also briefly discussed.

</details>


### [28] [Global Sketch-Based Watermarking for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04486)
*Daniel Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对掩码扩散语言模型，提出一种基于全局向量化草图表示的文本水印方法，该方法不依赖于局部上下文，具有顺序无关的检测统计量，并分析了其失真性、可靠性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法主要针对自回归语言模型，依赖局部上下文扰动下一个token的分布。扩散语言模型在生成过程中联合采样多个未确定位置的分布，使得全局加性统计量可追踪，这为设计更优的水印方案提供了机会。

Method: 提出一种针对掩码扩散语言模型的草图水印方法：控制文本的全局向量化草图表示，水印规则不表现为简单的token偏好，检测统计量与生成时的局部上下文顺序无关。

Result: 分析了该方法的失真性、可靠性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 草图水印相比上下文相关水印，将检测与生成时的局部上下文解耦，实现了顺序无关的统计量和更优的水印规则。

Abstract: Watermarking methods for language models have been studied extensively in the autoregressive setting, where tokens are generated sequentially. These works largely focus on local-context schemes that perturb the next token's distribution as a function of its preceding tokens. In diffusion language models, distributions over many unresolved positions are jointly sampled, allowing additive statistics of the entire sequence to be tractable during generation. We propose a watermark for masked diffusion language models that controls a global, vector-valued sketch representation of the text. Compared to context-dependent watermarking, the sketch formulation decouples detection from the local contexts seen during generation, resulting in an order-agnostic statistic and a watermarking rule which does not manifest as a simple token bias. We analyze the distortion, soundness, and robustness properties of the method.

</details>


### [29] [NLLog: Lightweight, Explainable SOC Anomaly Detection via Log-to-Language Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04957)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出NLLog管道，将系统日志的模板化格式重写为WHO-WHAT-SEVERITY自然语言句子，结合TF-IDF加权、树集成分类和TreeSHAP可解释性分析，在HDFS、BGL和AIT数据集上实现低误报率和高效率的日志异常检测与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统日志采用僵硬的模板化格式，既阻碍自动化分析也妨碍人类理解，亟需一种能同时提升机器可分析性和人工可读性的日志表示方法。

Method: 1) 将解析后的日志模板确定性重写为WHO-WHAT-SEVERITY自然语言句子；2) 使用TF-IDF加权进行特征表示；3) 采用树集成（随机森林等）对会话进行分类；4) 利用TreeSHAP进行证据回溯，支持分析师审查。

Result: 在HDFS和BGL数据集上超越两种复现的匹配协议基线；在HDFS、BGL和AIT数据集上保持低误报率；在商用硬件上的延迟满足安全运营中心（SOC）的工单分类需求。

Conclusion: 回退充分性依赖于语料库；部署前可通过注册阶段覆盖率检查发现优化需求；可审计的确定性重写结合轻量级稠密编码为日志异常检测和分类提供了可衡量的表示层。

Abstract: System-generated logs underpin security monitoring, yet their rigid template-based format hinders both automated analysis and human comprehension. We present NLLog (Natural-Language Log), a lightweight pipeline that deterministically rewrites parsed templates into WHO-WHAT-SEVERITY sentences, pools them with term-frequency-inverse-document-frequency weighting, classifies sessions with tree ensembles, and back-projects evidence with TreeSHAP for analyst review. On Hadoop Distributed File System (HDFS) and Blue Gene/L (BGL) corpora, NLLog exceeds two reproduced matched-protocol baselines; across HDFS, BGL, and the AIT Alert Data Set, it sustains low false-positive rates with commodity-hardware latency suitable for security operations center triage. Coverage, sparse-versus-dense, faithfulness, and adversarial ablations show that fallback sufficiency is corpus-dependent, that an enrollment-time coverage check can surface refinement requirements before deployment, and that an auditable deterministic rewrite combined with lightweight dense encoding provides a measurable representation layer for log-anomaly detection and triage.

</details>


### [30] [Preserving Data Privacy in Learning Causal Structure with Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05129)
*Jian Yang,Yuan Tong,Qinbin Li,Zeyi Wen,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全同态加密（FHE）的分布式因果结构学习方法，通过电路简化、除法和对数的近似计算以及SIMD加速批处理技术，在保护数据隐私的同时实现了与明文版本高度一致的因果结构学习，且可在数十分钟内完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式因果结构学习中数据隐私泄露问题持续存在，尤其是数据传输和计算过程中存在隐私风险，亟需一种能够在密文上进行计算且保证隐私的保护方法。

Method: 采用全同态加密对密文进行计算，保持数据在传输和计算过程中始终加密；通过电路简化提升效率；利用牛顿-拉弗森倒数法和泰勒展开近似除法和对数运算；采用SIMD加速的批处理技术优化整个学习过程；方法可扩展支持差分隐私。

Result: 在测试数据集上实现了与明文版本高度一致的因果结构，且能在数十分钟内完成加密状态下的因果结构学习，具备高效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于FHE的隐私保护因果结构学习方法在保证数据隐私的前提下，能够高效、准确地完成因果结构学习，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Preserving data privacy is an important topic in structural data management and data mining. However, the issue of privacy leakage in distributed causal structure learning is a persistent challenge, especially in cases where data transmission and computation are required. In this paper, we propose a method based on fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) that performs calculations on ciphertexts, keeping data encrypted in transition and computation. Nevertheless, adopting FHE to causal structure learning is challenging due to the high computation cost and limited support on division as well as logarithm operations in FHE. To tackle this challenge, we propose a series of novel techniques including (i) circuit simplification for better efficiency, (ii) approximation of division and logarithm through Newton-Raphson Reciprocal and Taylor expansion, and (iii) a batching technique with SIMD-acceleration to enhance the whole learning process. Additionally, our method can be easily extended beyond FHE by demonstration of its portability to support differential privacy. Empirical results show that our method achieves high consistency and comparable causal structure with the plaintext version in the datasets tested. Last, our method is efficient and practical to complete learning causal structures in tens of minutes even under the privacy protection of FHE.

</details>

### [31] [Early Detection of Alzheimer's Disease Using Explainable Machine Learning on Clinical Biomarkers: A Multi-Class Classification Study Using the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03995)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于XGBoost和8项常规临床评估特征（MMSE、CDR Global、CDR-SB、MoCA、FAQs、年龄、性别、教育程度），实现NC、MCI、AD三分类检测，宏AUC达0.982，SHAP可解释性分析揭示CDR Global主导NC与MCI分类，CDR-SB和MMSE共同驱动AD分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超5500万AD患者，缺乏基于常规临床评估的准确且可解释的NC、MCI、AD三分类检测方法。

Method: 采用XGBoost分类器，基于ADNI数据集8项临床特征进行三分类；Optuna超参数优化（50次）；SMOTE处理类别不平衡；5折交叉验证及独立测试集评估；SHAP进行特征级可解释性分析。

Result: 5折交叉验证宏AUC 0.983±0.007，准确率0.944±0.006，宏F1 0.929±0.008；独立测试集宏AUC 0.982（95%CI: 0.965-0.995），准确率0.943，平衡准确率0.932，宏F1 0.927，Cohen's kappa 0.909。

Conclusion: 基于常规临床评估的可解释机器学习模型实现了近乎完美的三分类AD检测；SHAP分析揭示了临床合理的类别特异性特征重要性模式；未来将融合语音生物标志物进行多模态检测。

Abstract: Background: Alzheimer's disease (AD) affects over 55 million people worldwide. Accurate, interpretable detection of normal cognition (NC), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and AD from routine clinical assessments remains a critical unmet need. Methods: An XGBoost classifier was developed for three-class detection using eight clinical features from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI): MMSE, CDR Global, CDR Sum of Boxes (CDR-SB), MoCA, FAQ, age, sex, and education. Hyperparameters were optimised using Optuna (50 trials); class imbalance was addressed with SMOTE. Performance was evaluated by macro AUC-ROC with 1,000-iteration bootstrap 95% confidence intervals, macro F1, balanced accuracy, and Cohen's kappa. SHAP values provided feature-level explainability. Results: The dataset comprised 1,641 baseline subjects (608 NC, 767 MCI, 266 AD). On five-fold cross-validation, mean macro AUC was 0.983 (SD 0.007), accuracy 0.944 (SD 0.006), and macro F1 0.929 (SD 0.008). On the held-out test set (n = 247), macro AUC was 0.982 (95% CI: 0.965--0.995), accuracy 0.943, balanced accuracy 0.932, macro F1 0.927, and Cohen's kappa 0.909. SHAP analysis identified CDR Global as the dominant predictor for NC and MCI, while CDR-SB and MMSE together drove AD classification. Conclusion: An explainable machine learning model trained on routine clinical assessments achieves near-perfect three-class Alzheimer's detection. SHAP analysis reveals clinically plausible, class-specific feature importance patterns supporting clinical validity. Future work will extend this framework with speech biomarkers for multimodal detection.

</details>


### [32] [Novel Aspects of IEEE SA P3109 Arithmetic Formats for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04028)
*Andrew Fitzgibbon,Christoph M. Wintersteiger,Jeffrey Sarnoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IEEE P3109草案标准定义了面向机器学习的参数化二进制浮点格式族，支持宽度、精度、符号性和无穷存在的参数化配置，并定义了丰富的舍入与饱和模式（含随机舍入），通过无异常加速设计、块缩放运算以及基于κ近似的近似实现规范，并已通过形式化规范完成机械验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器学习应用设计高效且一致的短位宽浮点表示格式，解决传统浮点标准在机器学习场景下位宽过宽、效率不足的问题。

Method: 采用参数化设计（宽度、精度、符号性、无穷存在）；通过解码到闭扩展实数集定义运算；定义广泛舍入/饱和模式（含随机舍入）；无异常设计（通过返回值传达异常）；块缩放运算统一定义；提出κ近似（scale-invariant measure）描述近似实现；使用形式化规范进行机械验证。

Result: 定义了一族参数化浮点格式及运算，支持多种舍入模式和无异常加速，块运算机制统一，κ近似用于量化近似实现精度，并通过形式化方法验证了标准函数定义及性质。

Conclusion: 该标准为机器学习提供了高效、灵活、可验证的短位宽浮点运算规范，兼顾表示效率与数值一致性。

Abstract: The IEEE P3109 draft standard defines a parameterized family of binary floating-point formats and associated operations, with a focus on facilitating machine learning. These formats allow efficient and consistent representation of values in a small number of bits. The defined formats are parameterized over width and precision in bits, signedness, and the presence of infinities. Operations are defined by decoding floating-point values to the set of closed extended reals: the reals augmented with positive and negative infinity and NaN (Not a Number). Explicit treatment of NaN and infinite operands ensures that only real arithmetic is invoked in operation definitions. Extensive rounding and saturation modes are defined; stochastic rounding is included. Operations are exception-free, accelerating throughput, with exceptional situations communicated through return values, e.g., NaN. Operations on blocks of values sharing a common scale factor are defined in terms of the underlying operations in a uniform manner. System vendors may describe approximate implementations via a novel scale-invariant measure, akin to units in the last place, called kappa-approximation. Standard function definitions and various other properties are mechanically verified and generated using formal specifications.

</details>


### [33] [Position: Deployed Reinforcement Learning should be Continual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04029)
*Parnian Behdin,Kevin Roice,Golnaz Mesbahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张，部署后无法达到最优但能接收评估性奖励信号的智能体本质上是一个持续强化学习问题。识别了部署后四种导致非平稳性的来源，论证了最佳部署智能体从不停止适应，并分析了现实世界中持续RL的成功案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数强化学习系统采用"训练-修复"范式，训练后的智能体在与世界交互时不再学习，直到性能下降才重新训练。作者认为这种范式存在根本性缺陷，需要转向持续学习范式。

Method: 作为立场论文，通过理论论证识别了部署后四种非平稳性来源，并分析现实世界中持续RL的成功案例来支持观点。

Result: 识别出部署后四种非平稳性来源：环境变化、任务分布漂移、智能体自身行为影响以及奖励函数变化。指出现有成功案例证明了持续RL的可行性。

Conclusion: 呼吁RL社区从"训练-修复"范式转向持续学习范式，并提供了相应的优势和评估指标。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has received increasing attention and adoption in real-world use cases. Most of these systems follow a train-then-fix paradigm, where trained agents do not learn while interacting with the world until performance degrades and retraining becomes necessary. In this position paper, we argue that deploying an agent that is incapable of optimality, but receives an evaluative reward signal, is inherently a continual RL problem. We identify four sources of non-stationarity after deployment that necessitate never-ending learning, and highlight why the best deployed agents never stop adapting. We analyze successful examples of continual RL in the real world, and present the community with the advantages and measures to move away from the current train-then-fix paradigm.

</details>


### [34] [Policy Gradient for Continuous-Time Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04335)
*Tanya Veeravalli,David M. Bossens,Atsushi Nitanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中的策略梯度算法，提出了基于路径和伴随公式的策略梯度和对抗梯度，并设计了双循环优化器和均值场优化器，分别实现了线性收敛和次线性收敛，以及在样本复杂度上的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RMDP考虑离散时间动力学，而连续时间RMDP的研究尚属空白。本文旨在将策略梯度算法扩展到连续时间RMDP框架中，并给出收敛性和样本复杂度的理论保证。

Method: 采用路径式和伴随式方法推导随机微分方程和常微分方程的策略梯度和对抗梯度；提出双循环优化器（实现线性收敛）和均值场优化器（作为分布优化器，具有次线性收敛率）。

Result: 双循环优化器在oracle设定下达到线性收敛，样本复杂度为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/ε^2)$；均值场优化器在oracle设定下收敛率为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/K)$，在$N$粒子近似下样本复杂度为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(N^2/ε)$。

Conclusion: 连续时间策略梯度算法在神经ODE动力学的连续时间RMDP上有效，为连续时间鲁棒强化学习提供了理论和方法基础。

Abstract: The framework of robust Markov decision processes (RMDPs) allows the design of reinforcement learning agents that satisfy performance guarantees under worst-case transition dynamics. Traditional RMDPs consider discrete-time dynamics and recently, sample-efficient policy gradient algorithms have been considered in this context. This paper investigates policy gradient algorithms within a continuous-time RMDP framework. Policy gradients and adversarial gradients are derived using pathwise and adjoint-based formulas for stochastic and ordinary differential equations. We propose double-loop optimisers to obtain linear convergence in the oracle-based setting and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{ε^2})$ sample complexity in the sample-based setting in an analysis which also derives novel tools for the framework of undiscounted total cost MDPs. Additionally, we propose mean-field optimisers as distributional optimisers with an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{1}{K})$ oracle-based convergence rate and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\frac{N^2}ε)$ sample complexity under $N$-particle approximation. The effectiveness of continuous-time policy gradient algorithms is confirmed for both optimisers on continuous-time RMDPs with neural ordinary differential equation dynamics.

</details>


### [35] [Pseudospectral Bounds for Transient Amplification in Coupled Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04031)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合梯度下降法中块三角雅可比矩阵的非正态性导致的瞬态放大现象，建立了尖锐的伪谱理论，给出了Kreiss常数的上界与极小极大下界，并推导出随机耦合下降的有限步迭代复杂度界，实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合梯度下降法在双层优化、两时间尺度随机逼近和对抗训练中广泛存在，当耦合雅可比矩阵为块三角结构时，虽然谱半径分析可保证渐近稳定性，但非正态性会导致收敛前出现任意大的瞬态放大，现有理论无法刻画这一现象。

Method: 发展了块三角雅可比矩阵的尖锐伪谱理论，证明当对角块对称且谱半径≤γ<1时Kreiss常数满足K(J) ≤ 2/(1-γ) + ‖C‖/(4(1-γ))，并建立匹配的极小极大下界；通过Neumann级数扰动框架将分析扩展到近自指系统，进而导出有限步迭代复杂度界O(K(J)² log(1/δ))。

Result: 建立了耦合临界阈值刻划谱不稳定性，给出了随机耦合下降的有限步迭代复杂度上界，在二次型问题、IQC对比和神经网络训练中验证了理论。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了非渐近、实例依赖的高维学习动力学标度律，该规律在谱半径分析中不可见，为理解耦合优化算法的瞬态行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Coupled gradient descent--where the update of one parameter block depends on another--underlies bilevel optimization, two-time-scale stochastic approximation, and adversarial training. When the coupled Jacobian is block-triangular, asymptotic stability is governed by the spectral radii of the diagonal blocks, yet transient amplification before convergence can be arbitrarily large due to non-normality. We develop a sharp pseudospectral theory for such block-triangular Jacobians, proving that the Kreiss constant satisfies $K(J) \leq 2/(1-γ) + \|C\|/(4(1-γ))$ when the diagonal blocks are symmetric with spectral radii at most $γ< 1$, and we establish matching minimax lower bounds. We characterize the critical coupling threshold for spectral instability and extend the analysis to nearly self-referential systems via a Neumann-series perturbation framework. As a consequence, we obtain a finite-horizon iteration-complexity bound of $O(K(J)^2 \log(1/δ))$ for stochastic coupled descent. Framed as scaling laws for non-stationary two-time-scale optimization, our results expose a non-asymptotic, instance-dependent regime of high-dimensional learning dynamics that is invisible to spectral-radius analysis. Experiments on linear-quadratic problems, IQC-based comparisons, and neural-network training confirm the theory.

</details>


### [36] [Do Transformers Need Three Projections? Systematic Study of QKV Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04032)
*Ali Kayyam,Anusha Madan Gopal,M Anthony Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了Transformer中QKV投影共享的三种变体（Q-K=V、Q=K-V、Q=K=V），发现Q-K=V（共享键值投影）在多种任务上表现与标准QKV相当甚至更优，在语言建模中可实现50%的KV缓存减少且仅造成3.1%的困惑度退化，与GQA/MQA结合可进一步实现87.5%-96.9%的缓存压缩，适用于边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管QKV注意力公式在Transformer中占据核心地位，但三个投影各自的贡献以及省略某些投影的影响尚未被充分理解，本文旨在系统性地填补这一空白。

Method: 定义了三种投影共享约束：Q-K=V（共享键值）、Q=K-V（共享查询键）、Q=K=V（单一投影），并通过2D位置编码解决对称注意力问题；在合成任务、视觉任务和语言建模（300M/1.2B参数，10B tokens）上进行实验评估。

Result: Q-K=V在多数任务上与标准QKV持平或更优；语言建模中KV缓存减少50%，困惑度仅上升3.1%；与GQA-4结合减少87.5%缓存，与MQA结合减少96.9%。Q=K-V因破坏注意力方向性导致质量下降。

Conclusion: 投影共享是一种被忽视的注意力权重绑定策略，Q-K=V由于键值可共享相似表示空间且注意力运作在低秩区域，能保持模型质量并带来可量化的推理内存收益，特别适合边缘部署。

Abstract: Transformers have become the standard solution for various AI tasks, with the query, key, and value (QKV) attention formulation playing a central role. However, the individual contribution of these three projections and the impact of omitting some remain poorly understood. We systematically evaluate three projection sharing constraints: a) Q-K=V (shared key-value), b) Q=K-V (shared query-key), and c) Q=K=V (single projection). The last two variants produce symmetric attention maps; to address this, we also explore asymmetric attention via 2D positional encodings. Through experiments spanning synthetic tasks, vision (MNIST, CIFAR, TinyImageNet, anomaly), and language modeling (300M and 1.2B parameter models on 10B tokens), we discovered that our transformers perform on par or occasionally better than the QKV transformer. In language modeling, Q-K=V projection sharing achieves 50% KV cache reduction with only 3.1% perplexity degradation. Crucially, projection sharing is complementary to head sharing (GQA/MQA): combining Q-K=V with GQA-4 yields 87.5% cache reduction, while Q-K=V + MQA achieves 96.9%, enabling practical on-device inference. We show that Q-K=V preserves quality because keys and values can occupy similar representational spaces and attention operates in a low-rank regime, whereas Q=K-V breaks attention directionality. Our results systematically characterize projection sharing as an underexplored instance of weight tying in attention, with direct, quantifiable inference memory benefits, particularly valuable for edge deployment. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/anushamadan02/Do-Transformers-Need-3-Projections

</details>

Abstract: This study aims to determine whether the application of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) as a specialized execution overlay can enhance pair trading in highly volatile cryptocurrency markets. Although classical implementations of the strategy have proven successful in traditional equities, they frequently exhibit rigidity and suffer from severe divergence risks when applied to high-variance environments. To address this need, this research introduces novel concepts. To construct a robust system, we developed a hierarchical "Filter-then-Rank" pair selection methodology and a proprietary "Fixed Risk, Adaptive Mean" execution model. The system employs a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent with a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) layer to govern execution decisions within strict deterministic risk management boundaries. Evaluated on 1-hour interval data from the Binance USD-M Futures market, the optimized RL policy achieved an out-of-sample performance that substantially outperformed the heuristic baseline. A stationary circular block bootstrap robustness check confirms that the agent's risk-adjusted outperformance is statistically significant at the 10 percent level. Although falling marginally short of the stricter 5 percent threshold, this result highlights the extreme idiosyncratic variance characteristic of digital assets. Ultimately, this thesis contributes to the quantitative finance literature by introducing a hybrid architecture that combines statistical arbitrage with DRL execution policies. Furthermore, it delivers a novel framework for safe reinforcement learning via deterministic shielding, proving that anchoring a neural policy to statistically robust boundaries successfully mitigates severe divergence risks.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进核反应堆和燃料概念的验证需要与目标技术具有高中子学相似性的临界实验，但现有实验覆盖有限。目前缺乏系统化的方法来自动设计能够最大化中子学相似性的实验几何构型。

Method: 采用U-Net卷积编码器-解码器结合多群注意力池化层构建深度神经网络代理模型，在OpenMC计算的网格化临界实验几何构型灵敏度向量上训练；利用代理模型的可微性，通过非参数梯度优化直接调整几何网格中每个位置的材料分配以最大化相关性系数$c_k$。

Result: 多群注意力池化在性能和可解释性上均优于传统池化方法；优化产生的实验几何构型对三种感兴趣配置分别达到0.97757、0.81324和0.93276的$c_k$值。

Abstract: The validation of advanced nuclear reactor designs and fuel concepts requires critical experiments with high neutronic similarity to the target technology. Neutronic similarity is quantified by the correlation coefficient $c_k$, which captures the shared bias in $k_\text{eff}$ induced by uncertainties in nuclear data. Generally, a $c_k\geq0.9$ is needed for an experiment to be sufficiently similar to a target technology. This work presents a methodology for the inverse design of critical experiments. Deep neural network surrogate modeling and nonparametric gradient optimization are used to generate experiment geometries that maximize $c_k$.
  A deep neural network is trained on OpenMC-calculated sensitivity vectors for grid-based critical experiment geometries. The model architecture combines a U-Net convolutional encoder-decoder with a novel multigroup attention pooling layer, introduced to capture the differing spatial dependencies of sensitivities. Multigroup attention pooling is shown to achieve better performance than traditional pooling, as well as interpretable internal behavior. The differentiability of the surrogate enables gradient-based optimization of the full combinatorial design space, allowing $c_k$ to be maximized by directly changing the material assignment of each position in the geometry grid.
  The method is applied to the validation of the TN-Americas TN-LC transportation cask with HALEU fuel, for which existing critical experiment coverage is limited. The optimization procedure is shown to produce experiment geometries achieving $c_k$ scores of 0.97757, 0.81324, and 0.93276 for three configurations of interest. This approach demonstrates the potential of deep learning and gradient optimization to accelerate the development of advanced nuclear technology.

</details>

*Yifeng Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SDPG框架，通过结合群体相对验证器优势、归一化标准差、精确全词汇在线自蒸馏和参考策略KL正则化，改进了稀疏奖励强化学习中语言模型的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏可以利用特权上下文为稀疏奖励强化学习提供密集监督信号，弥补奖励稀疏带来的学习困难。

Method: SDPG框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 群体相对验证器优势（Group-relative verifier advantages）与归一化标准差；(2) 精确全词汇在线自蒸馏（exact full-vocabulary on-policy self-distillation），实例化为学生到教师的逆向KL散度损失；(3) 参考策略KL正则化。

Result: SDPG在稳定性和性能上均优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: 在线自蒸馏结合策略梯度能够有效提升语言模型在稀疏奖励场景下的强化学习效果，SDPG框架为此提供了有效且稳定的实现方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation, where a language model conditions on privileged context to supervise its own generations, is a promising source of dense supervision for sparse-reward reinforcement learning. Actually, it can be instantiated as an auxiliary full-vocabulary student-to-teacher reverse Kullback-Leibler divergence loss. We therefore propose SDPG, a self-distilled policy-gradient framework that combines group-relative verifier advantages with normalized standard deviation, exact full-vocabulary on-policy self-distillation, as well as reference-policy KL regularization. Empirically, SDPG improves stability and performance over RLVR and self-distillation baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/lauyikfung/SDPG.

</details>


### [40] [Bayes-Sufficient Representations in Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04045)
*Vasileios Sevetlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了在固定监督决策问题下，表示学习中的"充分性"与"最小性"概念，提出贝叶斯商（Bayes quotient）作为保留最小必要信息的核心对象，并通过理论分析与实验验证了损失函数对所需信息的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表示学习中，传统观点认为表示应保留输入中与预测相关的信息，但"相关"的含义在固定监督决策问题中并不明确。本文旨在严格定义何为"相关"，探究损失函数对所需信息的影响，并确定实现贝叶斯最优预测所需的最小信息量。

Method: 引入贝叶斯充分性（Bayes-sufficiency）概念，定义表示在给定联合分布和损失下是充分的当且仅当存在某个预测头能实现贝叶斯最优动作规则。提出贝叶斯商（Bayes quotient）作为核心工具，将需要相同贝叶斯最优动作的输入归为一类，并定义表示充分当且仅当它细化该商，贝叶斯最小当且仅当它与该商信息等价。通过属性 elicitaiton 建立与不同损失函数（零一损失、平方损失、Brier损失、对数损失等）的联系，并通过控制实验、神经瓶颈实验和 iNaturalist 数据实验验证理论框架。

Result: 零一损失需要贝叶斯类，平方损失需要条件均值，Brier损失需要二元预测中的条件概率，对数损失或严格恰当评分规则需要预测分布。实验表明，充分性、最小性和保留非必需信息之间存在可区分的差异：贝叶斯商决定了贝叶斯最优预测所需的最小信息量，而不同的损失函数对表示的信息需求有本质影响。

Conclusion: 对于固定的监督问题，分布和损失决定贝叶斯动作，贝叶斯动作决定贝叶斯商，贝叶斯商决定贝叶斯最优预测所需的最小信息。该框架将表示学习中的充分性与最小性形式化，并揭示了损失函数在定义"相关"信息中的核心作用。

Abstract: Representation learning is often described as preserving the information in an input that is relevant for prediction. This work asks what relevance means for a fixed supervised decision problem.
