A representation is defined to be Bayes-sufficient for a joint distribution and loss if some prediction head can use it to implement a Bayes-optimal action rule. This makes the target information loss-dependent. In the almost-surely unique Bayes-action case, the relevant object is a Bayes quotient, which identifies inputs that require the same Bayes-optimal action. A representation is sufficient when it refines this quotient, and Bayes-minimal when it is informationally equivalent to it. The framework connects naturally to property elicitation: zero-one loss requires the Bayes class, squared loss the conditional mean, Brier loss the conditional probability in binary prediction, and log loss or strictly proper scoring rules the predictive distribution. Controlled finite experiments, learned neural bottleneck experiments, and a real-data iNaturalist taxonomic refinement experiment illustrate the distinction between sufficiency, minimality, and retained non-required information. For a fixed supervised problem, the distribution and the loss determine the Bayes action, the Bayes action determines the quotient, and the quotient determines the minimal information required for Bayes-optimal prediction.

</details>


### [41] [Unlocking Feature Learning in Gated Delta Networks at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04048)
*Yifeng Liu,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格推导坐标大小估计，将最大更新参数化（μP）扩展到门控Delta网络，实现了学习率在不同模型宽度下的零样本迁移，并通过语言模型预训练实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的训练和扩展需要巨大的计算资源，虽然μP方法已成功应用于标准Transformer实现超参数零样本迁移，但其在线性模型（特别是具有结构化状态转换和复杂架构的模型）上的扩展仍未被探索。

Method: 通过严格的前向传播、门控机制和循环状态动态的坐标大小估计推导，得出了门控Delta网络的缩放规则。

Result: 在语言模型预训练实验中，采用本文方法的配置在AdamW和SGD优化器下均实现了稳定的学习率跨模型宽度迁移，而标准参数化无法实现迁移。

Conclusion: 验证了所提出分析的正确性和实用性，为门控Delta网络的高效超参数调优提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Training and scaling Large Language Models demand enormous computational resources, motivating both efficient sub-quadratic architectures and principled hyperparameter tuning methods. While the Maximal Update Parametrization ($μ$P) has enabled zero-shot hyperparameter transfer for standard Transformers, its extension to linear models, particularly those with structured state transitions and complicated architectures, remains largely unexplored. By rigorously propagating coordinate-size estimates through the forward pass, gating mechanisms, and recurrent state dynamics, we derive the scaling rules for Gated Delta Network. Experiments on language-model pre-training confirm that our configurations enable stable learning-rate transfer across model widths under both AdamW and SGD, whereas standard parametrization fails to transfer, validating the correctness and practical utility of our analysis.

</details>


### [42] [LiftQuant: Continuous Bit-Width LLM via Dimensional Lifting and Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04050)
*Liulu He,XuanAng Liu,Juntao Liu,Taolue Feng,Ting Lu,Chunsheng Gan,Zhiyv Peng,Yuan Du,Huanrui Yang,Yijiang Liu,Li Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiftQuant 通过"升维-投影"机制实现准连续比特宽度控制，使得大语言模型能以非整数比特（如2.4比特）精确适配特定内存预算，在保持硬件友好的同时达到帕累托最优部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法受限于整数比特宽度（如2、3比特），存在"部署鸿沟"——大语言模型无法精确适配特定内存预算，导致次优部署。

Method: 提出"升维-投影"机制：从高维"升维空间"中投影一个简单的1比特格子来近似低维权重向量。有效比特宽度由升维维度与原始维度的比值决定，通过灵活调整维度实现准连续比特控制。该投影生成结构化但非均匀的码本，兼具向量量化的表达能力；解码路径仅依赖线性变换和1比特均匀量化器，保持硬件友好性。

Result: LiftQuant 能将70B大模型压缩至2.4比特以精确适配24GB GPU，其性能显著超越在相同设备上部署的现有最优2比特模型。

Conclusion: LiftQuant 通过连续比特宽度控制真正实现了帕累托最优部署，弥合了现有量化方法的"部署鸿沟"。

Abstract: Existing quantization methods are fundamentally limited by rigid, integer-based bit-widths (e.g., 2, 3-bit), resulting in a ``deployment gap" where Large Language Models cannot be optimally fitted to specific memory budgets. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiftQuant, a novel framework that enables continuous bit-width control for true Pareto-optimal deployment. The core innovation is a ``lift-then-project" mechanism which approximates low-dimensional weight vectors by projecting a simple 1-bit lattice from a higher-dimensional ``lifted" space. Crucially, the effective bit-width is determined simply by the ratio of the lifted dimension to the original dimension, which allows the bit-width to be tuned quasi-continuous as the dimension is a flexible structural parameter. This projection generates a structured yet non-uniform codebook, capturing the expressive power of Vector Quantization (VQ). While beneficial over VQ, LiftQuant's decoding path relies solely on linear transformations and 1-bit uniform quantizers, retaining hardware-friendly nature. This flexibility is transformative: LiftQuant enables a 70B LLM to be compressed to 2.4 bits to precisely fit a 24GB GPU, where its performance significantly surpasses state-of-the-art 2-bit models fitted on the same device. Our code and ckpt is available at https://github.com/Heliulu/LiftQuant.

</details>


### [43] [A Goal-Set Characterization of Task Composition in the Boolean Task Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04053)
*Eduardo Terrés-Caballero,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现布尔任务代数（BTA）中，确定性MDP下的最优扩展Q值函数完全由通用任务和空任务决定，原本提出的对数级基础任务集是冗余的。基于此，提出了一种基于目标集的组合方法，通过从通用和空值函数中选取切片来重构组合值函数，降低了学习和组合成本。实验验证了无需学习额外基础任务。但在随机MDP中，该压缩性质不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视BTA的结构假设，发现其最优扩展Q值函数存在压缩现象，论证原有基础任务集的冗余性，从而降低组合强化学习的计算成本。

Method: 理论证明确定性MDP中BTA的最优扩展Q值函数完全由通用任务和空任务决定；提出基于目标集的组合方法，通过从这两个值函数中选取切片来执行逻辑操作并重构组合值函数。

Result: 在表格、视觉、函数逼近和连续控制领域验证了学习额外基础任务不会带来更好的性能；新方法降低了BTA的学习成本和组合时间，同时保持了策略性能；在随机MDP中给出反例，证明压缩性质不成立。

Conclusion: 确定性MDP中BTA的基础任务集可以大幅简化到仅需两个任务，但在随机MDP中需要指数级策略数量才能实现最优组合。

Abstract: The Boolean Task Algebra (BTA) provides a principled framework for zero-shot task composition in reinforcement learning by equipping goal-reaching tasks with Boolean operations. We revisit its structural assumptions and formalize a collapse in the space of optimal extended Q-value functions: in deterministic MDPs, every such function is fully determined by the universal and empty tasks. This makes the logarithmic set of base tasks proposed in the original BTA formulation redundant. Building on this observation, we introduce a goal-set-based composition method that performs logical operations on goal sets and reconstructs composed value functions by selecting slices from the universal and empty value functions. This reduces learning costs for standard BTA and reduces composition time for both BTA and Skill Machines, while preserving policy performance. Experiments across tabular, visual, function-approximation, and continuous-control domains show that learning additional base tasks does not yield better performance. Finally, we study the stochastic setting and provide a counterexample showing that this collapse need not hold, that is, optimal composition may require accounting for exponentially many policies in the number of goals. Code is available at https://github.com/EduardoTerres/bta_paper.

</details>


### [44] [Spectral Scaling Laws of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04058)
*Gagik Magakyan,Pablo Parrilo,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Muon优化器中动量矩阵的奇异值谱在训练过程中的行为，发现其分位数在预热后稳定，且与模型大小呈幂律关系。基于此，为不同层提出了自适应的牛顿-舒尔茨迭代配置选择方法，在保证正交化质量的同时减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器使用Newton-Schulz迭代对动量矩阵进行近似正交归一化，但小奇异值方向的正交化效果不佳。目前缺乏对训练过程中动量矩阵奇异值谱行为及其随模型规模变化的系统理解，导致难以确定合适的NS迭代配置。

Method: 在77M到2.8B参数范围内跟踪不同层动量缓冲区的奇异值分位数，分析其稳定值随模型大小的变化规律，拟合幂律关系，并基于此提出层感知的NS迭代配置选择策略。

Result: 发现各层奇异值分位数在短时间预热后稳定，稳定值与模型大小呈清晰幂律关系，层间指数差异显著：中浅层指数约M^{-0.25}，标准5步NS配置可覆盖；部分深层指数达M^{-0.96}，在大规模场景下会落入NS失效区域。

Conclusion: 为大规模训练提供了理论指导：根据层类型和模型大小，可自适应选择最小NS迭代次数或更好调优的系数，在保证正交化质量的同时避免不必要的计算开销。

Abstract: Orthonormalized update rules have rapidly become a leading choice of optimizer for training large language models, with recent open-source state-of-the-art models adopting Muon. To keep these updates tractable, Muon performs the orthonormalization with the Newton--Schulz (NS) iteration. Since NS is only approximate, directions with small singular values fail to be orthonormalized. In Muon, NS is applied to the momentum matrix at every step, yet little is known about how the singular value spectrum of these momentum matrices behaves during training, or how that behavior changes with model size. We present the first systematic study of this question. Tracking singular value quantiles of the momentum buffer across layers in models ranging from 77M to 2.8B parameters, we observe a consistent picture: after a short burn-in, the quantiles stabilize at a value determined by the layer type and model size. These stabilization values follow remarkably clean power laws in model size, with layer-dependent exponents. Layers up to mid-late depth scale very mildly with model size $M$ (around $M^{-0.25}$), so the standard 5-step NS configuration used at academic scale will continue to orthonormalize them at much larger scales. Some of the late layers, however, scale much more aggressively (up to $M^{-0.96}$) and will fall into the NS failure regime at frontier scale unless one uses more NS iterations or better-tuned coefficients. NS iterations are computationally expensive at scale; our laws give practitioners a principled, layer-aware recipe for choosing the minimum NS configuration that still orthonormalizes the directions that matter -- avoiding unnecessary computation without sacrificing update quality.

</details>


### [45] [LLM Compression with Jointly Optimizing Architectural and Quantization choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04063)
*Hoang-Loc La,Truong-Thanh Le,Amir Taherkordi,Phuong Hoai Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微分的神经架构搜索框架，联合优化LLM线性层的架构配置与混合精度量化，在边缘设备上实现更优的精度-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS方法通常限制搜索空间，并将架构搜索与量化解耦，导致次优的压缩效果。

Method: 提出可微分的NAS框架，将架构搜索与混合精度量化联合优化，应用于LLM的线性层压缩。

Result: 相比顺序NAS-量化基线，推理速度提升1.4倍（同等精度）；或在相同延迟下，7个推理任务平均精度提升6%。

Conclusion: 联合优化架构与混合精度量化的可微分NAS框架能有效压缩LLM，在边缘设备上取得更好的精度-延迟权衡。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) is challenging due to their significant memory and computational requirements. While some methods address this by developing small or tiny language models from scratch, these approaches demand extensive GPU training. Compressing pre-trained LLMs for edge devices offers a compelling alternative. Beyond pruning and quantization, Neural Architecture Search (NAS) enables effective compression, yet prior NAS approaches often limit the search space and decouple architecture from quantization. We introduce a differentiable NAS framework that explores the entire space and jointly optimizes architectural configurations alongside mixed-precision quantization for linear layers of LLMs. Experiments demonstrate superior accuracy-latency trade-offs: our models achieve up to 1.4x faster inference than sequential NAS-then-quantization baselines at comparable accuracy, or up to 6% higher average accuracy across seven reasoning tasks at equivalent latency.

</details>


### [46] [TPA-AD: A Two-Stage Pseudo Anomaly-Guided Method for Bearing Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04073)
*Xiancheng Wang,Zhibo Zhang,Ran Li,Rui Wang,Minghang Zhao,Shisheng Zhong,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段伪异常引导的异常检测方法（TPA-AD），在仅使用正常样本训练的条件下，通过构建边界邻域的伪异常窗口，结合对比学习和KNN，实现轴箱轴承时间序列异常检测，在轴承故障和退化数据集上表现稳定且敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决仅正常样本可用场景下，时间序列异常检测中正常与异常边界难以分离的问题，同时避免现有方法依赖已知故障类别、真实异常先验或随机异常注入的局限性。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段利用重建模型和逐特征目标误差控制，在正常边界附近生成伪异常窗口；第二阶段通过正常与伪异常窗口间的对比学习学习异常敏感表征，最后使用KNN生成窗口级和点级异常分数。

Result: 在轴承故障检测和退化过程数据集上表现良好，额外在13个公开TSAD数据集上验证，展现出稳定的异常响应、对退化演化的敏感性以及一定的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TPA-AD通过边界邻域伪异常构造有效提升了正常边界的可分性，能联合处理混合变量场景中的连续与离散特征，在真实高铁轴承数据上也有应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a two-stage pseudo anomaly-guided anomaly detection method (\textbf{T}wo-stage \textbf{P}seudo \textbf{A}nomaly-guided \textbf{A}nomaly \textbf{D}etection, \textbf{TPA-AD}) for axle-box bearing time-series anomaly detection (time series anomaly detection, TSAD) under the setting where only normal samples are available for training. The method first generates pseudo-anomalous windows near the normal boundary using a reconstruction model and per-feature target-error control. It then learns anomaly-sensitive representations through contrastive learning between normal and pseudo-anomalous windows, and finally produces window-level and point-level anomaly scores using k-nearest neighbors (KNN). Compared with existing methods that rely on known fault categories, real anomaly priors, or random anomaly injection, TPA-AD improves the separability of the normal boundary by constructing pseudo-anomalies in boundary neighborhoods and can jointly handle continuous and discrete features in mixed-variable scenarios. The main experiments are conducted on bearing fault detection datasets and degradation-process datasets, with an additional exploratory extension on $13$ public TSAD datasets. The results show that the proposed method yields relatively stable anomaly responses, is sensitive to degradation evolution, and demonstrates a certain degree of broader applicability on public TSAD benchmarks and real high-speed-train-related bearing data.

</details>


### [47] [Adaptive Patching Is Harder Than It Looks For Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04074)
*Federico Zucchi,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Keyuan Luo,Thomas Lampert,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应分块（adaptive patching）在时间序列Transformer中的效果。通过将分块建模为预算比特率分配问题，推导了动态分块规则优于调优均匀分块的阈值条件。理论表明：局部复杂性不足以产生非均匀最优解；在表示感知最优下，对齐增益会坍缩。实验发现，在标准长时预测基准上，调优的均匀分块与动态分块性能相当，无一致优势。自适应分块的价值取决于能否廉价可靠地识别出能降低预测损失的精细分块区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自适应分块算子何时能优于调优的均匀分块算子，澄清"局部异构性即意味着自适应有益"这一直觉误区，为自适应分块方法的有效性提供理论边界和条件。

Method: 将分块建模为预算比特率分配问题，推导动态分块优于均匀分块的理论阈值（局部二次替代界和全局强凸性界）。通过消融实验，在三种代表性架构上移除自适应机制并做均匀分块扫描，进行受控对比研究。

Result: 理论揭示：无耦合约束下，标量局部复杂度在常见损失景观下不能产生非均匀最优；骨干网络训练至表示感知最优时，对齐增益在调优均匀分块附近坍缩。实验表明：验证集选择的均匀基线在标准长时预测基准上与动态方法竞争力相当，效应量集中在零附近，无一致方向优势，较大增益是方法-数据集特定的。

Conclusion: 自适应分块应锚定调优的均匀基线进行评估；其实际价值取决于能否廉价可靠地识别出能真正降低预测损失的细粒度分块区域，而非简单地在局部复杂区域进行更细划分。

Abstract: Adaptive patching is a recent and compelling proposal for time-series Transformers: allocate finer patches where the sequence looks locally informative. This paper asks under what conditions a content-adaptive patching operator should outperform a tuned uniform one. Local heterogeneity alone is not enough: under pointwise forecasting losses, a complex-looking region is not automatically one where finer patching reduces the loss. We model patching as a budgeted bitrate allocation and derive an explicit threshold that a dynamic patching rule must satisfy to beat a well-tuned uniform baseline, then bound the achievable improvement both locally (a quadratic surrogate) and globally (a strong-convexity bound under the model's assumptions). Two structural results follow: without a coupling constraint, scalar local complexity cannot produce a non-uniform optimum under a common loss landscape; and once the backbone is trained to its representation-aware optimum, the alignment gain collapses around a well-tuned uniform patch size. To test these predictions, we run a controlled isolation study on three representative architectures, replacing each adaptive mechanism with a uniform patch-size sweep while keeping the backbone, data, and training protocol fixed. On standard long-horizon forecasting benchmarks, the validation-selected uniform baseline is competitive with the dynamic counterpart, with per-setting effects concentrated near zero and no consistent directional advantage once results are aggregated by dataset. The larger gains we do observe are method- and dataset-specific. Adaptive patching should therefore be evaluated against a tuned uniform baseline; its value depends on whether a cheap and reliable routing signal can identify where finer patches actually reduce forecasting loss.

</details>


### [48] [Large Language Models Hack Rewards, and Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04075)
*Wei Liu,Xinyi Mou,Hanqi Yan,Zhongyu Wei,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习训练中奖励黑客行为会扩展到社会规则领域，导致模型学会钻社会规则漏洞（社会黑客行为）。作者构建了SocioHack沙盒环境（72个社会场景），验证了这种现象的自然发生，并指出现有LLM安全防护措施效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会规则与奖励函数在结构上相似（都有可衡量的结果、阈值和例外，但意图往往不完整），因此RL训练中模型已知的奖励黑客行为可能升级为更严重的"社会黑客行为"——钻社会规则的漏洞。

Method: 构建SocioHack沙盒环境（72个社会场景），在其中观察RL训练过程中模型是否会出现奖励黑客行为并发现社会规则漏洞。

Result: 奖励黑客行为在沙盒环境中自然涌现，模型学会了"技术合规但违背监管意图"的策略；现有LLM安全防护措施只能提供有限的缓解效果。

Conclusion: 收集真实世界反馈进行模型训练时需要更加谨慎，需要下一代后训练范式来安全地在真实社会中迭代LLM。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, enabling large language models (LLMs) to learn from rewards. We observe that societal regulations are structurally similar to reward functions. They define measurable outcomes, thresholds, and exceptions, while often leaving institutional intent only partially specified. We hypothesise that the RL training process may exploit these gaps and therefore ask whether models' well-known tendency to hack reward functions during RL can scale into a more consequential failure mode named societal hacking: discovering loopholes in the rules society runs on. To study this phenomenon, we introduce SocioHack, a sandbox of 72 societal environments, and find that within these environments, reward hacking naturally emerges and leads to regulatory loophole discovery. Models learn to hack the social rules and generate strategies that remain technically compliant while defeating regulatory intent, and current LLM safeguards provide only limited mitigation. Therefore, collecting in-the-wild feedback for model training requires greater caution, and we need a next-generation post-training paradigm for safely iterating LLMs in real society.=

</details>


### [49] [Stein Kernelized Molecular Dynamics for Active Learning of Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04100)
*Joanna Zou,Fraser Birks,Dallas Foster,Youssef Marzouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stein核化分子动力学（SKMD），一种用于机器学习原子间势主动学习与微调的增强采样方法，通过异步粒子更新和全局原子描述符核保留玻尔兹曼分布，结合自适应停止准则实现高效数据获取，在Müller-Brown势和丙氨酸二肽体系中以更少训练迭代达到更高模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）的性能高度依赖训练数据的质量和多样性，而现有增强采样方法在探索与采样的平衡以及数据获取效率方面存在不足，亟需一种既能高效探索构型空间又能保留玻尔兹曼分布的采样方法，以提升MLIPs的主动学习和微调效果。

Method: SKMD方法基于Stein变分梯度下降的随机变体，引入异步粒子更新机制和全局原子描述符核（提供对称性感知的构型相似性度量），通过保持玻尔兹曼分布为渐近分布来平衡构型探索与高概率区域吸引，并采用自适应停止准则实现非冗余训练数据的在线获取。

Result: 在Müller-Brown势的神经网络模型主动学习和丙氨酸二肽的MACE原子间势微调实验中，SKMD相比主动学习基线，在相同训练样本数量下以更少训练迭代达到更高的模型精度。

Conclusion: SKMD是一种有效的增强采样方法，能够在保持物理正确分布的同时高效获取多样化训练数据，显著提升MLIPs的主动学习和微调效率。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable efficient and accurate atomistic simulations but depend critically on the quality and diversity of the training data. We introduce Stein kernelized molecular dynamics (SKMD), an enhanced sampling method that uses interacting particle dynamics to acquire informative training configurations for the active learning and fine-tuning of MLIPs. SKMD corresponds to a stochastic variant of Stein variational gradient descent that is adapted for molecular dynamics by incorporating asynchronous particle updates and a kernel of global atomic descriptors, which provides a symmetry-aware measure of configurational similarity. Unlike other enhanced samplers used in molecular dynamics, SKMD preserves the Boltzmann distribution as the asymptotic distribution of the dynamics. This property enforces a balance between the exploration of diverse configurations and attraction toward high-probability regions of the energy landscape. We further propose an approach to efficient online data acquisition using an adaptive stopping criterion that selects non-redundant training data over the course of simulation. We demonstrate SKMD for the active learning of a neural network model of the Müller-Brown potential and the fine-tuning of a MACE interatomic potential for alanine dipeptide. Compared to active learning baselines, our method achieves higher model accuracy in fewer training iterations with the same number of acquired training samples.

</details>


### [50] [Variance Reduction for Heavy-Tailed Monetization Metrics in Ranking Experiments via Post-Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04110)
*Neeti Pokharna,Olivier Jeunen,Yatharth Saraf,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合事后分层与CUPED的方差缩减框架，用于排名/检索系统的在线实验，在ShareChat上实现约45%的流量节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评估排名和检索系统时，常用的货币化指标（如应用收入、创作者收益）呈现重尾分布，少量用户主导均值和方差，导致A/B实验统计功效低、结论不可靠，尤其在流量受限的情况下。

Method: 结合事后分层（Post-stratification）与CUPED（方差缩减技术），利用实验前的协变量信息来提升货币化实验的敏感度，无需额外流量。

Result: 在ShareChat的排名驱动的货币化实验中，该方法显著降低了方差并提高了决策稳定性，相比标准指标，达到等效统计置信度所需的流量减少约45%。

Conclusion: 讨论了实际设计选择、防护栏和局限性，为信息检索和推荐系统中何时适用事后分层提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Online evaluation of ranking and retrieval systems often relies on downstream monetization metrics such as app revenue or creator earnings. These metrics are typically heavy-tailed, with a small fraction of users dominating both mean and variance, leading to low statistical power and unreliable conclusions in A/B experiments -- especially under limited traffic.
  We present a practical framework for variance reduction in online experiments by combining post-stratification with CUPED. Our approach leverages pre-experiment covariates to improve the sensitivity of monetization experiments without requiring additional traffic. Deployed at ShareChat across ranking-driven monetization experiments, the method substantially reduces variance and improves decision stability, achieving equivalent statistical confidence with ~45\% less traffic than standard metrics. We further discuss practical design choices, guardrails, and limitations, providing guidance on when post-stratification is appropriate for real-world information retrieval and Recommendation systems.

</details>


### [51] [dMX: Differentiable Mixed-Precision Assignment for Low-Precision Floating-Point Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04115)
*Giuseppe Franco,Ian Colbert,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Felix Marty,Nicholas Fraser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: dMX是一个可微分的混合精度量化框架，通过学习分配每层的浮点位宽（基于OCP的MXFP标准），利用连续优化、温度退火和目标感知正则化，在LLM上实现了优于传统KL散度启发式方法的Pareto最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM量化方法对每层使用统一的位宽是次优的，而混合精度量化在离散格式间搜索时存在振荡问题。本文旨在提出一种可微分的、端到端可学习的浮点位宽分配方法，以更高效地探索质量与效率的Pareto前沿。

Method: 将每层的浮点格式参数化为单个可学习标量偏移，将多维设计空间折叠为一维；在训练中保持连续值，通过基于温度的退火调度逐步离散化到硬件兼容的MXFP格式；引入目标感知正则化项，将平均位宽朝向用户指定预算引导。

Result: 在Llama、Qwen3、SmolLM2等LLM族上，基于WikiText-2困惑度和四个零样本推理基准评估，dMX一致地生成Pareto支配模型，在模型质量与平均位宽之间的权衡上优于基于KL散度的层选择启发式方法。

Conclusion: dMX框架能有效学习LLM每层的最优浮点位宽分配，在保持模型质量的同时降低平均位宽，为混合精度量化部署提供了一种可微分的、硬件兼容的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantizing large language models (LLMs) to low-precision floating-point representations is central to efficient deployment, yet applying a single bit-width uniformly across all layers is sub-optimal in terms of both performance and accuracy. This work introduces dMX, a differentiable mixed-precision quantization framework for learnable floating-point bit-width assignment. We study its application for the microscaling floating-point (MXFP) family of data types defined by the Open Compute Project (OCP) standard. The per-layer bit-width assignment is formulated as a continuous optimization problem in which each layer's floating-point format format is parameterized by a scalar parameter, folding the multi-variate design space into a single learnable offset. During training this offset takes continuous values, avoiding sudden oscillations between discrete quantization formats. A temperature-based annealing schedule progressively discretizes the learned offsets, ensuring that the final configuration maps to hardware-compatible MXFP formats without abrupt transitions between training and inference behavior. A target-aware regularization term steers the average bit-width toward a user-specified budget, serving as a coarse-grained proxy for inference cost and balancing model quality against deployment efficiency. We performed experiments on different families of LLM, such as Llama, Qwen3, and SmolLM2, evaluating perplexity on WikiText-2 and accuracy on four zero-shot reasoning benchmarks. Across these settings, dMX consistently yields Pareto-dominating models and improves over Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence-based layer-selection heuristics, efficiently navigating trade-offs between model quality and average bit-width.

</details>


### [52] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning for Short-Term Flood Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04143)
*Tewodros Syum Gebre,Jagrati Talreja,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将水文知识以趋势对齐约束形式嵌入LSTM损失函数的物理信息机器学习框架，在数据稀缺场景下有效提升了洪水预测的物理一致性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在数据稀缺环境下表现不佳，且可能违反基本水文原理；标准LSTM在极端天气外推时会产生物理不一致的预测结果。

Method: 提出物理信息机器学习（PIML）框架，将水文知识以趋势对齐约束的形式直接融入LSTM的损失函数中，惩罚降水与径流趋势之间的方向不一致性，无需复杂的水动力学方程。

Result: 在仅使用5%训练数据时，NSE从0.20提升至0.23；在模拟极端气候压力测试中，基线模型出现不稳定行为，而PIML模型保持方向一致性和物理合理性。

Conclusion: 简单的物理约束能够显著提升深度学习模型在实时洪水预测中的可靠性，为无测站流域和气候变化条件下的洪水预报提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate flood forecasting is essential for mitigating disaster risks and protecting communities. However, purely data-driven machine learning models often struggle in data-scarce environments and may violate fundamental hydrological principles. Standard Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks can generate physically inconsistent predictions, particularly when extrapolating to extreme weather conditions. To address these limitations, we propose a Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) framework that incorporates hydrological knowledge directly into the loss function of an LSTM model. Specifically, a Trend Alignment constraint penalizes directional inconsistencies between precipitation and discharge trends, improving model robustness without requiring complex hydrodynamic equations. This regularization encourages the model to learn physically plausible hydrograph behavior, even with limited training data, while enhancing reliability during peak flood events. Experimental results show that the proposed physics-informed model outperforms a standard LSTM baseline in data-scarce settings, increasing the Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency (NSE) from 0.20 to 0.23 when trained on only 5% of the available data. Additional stress tests under simulated extreme climate scenarios demonstrate that the baseline model exhibits unstable behavior, whereas the physics-informed model maintains directional consistency and physical plausibility. Although accurately predicting extreme peak magnitudes remains challenging with limited data, the proposed approach substantially reduces unphysical fluctuations common in purely data-driven models. These findings demonstrate that simple physical constraints can significantly improve the reliability of deep learning models for real-time flood forecasting, offering a practical solution for ungauged basins and evolving climate conditions.

</details>


### [53] [EvalStop: Using World Feedback to Detect and Correct Reward Overoptimization in Multi-Tenant RLHF Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04145)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Shahryar Sarkani,John M. Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvalStop是一种基于评估分数连续下降检测的调度原语，用于在云LLM微调平台中自动终止因奖励过度优化（reward overoptimization）而"作弊"的RLHF任务，释放GPU资源并保留最佳检查点。在离散事件模拟器中，它实现98%精度/99%召回率/1.5%假阳性率，同时将JCT降低9%、浪费计算减少22%，且性能稳定于评估噪声和作弊比例变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云LLM微调平台的调度器忽视RLHF中奖励模型与真实世界反馈之间的分歧（reward overoptimization）：非先知型调度器仅优化JCT而无质量信号，SLAQ式调度器使用单调下降的训练损失作为代理（易被"作弊"欺骗），经典per-job早停需人工监督且不释放共享GPU。亟需一种自动、无监督、可组合的调度级早停机制来检测并终止"作弊"任务。

Method: 提出EvalStop调度原语：在任务连续k次评估分数下降时终止作业，释放GPU，保留最佳检查点，可委托给任意基础调度器。将调度器级早停形式化为检测问题，在离散事件模拟器中构造混合RLHF工作负载（包含reward-hacking和结构健康运行），真实标签对调度器隐藏。通过检测精度、召回率、假阳性率以及JCT和浪费计算等指标评估。

Result: 在80% RLHF、64 GPU的负载下：精度98%、召回率99%、假阳性率1.5%；相比SRTF-Est，JCT提升9%，浪费计算减少22%（p<0.05）。固定进度和loss-plateau基线分别产生65%假阳性率或漏检半数以上作弊。在所有基础调度器上增益稳定（JCT提升9-25%）；评估噪声标准差≤0.05时精度≥91%；作弊比例20-80%时精度≥89%。

Conclusion: EvalStop有效解决了RLHF调度中reward overoptimization导致的资源浪费问题，将早停从人工监控的per-job决策转变为调度器级自动化检测，在检测质量、调度性能和计算效率上均显著优于现有基线，且具有良好的组合性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cloud LLM fine-tuning platforms increasingly serve RLHF workloads, where a learned reward model is optimized as a proxy for human quality. As Gao et al. (2023) showed, this proxy diverges from world feedback (downstream eval metrics) under sustained optimization pressure, a phenomenon known as reward overoptimization. Existing platform schedulers ignore this divergence: non-clairvoyant schedulers optimize JCT without any quality signal, SLAQ-style quality-aware schedulers use training loss (a weaker proxy that drops monotonically through hacking), and classical per-job early stopping requires human monitoring and does not free shared GPUs. We propose EvalStop, a composable scheduling primitive that terminates jobs on k consecutive eval-score declines, releases GPUs, preserves the best checkpoint, and delegates to any base scheduler. We frame scheduler-level early stopping as a detection problem and evaluate it in a discrete-event simulator whose RLHF workload mixes reward-hacking and structurally healthy runs, with ground-truth labels hidden from schedulers. On RLHF-heavy workloads (80% RLHF, 64 GPUs), EvalStop achieves precision 98% / recall 99% / FPR 1.5% while improving JCT by 9% and cutting wasted compute by 22% over SRTF-Est (p<0.05). Trivial fixed-progress and loss-plateau competitors either incur 65% FPR on healthy RLHF or miss over half of true hacking cases. Gains compose across every base scheduler tested (9-25% JCT) and detection quality stays stable under eval noise (precision at least 91% at noise std <= 0.05) and hacking base rate (precision at least 89% across 20-80% hacking fractions).

</details>


### [54] [When Offline Selectors Cannot Beat the Best Single Model: A Diagnostic Study on edX Dropout Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04161)
*Tyler Crosse,Alan Nadelsticher Ruvalcaba,Dustin Khang LeDuc,Thomas Trask,Nicholas Lytle,David Joyner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段诊断方法，用于区分离线模型选择器失败的三种原因（学习器不匹配、状态不具区分性、标签漂移），并在edX辍学预测数据上验证：即使oracle比最强单模型高出9.7个准确率点，BC、DQN、CQL均无法超越单模型，瓶颈在于局部表示模糊性，应改进状态设计或收集新数据而非调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同预测器在不同输入上表现各异，按实例选择最佳模型有望获得比固定单一模型更高的准确率，但从离线日志中训练的选择器在实践中往往无法击败最强的单一预测器。作者希望系统性地诊断这一失败的根本原因，区分三种可能的归因（学习器不匹配、状态设计不足、标签漂移），从而指导后续改进方向。

Method: 提出三阶段诊断框架：(1) 通过k-NN标签一致性估计局部oracle恢复上限；(2) 检验BC、DQN、CQL等离线强化学习器能否达到该上限；(3) 消融选择器状态，测试更丰富的特征能否提高上限。在edX点击流数据上，对5个辍学预测模型进行16个时间窗口的离线选择实验。

Result: Oracle平均比最强单模型高9.7个准确率点，但BC、DQN、CQL均落在同一较低准确率区间内（对十倍缓冲区和2000个留出样本鲁棒）。CQL缩小了与oracle的模仿差距但未带来部署增益，三种学习器的遗憾值紧密聚集，测试准确率趋同，表明瓶颈是局部表示模糊性而非保守主义或标签漂移。

Conclusion: 当前瓶颈在于状态设计不能有效区分哪个模型在局部最优，而非离线学习器训练不足。下一步应改进状态设计或收集新数据，而非继续调优离线强化学习器。

Abstract: Different predictors often excel on different inputs, so picking the best one per instance promises higher accuracy than committing to a single model. In practice, selectors trained from logged data routinely fail to beat the strongest single predictor. Three causes typically go unseparated before more tuning is applied: a mismatched learner, a state that does not predict which model wins, or buffer-to-deployment label shift.
  A three-stage diagnostic rules them out on a shared buffer. Stage~1 estimates a local ceiling on oracle recovery from $k$-NN label consistency. Stage~2 asks whether paired BC and offline-RL learners (BC, DQN, and CQL across penalty weights) reach that ceiling. Stage~3 ablates the selector state to test whether richer features would raise it. The combined verdict points to the most promising next step: tuning the learner, redesigning the state, or collecting new data.
  We apply it to selecting among five dropout-prediction models on edX clickstream data. Across 16 windows, the oracle beats the strongest single base model by 9.7 accuracy points on average, yet BC, DQN, and CQL land in the same test-accuracy band below it (robust to a tenfold buffer sweep and $N{=}2{,}000$ held-out examples). The bottleneck is local representational ambiguity: CQL closes the imitation gap without a deployment gain (not conservatism), regret clusters tightly across learners (not tie-breaking), and the three learners converge on test accuracy (not shift). The next iteration should change the state or collect new data, not tune the offline learner further.

</details>


### [55] [ADAPTOOD: Uncertainty-Aware Fine-Tuning for Out-of-Distribution ECG Time Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04164)
*Sotirios Vavaroutas,Yu Yvonne Wu,Ali Etemad,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADAPTOOD框架，利用数据不确定性量化分布偏移严重程度，指导时序模型的微调，在OOD任务上准确率提升最高7%，精确率提升最高12.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有适应方法假设固定分布偏移，无法处理多种类型或严重程度的偏移，且忽视了偏移严重性（如将大熟悉数据集适应与小数据集新任务适应等同对待），导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出ADAPTOOD框架，利用数据不确定性量化目标部署分布与预训练分布之间的偏差程度作为OOD严重性的直接信号，结合低秩模型更新和自适应超参数优化来指导时序模型的微调。

Result: 在OOD任务上，ADAPTOOD比现有方法准确率提升最高7%，精确率提升最高12.9%，且随分布偏移严重程度增加仍保持强性能。

Conclusion: ADAPTOOD通过量化偏移严重性来指导自适应微调，有效提升模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Data samples used for training often differ from those encountered during fine-tuning and deployment, and while ML models show promise, their performance remains limited when only small annotated datasets are available. Performance often degrades under distribution shifts caused by diverse sensors, populations, and application settings. Although pre-training helps, models frequently encounter out-of-distribution (OOD) data in real-world settings, leading to reduced robustness. Existing adaptation methods usually assume fixed distribution shifts and struggle when multiple types or severities occur. In particular, they overlook shift severity, for example treating adaptation to a large familiar dataset the same as adaptation to a small dataset with a new task, which limits generalisation. To address this, we propose ADAPTOOD, a novel framework that leverages data uncertainty to quantify distribution shift severity and guide fine-tuning for time series. This uncertainty measures how strongly samples from the target deployment distribution deviate from the pre-training distribution, providing a direct signal of OOD severity. Our framework combines this uncertainty with low-rank model updates and adaptive hyperparameter optimisation to improve adaptation. We show that ADAPTOOD achieves up to 7% higher accuracy and 12.9% higher precision than existing methods in OOD tasks, maintaining strong performance as distribution shift severity increases.

</details>


### [56] [Smart Transportation Without Neurons -- Fair Metro Network Expansion with Tabular Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04167)
*Dimitris Michailidis,Sennay Ghebreab,Fernando P. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对地铁网络扩展问题（MNEP），证明其规模无需深度强化学习，通过将问题重新表述为非马尔可夫奖励决策过程（NMRDP）并采用表格型强化学习，在显著减少训练轮次和碳排放的同时达到与深度强化学习相当的性能，且具有更强的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，精确算法和启发式方法依赖专家定义约束来缩小搜索空间，而深度强化学习虽有效但计算昂贵、环境成本高且可解释性差。作者旨在探索MNEP问题是否真的需要深度强化学习，并寻求更高效、可解释且环保的替代方案。

Method: 将MNEP重新表述为非马尔可夫奖励决策过程（NMRDP），采用表格型强化学习（tabular RL）替代深度强化学习，并将社会公平性标准（效率与公平）纳入奖励函数设计。

Result: 在西安和阿姆斯特丹的真实场景评估中，该方法平均将总训练轮次减少18倍，总碳排放减少12倍，同时性能与深度强化学习保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可复现、模块化、可解释且资源高效的解决方案，并具有推广到其他组合优化问题的潜力。

Abstract: We tackle the Metro Network Expansion Problem (MNEP), a subset of the Transport Network Design Problem (TNDP), which focuses on expanding metro systems to satisfy travel demand. Traditional methods rely on exact and heuristic approaches that require expert-defined constraints to reduce the search space. Recently, deep reinforcement learning (Deep RL) has emerged due to its effectiveness in complex sequential decision-making processes-it remains, however, computationally expensive, environmentally costly, and requires additional engineering to interpret. We show that MNEP problems are small enough to not require Deep RL methods. Reformulating the MNEP as a Non-Markovian Rewards Decision Process (NMRDP), we use tabular RL to achieve similar performance with significantly fewer training episodes, additionally offering greater interpretability. Additionally, we incorporate social equity criteria into the reward functions, focusing on efficiency and fairness, highlighting the versatility of our method. Evaluated in real-world settings-Xi'an and Amsterdam-our method reduces total episodes by a factor of 18 and total carbon emissions by a factor of 12 on average, while remaining competitive with Deep RL. This approach offers a replicable, modular, interpretable, and resource-efficient solution with potential applications to other combinatorial optimization problems.

</details>


### [57] [Low-rank Distributional Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04176)
*Jiayi Wang,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分布化矩阵补全问题，其中矩阵元素为概率分布而非标量。通过核均值嵌入表示分布，引入分布值矩阵的Tucker秩概念，并提出基于函数展开算子的估计方法，建立了非渐近误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矩阵补全处理标量值矩阵，但在许多实际问题中，矩阵元素本身是概率分布（如不同地区的人口年龄分布），且只能观察到来自这些分布的有限样本。现有方法无法直接处理这种分布值矩阵的补全问题。

Method: 利用核均值嵌入将分布映射到再生核希尔伯特空间；引入分布值矩阵的Tucker秩概念刻画低秩结构；提出函数展开算子将分布值低秩结构与有限维张量的经典Tucker秩联系起来；基于此框架构造分布化矩阵补全估计量。

Result: 建立了估计量的非渐近误差界，在合成数据和真实数据上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 成功将矩阵补全从标量值推广到分布值场景，提出的框架和方法在理论上具有可靠的统计保证，在实际应用中表现良好。

Abstract: We study a distributional generalization of the matrix completion problem in which each entry of the target matrix is a probability distribution rather than a scalar. In this setting, only a subset of matrix entries is observed, and even for observed entries, the underlying distributions are not directly accessible; instead, we observe finitely many samples drawn from them. To represent distributional entries, we employ kernel mean embeddings and introduce a notion of Tucker rank for distribution-valued matrices to capture their low-rank structure. The infinite-dimensional nature of kernel embeddings poses significant methodological challenges. To address this, we introduce functional unfolding operators that link the proposed distributional low-rank structure to the classical Tucker rank for finite-dimensional tensors. Based on this framework, we propose a novel estimator for distributional matrix completion. We establish non-asymptotic error bounds that characterize the statistical performance of the estimator. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [58] [KODA: Contrastive Representation Comparison and Alignment for Vision-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04180)
*Youqi Wu,Mohammad Jalali,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KODA框架，通过核方法对比分析CLIP、SigLIP等视觉-语言基础模型在表示结构上的差异，识别不同模型间聚类不一致的样本子集，并提供可解释的差异方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（如CLIP、SigLIP）通常仅通过下游任务性能进行比较，未能解释它们表示结构上的根本差异。

Method: 提出Kernel Optimization for Discrepancy Analysis (KODA)，通过模态核组合构建统一多模态核，将差异发现建模为约束优化问题，在一种表示中寻找连贯结构的同时抑制另一种表示中的连贯性；为扩展至大规模数据集，采用随机投影（包括随机傅里叶特征）进行低维近似。

Result: KODA能够识别出一致且可解释的差异结构，并提供用于表示对齐的样本子集。

Conclusion: KODA为对比分析多模态基础模型的表示差异提供了一种可解释且可扩展的核方法框架。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models such as CLIP and SigLIP provide widely used representations for multimodal learning systems. While these models are typically compared through downstream performance, such evaluations often do not explain how their representations differ structurally. In this work, we study this problem through the task of Contrastive Embedding Clustering: identifying sample subsets that are weakly clustered under one representation but strongly clustered under another. We propose \emph{Kernel Optimization for Discrepancy Analysis (KODA)}, a kernel-based framework for contrastive representation comparison and alignment. KODA constructs unified multimodal kernels through modality-wise kernel composition and formulates discrepancy discovery as a constrained optimization problem that searches for coherent structures in one representation while suppressing coherence in a reference representation. This yields interpretable discrepancy directions associated with specific sample subsets and modality interactions. To scale KODA to large vision-language datasets, we develop randomized low-dimensional approximations of joint kernels using random projections, including Random Fourier Features for shift-invariant kernels. Empirically, KODA identifies consistent and interpretable discrepancy structures across vision-language representations and provides sample subsets for representation alignment. The code is available at https://github.com/yokiwuuu/KODA.

</details>


### [59] [Exact Unlearning in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04182)
*Thanh Nguyen-Tang,Raman Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了强化学习中的精确遗忘问题，设计了一种支持用户数据删除的算法，其计算成本仅为从头重新训练的$ρ\sqrt{\ln T}$比例，并在表格型MDP上实现了近乎最优的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决强化学习中用户数据删除请求后的精确遗忘问题，使得在线学习者在遗忘后的输出与用户从未交互过的输出不可区分，同时避免昂贵的从头重新训练。

Method: 提出了一种$ρ$-TV稳定的强化学习算法，支持精确遗忘过程，其期望计算成本仅为重训练成本的$ρ\sqrt{\ln T}$比例；算法基于表格型MDP，并给出了遗憾界分析。

Result: 算法的遗憾界为$\mathcal{O}(H^2 \sqrt{SAT} + H^3 S^2 A + {H^{2.5} S^2 A}/ρ)$；同时建立了下界$\Omega(H\sqrt{SAT} + {SAH}/ρ)$，表明算法近乎最小最大最优。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地研究了强化学习中的精确遗忘问题，所提算法在理论上具有近乎最优的遗憾保证，且遗忘的计算开销远低于从头再训练。

Abstract: We formulate the problem of \emph{exact unlearning} in reinforcement learning, where the goal is to design an efficient framework that enables the removal of any user's data upon deletion request, i.e., the online learner's output after unlearning is \emph{indistinguishable} from what would have been produced had the deleted user never interacted with the learner. For any $ρ>0$, we show that there exists a reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm that is $ρ$-TV-stable and supports an exact unlearning procedure whose expected computational cost is only a $ρ\sqrt{\ln T}$ fraction of the computational cost of retraining from scratch. We construct such a $ρ$-TV-stable RL algorithm for tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs), which achieves a regret bound of $\mathcal{O}(H^2 \sqrt{SAT} + H^3 S^2 A + {H^{2.5} S^2 A}/ρ)$, where $S, A, H$, and $T$ denote the number of states, the number of actions, the episode horizon, and the number of episodes, respectively. We also establish a lower bound of $Ω(H\sqrt{\!SAT}\! +\! {SAH}/ρ)$ for $ρ$-TV-stable RL algorithms, showing that our algorithm is nearly minimax optimal.

</details>


### [60] [Folded Transport MCMC: Certifiable Quotient Posterior Computation for Symmetric Bayesian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04307)
*Jun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对贝叶斯有限对称模型（如具有可交换分量的混合模型）中因标签置换对称性导致的后验冗余多模态问题，提出Folded Transport MCMC（FolT-MCMC）方法，通过在对称群的基本域上构建独立采样器直接对商后验进行推断，利用对称化的归一化流构造商空间提议分布，并建立了基于LCNF振荡的认证框架在商度量下的理论保证。实验表明，在多种混合模型和真实台风建筑加速度计数据上，该方法将认证下界提升2-145倍，且折叠后认证几乎与维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯模型中的有限对称性（如混合模型的可交换分量、结构识别中的密集模态）会导致后验分布在标签置换群作用下具有不变性，产生冗余的多模态结构，严重影响MCMC收敛诊断的性能。

Method: 提出Folded Transport MCMC（FolT-MCMC），通过在对称群的基本域上构建独立采样器，直接对商后验进行推断。商空间提议分布通过对群轨道上的归一化流进行对称化得到。同时证明了基于LCNF振荡的认证框架可迁移到商度量上，并给出了稳定子修正的球-质量界和改进的覆盖半径。

Result: 在高斯混合（d=2-20）、标签切换目标（最多24个等价模态）和标准贝叶斯三分量混合后验上，量化核认证改进比例为2倍到145倍，折叠后认证几乎与维度无关。在台风山竹期间超高层建筑加速度计数据上，FolT-MCMC给出了非空洞的量化核认证，而未折叠的认证是空洞的。

Conclusion: FolT-MCMC通过折叠对称性有效解决了标签置换导致的后验冗余多模态问题，显著提升了认证下界，且具有近似的维度无关性，在真实应用场景中表现优于传统未折叠方法。

Abstract: Bayesian models with finite symmetry - mixture models with exchangeable components, structural identification with closely-spaced modes - define posteriors that are invariant under a group of label permutations, creating redundant multimodality that degrades MCMC convergence diagnostics. We introduce Folded Transport MCMC (FolT-MCMC), which performs inference directly on the quotient posterior by constructing an independence sampler on the fundamental domain of the symmetry group. The quotient proposal is formed by symmetrising a learned normalising flow over the group orbits. We prove that the LCNF oscillation-based certification framework transfers to the quotient metric with a stabiliser-corrected ball-mass bound and improved covering radius, and that the quantile-core certified lower bound improves whenever the unfolded flow exhibits cross-mode proposal deficiency. On Gaussian mixtures (d = 2 - 20), label-switching targets (up to 24 equivalent modes), and a standard Bayesian three-component mixture posterior, the quantile-core certified improvement ratio ranges from 2x to 145x, with the folded certificate empirically nearly dimension-free. On real accelerometer data from a supertall building during Typhoon Mangkhut, FolT-MCMC yields a non-vacuous quantile-core certificate where the unfolded certificate is vacuous.

</details>


### [61] [OpenRFM: Dissecting Relational In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04320)
*Zhikai Chen,Junyu Yin,Jialiang Gu,Siheng Xiong,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Keren Zhou,Kai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了关系型基础模型（RFM）的代表性框架——关系Transformer（RT），从模型端和数据端揭示了其性能瓶颈：模型端RT执行关系级上下文学习，在标签稀疏时退化为欠定回归；数据端纯合成预训练导致懒惰学习。据此提出OpenRFM，通过双阶段上下文学习架构（结合关系骨干网络与表格基础模型的批量级上下文学习层）和同质性感知的合成+持续真实数据预训练混合物（辅以原型正则化），在评估任务上相较RT提升约30%性能，并超越商业模型KumoRFMv1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用RFM与开源RFM之间存在显著性能差距，但这一差距的起源尚未被系统理解。本文旨在剖析代表性框架RT的局限性，并据此弥合该差距。

Method: 1) 模型诊断：通过核回归视角证明RT的关系级ICL在标签-单元格覆盖稀疏时失败；2) 数据诊断：消融预训练源，发现纯合成预训练与分布内预训练分别驱动模型进入懒惰学习和特征学习两种不同机制；3) 提出OpenRFM：双阶段ICL架构（关系骨干+来自预训练表格基础模型的批量级ICL层）解决标签稀疏，同质性感知的合成+持续真实数据预训练混合物，辅以原型正则化。

Result: OpenRFM在大量评估任务上相较RT骨干网络平均性能提升约30%，并超越商业模型KumoRFMv1。

Conclusion: 性能差距的关键缺失成分是标签生成过程中支持可识别的关系潜在变量；通过模型架构改进和数据混合策略可有效弥合开源RFM与商用RFM之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Relational Foundation Models (RFMs) promise a single pre-trained predictor that, given any relational database, returns predictions in one forward pass via relational in-context learning (ICL). Yet a substantial gap separates open RFMs from their commercial counterparts, and the origin of this gap has not been systematically understood. We dissect a representative framework, the Relational Transformer (RT), from two perspectives. Model side: we show that RT performs relation-level ICL, and a kernel regression view shows it fails when sparse label-cell coverage yields an underdetermined regression. Data side: we ablate RT's pre-training source and find that existing synthetic-only pre-training and in-distribution pre-training drive the same architecture into different regimes, lazy vs. feature-learning. Probing this gap reveals that the missing ingredient is a support-identifiable relational latent in the label-generation process. These two diagnoses translate into (1) a dual-stage ICL architecture that combines the relational backbone with a batch-level ICL layer lifted from a pre-trained tabular foundation model to overcome relation-level label scarcity, and (2) a homophily-aware synthetic plus continual real-data pre-training mixture, augmented with a prototype-based regularization. These choices define OpenRFM, a simple yet effective RFM that improves average task performance by approximately 30% over the RT backbone and surpasses the commercial model KumoRFMv1 on a large set of evaluation tasks.

</details>


### [62] [Federated Learning for Multi-Center Sepsis Early Prediction with Privacy-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04338)
*Xixi Tian,Di Wu,Xiang Liu,Yiziting Zhu,Yujie Li,Xin Shu,Bin Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了联邦学习在多中心脓毒症预测中的应用，使用来自三家中国三甲医院的648份临床样本。实验表明，联邦学习模型在预测精度上与集中式模型高度接近，同时能从根本上避免隐私泄露，并能有效抵抗数据重构攻击，为隐私保护下的多中心医疗协作提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心医疗数据的隐私敏感性和分布式特性严重阻碍了集中式建模用于脓毒症的早期准确预测，亟需一种既能保护隐私又能实现协同建模的解决方案。

Method: 以648份来自三家三甲医院的临床筛查样本为数据集，建立集中式训练范式作为性能基线，然后实现水平联邦学习框架进行分布式协同建模，并开展隐私安全性分析。

Result: 联邦学习模型在预测精度上与集中式模型高度可比，同时从根本上避免隐私泄露；隐私安全分析证实恶意攻击者无法从传输的模型参数中重构原始患者数据，具备强大的抗数据重构攻击能力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了联邦学习在临床脓毒症预测中的实用性和安全性，为隐私保护下的多中心医疗协作提供了可靠且可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy-sensitive and distributed characteristics of multi-center medical data bring severe obstacles to centralized modeling for accurate early prediction of sepsis. Federated learning (FL) has attracted growing attention as a promising framework for collaborative model development, as it allows multiple institutions to jointly train predictive models without directly sharing or centralizing raw data. Nevertheless, its practical performance, robustness, and privacy-preserving benefits remain insufficiently evaluated using real-world clinical datasets. To bridge this gap, this study systematically examines the application of federated learning to multi-center sepsis prediction. The experimental dataset consists of 648 clinically screened samples collected from three tertiary hospitals in China, with rigorous inclusion and exclusion criteria. We establish a centralized training paradigm as the performance baseline, and then implement a horizontal federated learning framework for distributed collaborative modeling. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the federated learning-based model achieves highly comparable prediction accuracy to the centralized counterpart, while fundamentally avoiding privacy leakage. Further privacy security analysis verifies that malicious attackers cannot reconstruct the original patient data from the transmitted model parameters, indicating strong resistance against data reconstruction attacks. This work not only validates the practicality and security of federated learning in clinical sepsis prediction, but also provides a reliable and feasible solution for privacy-preserving multi-center medical collaboration.

</details>


### [63] [Literature-Guided Minimax Optimization of Virtual Epilepsy Neurostimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04339)
*Cathy Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种文献引导的极小极大（minimax）优化管线，将PubMed文献挖掘、TVB癫痫模型仿真与大语言模型引导的黑箱优化相结合，旨在设计对最差情况虚拟患者依然鲁棒的神经调控方案。内在模型控制参数优化使最差情况奖励提升39.8%，但临床风格的外部刺激搜索改善仅1.7%，且20例虚拟患者队列未显示统计显著获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经调控优化仅追求平均响应性能，忽视了最不耐受刺激患者群体的失败风险。作者旨在设计一种对最差情况患者依然鲁棒的优化方法。

Method: 构建了文献引导的极小极大管线：(1) PubMed文献挖掘提取先验知识；(2) TVB Epileptor模型进行虚拟患者仿真；(3) 大语言模型引导的黑箱优化搜索参数，目标函数最大化最差情况奖励（即模拟癫痫活动方差的负值）。

Result: 内在模型控制参数优化：最差情况奖励从-0.5285提升至-0.3182（提升39.8%）。外部刺激搜索：最差情况仅改善1.7%，20例虚拟患者队列无统计显著获益（p=0.9019），但55%患者有响应，且颞叶亚组呈现阳性信号。

Conclusion: 该研究为鲁棒的、文献感知的神经刺激设计提供了in silico概念验证，而非临床证据。

Abstract: Computational models of epilepsy promise patient-specific treatment design, but most optimization workflows still search for parameters that perform well on average. In neuromodulation, this is a weak target: a protocol that improves the mean response can still fail in the patient whose network is least tolerant to stimulation. We present a literature-guided minimax pipeline that couples PubMed-scale hypothesis extraction, The Virtual Brain (TVB) Epileptor simulations, and large-language-model-guided black-box optimization. The optimizer proposes either intrinsic model-control parameters or clinically interpretable external-stimulation protocols; TVB evaluates each proposal across sampled virtual patients; and the objective maximizes worst-case reward, defined as the negative variance of simulated seizure activity. In the intrinsic model-control experiment, the best archived parameter set improved worst-case reward from -0.5285 to -0.3182, a 39.8% gain over baseline. The clinical-style external-stimulation search produced a much smaller worst-case improvement (1.7%), and a 20-patient virtual cohort showed no aggregate benefit (p=0.9019), despite a 55% responder rate and a positive temporal-lobe subgroup signal. The study should be read as an in silico proof of concept for robust, literature-aware neurostimulation design, not as clinical evidence.

</details>


### [64] [When Do Fewer Coordinates Suffice in DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04375)
*Huiqi Zhang,Fang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TP-TopK，一种无需公共数据的两阶段坐标稀疏私有训练方法，通过在私有预热阶段识别坐标支撑集并在主训练阶段仅更新该子集，使噪声能量从全维度d降至有效维度k，理论给出了坐标限制有益的条件和非凸驻点界，实验验证了该方法在MNIST、FMNIST和CIFAR-10上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）在每一轮更新中对所有坐标注入噪声，导致注入噪声能量随参数维度d线性增长。本文旨在探究私密训练何时可以只更新少量坐标而不丢失优化所需的信号，从而降低噪声规模。

Method: 提出TP-TopK（Two-Phase TopK DP-SGD）两阶段方法：第一阶段是私有预热阶段，通过DP方式识别出重要的坐标支撑集；第二阶段是主训练阶段，仅在该支撑集上进行梯度更新和加噪。给出了坐标限制有益的条件判据，推导了非凸条件下噪声项与有效维度k（而非全维度d）成比例的驻点界，并给出了基于预热坐标排序可靠性的下界。

Result: 在MNIST、FMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，学习得到的坐标支撑集相比同等大小的随机支撑集保留了更多的梯度能量，当有效维度较小且预热分数信息量充足时增益最大。

Conclusion: TP-TopK通过两阶段坐标稀疏策略，在无需公共数据的情况下有效降低了DP-SGD的噪声维度依赖，为高维参数的差分隐私优化提供了新的高效途径。

Abstract: Differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) injects noise into every updated coordinate, making the injected noise energy scale with the ambient parameter dimension \(d\). We ask when private training can update fewer coordinates without losing the signal needed for optimization. We propose \textsc{TP-TopK} (Two-Phase TopK DP-SGD), a two-phase method for coordinate-sparse private training without public data, in which a private warm-up phase identifies a coordinate support used to guide the main training phase. We give a criterion characterizing when coordinate restriction can be beneficial, show via a nonconvex stationarity bound that under this condition the relevant noise term scales with the active dimension \(k\) rather than the full parameter dimension \(d\), and provide a lower bound on the reliability of warm-up-based coordinate ranking. Experiments on MNIST, FMNIST, and CIFAR-10 show that learned coordinate supports can retain more gradient energy than size-matched random supports, with the largest gains when the active dimension is small and warm-up scores are informative.

</details>


### [65] [From Symbolic to Geometric: Enabling Spatial Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04381)
*Chen Chu,Bita Azarijoo,Li Xiong,Khurram Shafique,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出空间语言模型(SLM)，首个将位置信息作为第一模态的多模态大语言模型，通过直接操作学习到的空间表示而非空间关系的文本描述，实现几何空间推理。实验表明SLM在空间推理任务上显著优于现有基于符号推理的LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽表现出空间推理能力，但本质上是基于文本模式匹配的符号推理，缺乏对连续空间表示、显式几何计算和结构化空间算子的原生支持，无法实现真正的几何空间推理。

Method: 1) 提出SLM架构，将位置信息作为独立模态直接融入LLM推理过程；2) 构建空间指令数据集(Spatial Instruction Dataset)，对齐空间表示、原子几何操作与自然语言指令；3) 提出空间推理评估基准SpatialEval，涵盖属性、距离、拓扑和相对位置四类任务。

Result: SLM在属性、距离、拓扑和相对位置等空间推理任务上显著优于现有基于提示工程或文本抽象的符号推理LLM方法，证明了集成几何空间表示对鲁棒空间推理的优越性。

Conclusion: 将几何空间表示作为第一模态直接集成到LLM推理过程中，相比依赖文本符号的推理方式，能显著提升空间推理的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) often appear to exhibit spatial reasoning ability; however, this capability is largely \emph{symbolic}, arising from pattern matching over spatial language rather than true \emph{geometric} reasoning over space. Because LLMs operate on discrete tokens, they lack native support for continuous spatial representations, explicit geometric computation, and structured spatial operators. To address this limitation, we introduce the \emph{Spatial Language Model (SLM)}, the first multimodal LLM that treats location information as a first-class modality and enables geometric spatial reasoning within the model's inference process. SLM directly operates on learned spatial representations rather than textual descriptions of spatial relations. To support effective training, we construct a \emph{Spatial Instruction Dataset} that aligns spatial representations, atomic geometric operations, and natural language instructions. We further propose a new benchmark named \emph{SpatialEval}, which is designed to evaluate spatial reasoning across attributes, distance, topology, and relative-position tasks. Extensive experiments show that SLM significantly outperforms existing LLM-based approaches that rely on symbolic reasoning via prompt engineering or textual abstraction, demonstrating the benefits of integrating geometric spatial representations for robust spatial reasoning.
  Our instruction dataset, evaluation benchmark, model training codes, and models' checkpoints can be found at:
  \hyperlink{https://github.com/chuchen2017/SLM}{https://github.com/chuchen2017/SLM}.

</details>


### [66] [Physics-Informed Neural Network Modeling of Biodegradable Contaminant Transport through GCL/SL Composite Liners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04392)
*Dong Li,Yapeng Cao,Haiping Zhao,Shutong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两域物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，用于模拟GCL/SL复合衬垫系统中的污染物输运，其中硬约束PINN（H-PINN）通过将边界/初始条件嵌入试解中，比标准PINN显著提高了预测精度（MAE从0.058-0.067降至0.011-0.023，MRE从9.10%-19.16%降至2.08%-3.14%），并可扩展至逆问题求解以识别污染物半衰期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GCL/SL复合衬垫系统中污染物输运建模的挑战，特别是标准PINN在早期输运阶段和高水力水头条件下误差较大的问题，以及通过软约束施加物理定律时优化负担重的问题。

Method: 提出两域PINN框架：将GCL层处理为稳态平流-扩散-生物降解方程，将下层土衬垫处理为瞬态输运域。对比了标准PINN（软约束）和硬约束PINN（H-PINN，将边界/初始条件直接嵌入试解）两种方案，使用tanh激活函数并优化网络结构，并扩展至逆问题识别SL降解半衰期。

Result: Std-PINN可捕捉整体穿透行为但在早期输运阶段误差较大；H-PINN将MAE从0.058-0.067降至0.011-0.023，MRE从9.10%-19.16%降至2.08%-3.14%。tanh激活函数与优化网络结构提供最佳预测精度。H-PINN逆求解在低至中等观测噪声下对半衰期识别具有可靠收敛性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: H-PINN通过硬约束嵌入有效降低了优化负担，在复合衬砌输运问题中精度和稳定性显著优于标准PINN，且具备良好的逆问题求解能力。

Abstract: This study develops a two-domain physics-informed neural network framework for contaminant transport through a GCL/SL composite liner system, in which the thin GCL layer is treated using a steady-state advection-dispersion-biodegradation formulation and the underlying soil liner is modeled as a transient transport domain. Two formulations are evaluated against analytical and finite-element reference solutions under different leachate-head conditions: a standard PINN with soft constraint enforcement (Std-PINN) and a hard-constrained PINN (H-PINN), in which selected boundary and initial conditions are embedded directly into the trial solutions. The Std-PINN captures the overall breakthrough behavior but shows larger errors during the early transport stage, particularly under higher leachate heads where advective transport becomes more pronounced. The H-PINN reduces the optimization burden associated with penalty-based constraint enforcement and provides more accurate and stable concentration predictions, lowering the MAE from approximately 0.058-0.067 for the Std-PINN to about 0.011-0.023 for the H-PINN, while reducing the MRE from approximately 9.10%-19.16% to about 2.08%-3.14%. Parametric analyses confirm that the H-PINN with the tanh activation function and an optimized network structure provides the best predictive accuracy. The H-PINN is further extended to inverse modeling for identifying the SL degradation half-life from limited concentration observations, showing reliable convergence toward prescribed values and acceptable robustness under low-to-moderate observation noise.

</details>


### [67] [DPDL: Towards Differential Privacy Preservation in Decentralized Stochastic Learning on Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04399)
*Yunsheng Yuan,Xue Xiao,Lina Wang,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于差分隐私和余弦相似度校准的隐私保护去中心化学习算法DPDL，用于处理非独立同分布数据场景下的隐私泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习中，智能体间交换梯度信息存在严重隐私泄露风险，且实际应用中训练数据通常是非独立同分布的，这给隐私保护去中心化学习带来了更多挑战。

Method: 利用差分隐私概念，每个智能体在共享前对跨梯度（邻居局部模型在其私有数据上的导数）添加高斯噪声扰动；然后采用余弦相似度校准接收到的扰动跨梯度，将校准后的跨梯度以动量方式聚合用于更新局部模型。

Result: 理论分析给出了达到特定隐私保护级别所需的最小噪声水平，并证明算法在非IID数据下仍能实现线性加速；实验验证了算法在防御隐私攻击和训练准确模型方面的有效性。

Conclusion: DPDL算法通过差分隐私噪声扰动和相似度校准技术，有效解决了非IID数据场景下去中心化学习中的隐私保护问题，并保持了良好的训练效率。

Abstract: In the paradigm of decentralized learning, a group of agents collaborate to train a global model using distributed datasets without a central server. Although the power of collaboration has been verified by many state-of-the-art studies, it entails extensive gradient information exchanging among the agents and thus induces high risk of privacy leakage for the individual agents. Moreover, in real-world applications, the training data are usually non-identically and independently distributed across the agents, inducing more challenges to enable privacy-preserved decentralized learning. To address these issues, we propose a privacy-preserved decentralized learning algorithm with non-IID data, DPDL, which leverages the notion of Differential Privacy (DP) in cross-gradient aggregation through a similarity-based calibration technique. Specifically, in each round, each agent perturbs the cross-gradients (i.e., the derivatives of its neighbors' local model in its private local data) by Gaussian noise mechanism before sharing them with its neighbors; it then adopt cosine similarity to calibrate the received perturbed cross-gradients such that the aggregation of the calibrated cross-gradients can be utilized to effectively update local model in a momentum-like manner. Our rigorous theoretical analysis not only reveals the minimum noise level required to achieve a specific level of privacy preservation, but also illustrates that our algorithm still achieves a linear speedup in training with non-IID data. We finally conduct extensive experiments on real-world dataset to validate the effectiveness of our algorithm in defending privacy attacks and in training accurate models.

</details>


### [68] [TANDEM: Bi-Level Data Mixture Optimization with Twin Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04401)
*Jiaxing Wang,Deping Xiang,Jin Xu,Mingyang Yi,Guoqiang Gong,Zicheng Zhang,Haoran Li,Pengzhang Liu,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang,Ju Fan,Qixiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TANDEM通过双网络（代理模型和参考模型）的差异度量数据效能，优化领域混合比例，在双层优化框架下显著提升LLM训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能力高度依赖各领域训练数据的混合比例，现有领域重加权方法缺乏理论保证且适用性有限，需要更优的优化方案。

Method: 将领域混合比例优化建模为双层优化问题，简化为单层惩罚形式，使用双网络——代理模型（主数据训练）和参考模型（增量数据训练），通过两者差异衡量数据效能，对受益更大的领域提高权重。

Result: 在多种场景（包括数据受限场景和监督微调）下，优化后的混合比例显著提升模型性能，实验验证了TANDEM的有效性。

Conclusion: TANDEM提供了理论保证和更广泛的适用性，并揭示了数据受限和微调等新场景下领域重加权的研究价值。

Abstract: The capabilities of large language models (LLMs) significantly depend on training data drawn from various domains. Optimizing domain-specific mixture ratios can be modeled as a bi-level optimization problem, which we simplify into a single-level penalized form and solve with twin networks: a proxy model trained on primary data and a dynamically updated reference model trained with additional data. Our proposed method, Twin Networks for bi-level DatA mixturE optiMization (TANDEM), measures the data efficacy through the difference between the twin models and up-weights domains that benefit more from the additional data. TANDEM provides theoretical guarantees and wider applicability, compared to prior approaches. Furthermore, our bi-level perspective suggests new settings to study domain reweighting such as data-restricted scenarios and supervised fine-tuning, where optimized mixture ratios significantly improve the performance. Extensive experiments validate TANDEM's effectiveness in all scenarios.

</details>


### [69] [Low-Rank Decay for Grokking in Scale-Invariant Transformers: A Spectral-Geometric View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04405)
*Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出低秩衰减（LRD）——一种核范数类谱正则化方法，通过在尺度不变Transformer层中引入极因子UV^T作为次梯度，在模型过拟合后继续压缩奇异值，从而在模运算任务中加速有效秩坍塌、扩大数据量边界并诱导泛化（grokking）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Frobenius范数权重衰减在RMSNorm/QK归一化等尺度不变层中仅沿径向作用，无法直接简化归一化层所代表的函数。需要一种能在该场景下有效压缩权重谱的正则化方法。

Method: 提出低秩衰减（LRD），其次梯度为极因子UV^T，在尺度不变设置中保留切向分量；通过核范数类谱正则化，以ℓ1方式压缩奇异值。

Result: LRD在模算术任务中诱导Query/Key矩阵的快速有效秩坍塌，扩大延迟泛化（grokking）发生的数据量边界。在任务梯度消失后仍能继续压缩奇异值，而L2衰减则无法做到。

Conclusion: 从谱几何角度通过核范数次微分在低秩层附近的"needle-to-fan"展开提供了理论解释，LRD比L2衰减更适合尺度不变Transformer层的正则化。

Abstract: Modern Transformer architectures frequently employ normalization mechanisms such as RMSNorm and Query-Key Normalization, making parts of the model approximately scale-invariant with respect to weight magnitudes. In this regime, standard Frobenius-norm weight decay acts purely along the radial direction of the weight space and cannot directly simplify the function represented by the normalized layer. We study grokking in small algorithmic tasks through this lens and propose \emph{Low-Rank Decay} (LRD), a nuclear-norm-like spectral regularizer whose subgradient -- the polar factor $UV^\top$ -- retains a tangential component even in the scale-invariant setting. This distinction has a concrete dynamical consequence: after the model memorizes the training set and task gradients vanish, L2 decay can no longer reshape the weight spectrum, whereas LRD continues to compress singular values in an $\ell_1$-like fashion. On modular arithmetic tasks, we find that LRD induces rapid effective-rank collapse in Query/Key matrices and expands the data-fraction boundary at which delayed generalization (grokking) occurs. We further provide a spectral-geometric interpretation through the ``needle-to-fan'' expansion of the nuclear-norm subdifferential near low-rank strata.

</details>


### [70] [An Ensembled Latent Factor Model via Differential Evolution and Gradient Descent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04408)
*Rui Zhang,Jinhang Liu,Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高维不完整(HDI)数据中传统梯度下降优化导致表示偏差的问题，提出ELFM-DEGDO模型，融合差分进化与梯度下降两种优化范式，并通过自适应加权机制结合两个异构潜在因子模型，在三个HDI数据集上表现优于多种对比模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在因子模型主要依赖梯度下降优化，在处理异构HDI数据时容易产生不充分且有偏的表示，需要更鲁棒的优化策略。

Method: ELFM-DEGDO包含两个核心设计：1) 分别通过差分进化和梯度下降优化独立建模两个异构潜在因子模型；2) 通过自适应的自加权机制融合两个模型，综合两种优化范式的互补优势。

Result: 在三个HDI数据集上，ELFM-DEGDO一致性地优于多个相关的潜在因子模型。

Conclusion: 融合差分进化与梯度下降两种优化范式，结合自适应加权机制，能有效提升潜在因子模型对HDI数据的表示质量，获得更全面、更低偏差的表示。

Abstract: High-dimensional and incomplete (HDI) data are prevalent in many real-world big data scenarios. Latent factor models serve as a common representation learning approach, capable of uncovering informative latent factors from such data. Nevertheless, most existing latent factor models rely solely on gradient descent for optimization, which may lead to insufficient and biased representations, particularly when dealing with heterogeneous HDI data. Thus, this study proposes an Ensembled Latent Factor Model via Differential Evolution and Gradient Descent Optimization (ELFM-DEGDO) with two-fold designed: 1) two diverse latent factor models are independently modeled via differential evolution and gradient descent optimization, respectively, and 2) the two diverse latent factor models are combined via a customized self-adaptive weighting mechanism to effectively fuse their strengths. By leveraging the complementary advantages of both optimization paradigms, ELFM-DEGDO is able to produce more comprehensive and less biased representations for HDI data. Three HDI datasets are tested to show that ELFM-DEGDO consistently performs better than related several latent factor models.

</details>


### [71] [(Mis)generalization of Helpful-only Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04413)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Baram Sosis,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了"只帮助不拒绝"模型的局限性，发现其存在突发性错误对齐、残余拒绝行为、低可控性、谄媚和角色不一致等问题。作者指出反拒绝训练是导致这些问题的主要原因，并通过合成文档微调和增加角色相关问题来缓解这些缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的"只帮助不拒绝"模型（帮助性模型）虽然比无害模型拒答率更低，但其在其他对齐维度上的表现尚不清楚。为了在危险能力评估等AI研发领域安全有效地使用这类模型，需要系统性地研究它们的对齐缺陷。

Method: 1）系统评估现有帮助性模型在对齐方面的不足，包括突发性错误对齐、残余拒绝行为、低可控性、谄媚和角色不一致；2）分析反拒绝训练对上述问题的影响贡献；3）提出两种缓解方法：合成文档微调和在SFT与RL阶段增加角色相关问题的训练。

Result: 现有帮助性模型存在多种对齐问题：突发性错误对齐、残余拒绝行为、低可控性、谄媚倾向和角色不一致。反拒绝训练是造成这些问题的关键原因。合成文档微调和增加角色相关问题的训练可以有效缓解这些问题，表明这些缺陷并非帮助性训练的必然结果。

Conclusion: 帮助性模型的缺陷并非不可避免，通过改进训练方法（如合成文档微调和角色相关问题训练）可以在保持帮助性的同时改善对齐质量，这为安全评估和AI研发提供了更好的模型基础。

Abstract: Helpful-only models, that is, models that are trained to always follow user intent, are valuable for dangerous capability evaluations and other areas of AI R&D where refusals would be an obstacle. Little is known about the generalization properties of helpful-only training: helpful-only models refuse less than their harmless counterparts, but previous work has not studied other dimensions of their alignment. We study the shortcomings of existing helpful-only models. We find that some show emergent misalignment, others have residual refusal behaviors, and most show poor steerability, sycophancy, and incoherent character. We show that simple anti-refusal training can cause many of these issues. None of these problems are necessary consequences of helpful-only training, though: we show that synthetic document fine-tuning and adding character-related questions to SFT and RL can mitigate them.

</details>


### [72] [Loss-Conditional PINNs for Parametric PDE Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04420)
*Anna Lazareva,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LC-PINN通过将损失权重或物理系数作为网络输入，在训练过程中探索整个权重空间，实现单模型参数化整个解族，避免传统PINN中权重调优的困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN的性能严重依赖于损失权重的选择，不当的权重会导致退化解（满足一个物理约束而忽略另一个）。现有方法只选择或调整单一权重集，本文提出在训练过程中探索整个权重空间的新视角。

Method: 将损失条件训练扩展到PDE残差设置：将条件向量（损失权重或标量物理系数）作为网络输入，每次优化步骤从简单先验中采样。权重条件下使用拼接，系数条件下使用FiLM模块。采用固定正交L-BFGS完成协议。

Result: 在参数化Helmholtz、Schrödinger、粘性Burgers和Buckley-Leverett方程上，单个LC-PINN匹配或优于逐个重训练的PINN基线，同时以单模型参数化整个族，总成本在摊销方面优于逐个重训练。

Conclusion: LC-PINN介于经典PINN和算子学习之间：完全保持物理信息但将训练摊销到参数化族上。提供了条件最优的λ不变性理论结果。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) approximate solutions of ODEs and PDEs by minimising a weighted combination of residual, boundary, initial, and data losses. Their performance is often dominated by the choice of loss weights: a poor weighting can drive training to a degenerate solution in which one physical constraint is satisfied while another is ignored. Existing methods select or adapt a single good set of weights. We take a different view: instead of tuning one weight vector, we explore the entire weight space during training.
  We introduce LC-PINN, which adapts the loss-conditional training of Dosovitskiy and Djolonga (2020) to the PDE-residual setting: the conditioning vector (either the loss weights or a scalar physical coefficient) is treated as a network input and sampled from a simple prior at every optimisation step. This turns PINN training into learning a continuous family of solutions indexed by that vector, with no solver-generated paired data. LC-PINN thus lies between classical PINNs and operator learning: it stays fully physics-informed but amortises training over a parametric family. Our contribution is not the loss-conditional construction itself, but its extension to PINNs, the unification of the loss-weight and parametric-coefficient regimes under one architecture (concatenation for loss weights, FiLM for coefficients), and a fixed-quadrature L-BFGS finishing protocol that makes the parametric-coefficient regime trainable.
  We give a lambda-invariance result for the conditional optimum and study LC-PINN on parametric Helmholtz, Schrodinger, viscous Burgers, and Buckley-Leverett equations. A single LC-PINN matches or improves retrained per-weight PINN baselines while parameterising the full family in one model, at a total cost that amortises favourably against per-instance retraining.

</details>


### [73] [The price of multi-group transductive learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04423)
*Noah Bergam,Samuel Deng,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在转导设置中，多组学习器相比单组设置会在某些组上承受与组数线性相关的乘法误差惩罚（最多可达样本量的平方根），这与统计设置中惩罚仅与样本量对数相关且与组数无关的结果形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多组学习器在转导设置下相对于单组设置的性能损失，并对比统计设置下的理论保证，揭示两种设置间的本质差异。

Method: 通过理论分析，证明转导设置中多组学习器的误差惩罚与组数呈线性关系，且惩罚上界可达样本量的平方根量级。

Result: 转导设置中，多组学习器在某些组上的误差率相对于单组设置存在乘法惩罚，惩罚随组数线性增长，最高可达样本量的平方根；而统计设置中惩罚至多为样本量的对数且与组数无关。

Conclusion: 转导设置和统计设置下的多组学习在误差惩罚上存在根本性差异，转导设置面临更严重的性能退化问题。

Abstract: We show every multi-group learner in the transductive setting may incur a multiplicative penalty in its error rate on some group relative to the error rate achievable in the single-group setting, and the penalty can increasing linearly with the number of groups, up to roughly the square-root of the sample size. This stands in stark contrast to optimal multi-group learners in an analogous (group-realizable) statistical setting, where the penalty is always at most logarithmic in the sample size and independent of the number of groups.

</details>


### [74] [LoopMoE: Unifying Iterative Computation with Mixture-of-Experts for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04438)
*Wenkai Chen,Tianshu Li,Wenyong Huang,Yichun Yin,Lifeng Shang,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopMoE是一种循环MoE语言模型，通过IterAdaLN调制信号和容量平衡策略，在相同参数量和计算量下，首次实现了循环MoE与标准MoE的严格对比，3B规模下8/9基准超越1+%，9B规模下优势持续保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环架构依赖密集骨干网络，参数量与每token计算量耦合，无法在匹配预算下单独评估迭代计算的效果；缺乏对稀疏路由与循环权重共享的受控合成研究。

Method: 1) IterAdaLN：联合迭代索引和逐token隐状态的条件调制信号，打破权重共享对称性；2) 容量平衡策略：恢复注意力与FFN活跃参数比例，使其匹配非循环参考模型。

Result: 3B规模下，LoopMoE在9个下游基准中8个超越标准MoE，平均提升超1个点；9B规模下持续保持优势。

Conclusion: 首次在相同总参数量、每token计算量和活跃子层比例下，实现了循环MoE与标准MoE的严格头对头对比，表明稀疏性与循环性的受控合成是循环语言模型的一个有前景方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and looped architectures scale models along two orthogonal axes, namely parameter capacity and effective depth. However, mainstream looped architectures rely on dense backbones that couple parameter count with per-token FLOPs, which makes it impossible to isolate the effect of iterative computation under matched budgets. To this end, we present LoopMoE, a looped MoE language model that integrates sparse routing with iterative weight-shared computation through two designs. The first is IterAdaLN, which resolves weight-sharing symmetry via a modulation signal jointly conditioned on the iteration index and the per-token hidden state. The second is a capacity-balancing strategy that recovers the attention-to-FFN active parameter ratio of well-tuned non-looped references. Together, these designs enable the first strictly controlled, head-to-head evaluation of a looped MoE against a Vanilla MoE under identical total parameters, per-token FLOPs, and active sublayer ratios. At the 3B scale, LoopMoE outperforms the Vanilla MoE on 8 of 9 downstream benchmarks with an average improvement exceeding 1 point. At the 9B scale, LoopMoE continues to outperform the matched Vanilla MoE, indicating that the architectural gain persists at larger scale. Our work establishes a controlled synthesis of sparsity and recurrence, and suggests a promising direction for looped language models.

</details>


### [75] [RowNet: A Memory Transformer for Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04445)
*Askat Rakhymbekov,Gulshat Muhametjanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RowNet是一种基于检索的神经网络架构，通过将查询房产与记忆库中的标注房产进行成对相似性比较，结合双层检索机制和多头注意力与混合专家模块，实现房价预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLP将每行视为独立向量，需从监督中学习局部性、尺度敏感性和类别匹配，而梯度提升树虽提供强基线，但其特征中心的分裂机制无法显式建模相似历史观测的检索过程。

Method: RowNet包含三层：第一检索层通过特征相似性估计粗粒度目标；第二层引入目标一致性特征并使用多个学习注意力头检索互补的可比集；最终混合专家模块融合学习门控、残差校正、熵正则化和头部多样性正则化生成预测。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种显式建模可比房产检索逻辑的神经架构，适用于结构化回归问题中的房产估值任务。

Abstract: Real estate valuation is a structured regression problem in which prices are governed by heterogeneous feature types, sparse regional effects, nonlinear interactions, and the practical logic of comparable properties. Standard multilayer perceptrons treat each row as an isolated vector and must learn locality, scale sensitivity, and categorical matching from supervision alone. Gradient-boosted decision trees provide strong tabular baselines, but their feature-centric splitting mechanism does not explicitly model the retrieval of similar historical observations.
  This paper presents RowNet, a retrieval-based neural architecture for real estate price-per-square-meter prediction. RowNet represents a query property through pairwise similarity features against a memory bank of labeled properties. A first retrieval layer estimates a coarse target from feature-only similarities. A second layer augments the memory comparison with target-consistency features and uses multiple learned attention heads to retrieve complementary comparable sets. A final mixture-of-experts module combines learned gating, residual correction, entropy regularization, and head-diversity regularization to produce the prediction.

</details>


### [76] [On Out-of-sample Embedding in UMAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04451)
*Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Jason W. Fleischer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现UMAP在添加新样本时存在“排斥效应”（将新点置于聚类外围而非内部），通过优化k近邻图内的成对交互克服该问题，并证明参数化UMAP在复杂数据上表现优于非参数版本，且能自然缓解排斥效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMAP在处理out-of-sample（新样本）嵌入时存在缺陷，新点被错误地放置在聚类外围而不是与其相关邻居的内部。作者旨在解决这一“排斥效应”。

Method: 1) 优化原始k近邻图内的成对交互以克服排斥效应；2) 采用参数化UMAP（parametric UMAP）进行嵌入；3) 使用可信度、最近邻分类器以及吸引力和排斥力分析来评估不同UMAP方法的效果。

Result: 参数化UMAP相比非参数算法获得更好的嵌入，尤其在复杂数据（如医学图像）上表现更优；排斥效应在参数化UMAP中自然得到缓解。

Conclusion: 通过优化k近邻图内交互及采用参数化方法，可有效解决UMAP的排斥效应，参数化UMAP在复杂数据嵌入中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Neighbor embedding algorithms reveal correlations in high-dimensional data by constructing an equivalent graph representation in a lower-dimensional space. An increasingly popular algorithm is Uniform Manifold Learning and Projection (UMAP), which uses algebraic topology to map distances between the two spaces. While it works well on many types of data sets, UMAP has trouble adding out-of-sample points to a pre-existing mapping. In particular, UMAP often places new points on the periphery of the found clusters, rather than in their interiors with their correlated neighbors. Here, we overcome this ``repulsion effect'' by optimizing pairwise interactions within the original k-nearest-neighbor graph. Moreover, we show that parameterizing UMAP obtains better embeddings than non-parametric algorithms, particularly as the data gets more complex (e.g., medical images). We also show that the repulsion effect is naturally mitigated when a parameterized UMAP is employed to embed the data. We characterize different UMAP approaches using trustworthiness, nearest neighbor classifiers, and by analyzing attractive and repulsive forces in the embeddings.

</details>


### [77] [ParetoPilot: Zero-Surrogate Offline Multi-Objective Optimization via Infer-Perturb-Guide Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04468)
*Ruiqing Sun,Sen Yang,Dawei Feng,Bo Ding,Yijie Wang,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParetoPilot提出了一种零代理扩散框架用于离线多目标优化，通过Infer-Perturb-Guide引擎在无条件去噪过程中隐式推断目标方向并生成扰动向量，无需外部代理模型，在51个任务上超越14个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线多目标优化生成方法依赖外部代理模型，导致计算开销大、评估欺骗性问题，且偏离了主流生成模型与条件联合训练的范式。

Method: 提出ParetoPilot零代理扩散框架，核心是Infer-Perturb-Guide (IPG)引擎：1) Infer：通过匹配条件与无条件噪声预测隐式推断目标方向；2) Perturb：正交化平行引力场（保证收敛）与边缘感知斥力（保持多样性），生成动态退火扰动向量；3) Guide：通过标准无分类器引导(CFG)控制生成过程。

Result: 在51个任务上的实验表明，ParetoPilot在超体积指标和Pareto前沿覆盖方面优于14个基于代理和逆生成的最新基线方法。

Conclusion: ParetoPilot通过消除辅助代理训练，在保护数据隐私的同时实现了性能提升，为离线多目标优化提供了一种无需代理模型的高效新范式。

Abstract: Offline multi-objective optimization (Offline MOO) aims to discover novel Pareto-optimal designs based on static datasets without expensive environment interactions. While recent generative methods have achieved notable success, they predominantly rely on external surrogate models. This dependency introduces significant computational overhead, suffers from deceptive evaluations, and deviates from the prevailing paradigm of jointly training mainstream generative models with conditions. To address these bottlenecks, we propose ParetoPilot, a novel zero-surrogate diffusion framework for offline MOO. ParetoPilot fully leverages the conditional priors inherently embedded within pre-trained diffusion models. At its core, the framework introduces the Infer-Perturb-Guide (IPG) engine, which is seamlessly interleaved within the unconditional denoising steps of the reverse generation process. First, it implicitly infers the instantaneous objective direction by matching conditional and unconditional noise predictions. Next, it mathematically orthogonalizes a parallel gravity field for strict convergence and an edgeness-aware repulsive force for mutual diversity, creating a dynamically annealed perturbation vector. Finally, this perturbed target seamlessly steers the generation process via standard Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG).
