We found services belonging to several communities, as well as services within the same community with both incoming and outgoing connections. The membership strength metric provided by the leveraged algorithm enables fine-grained assessment of the microservice communities.

</details>


### [151] [Proof-Carrying Agent Actions: Model-Agnostic Runtime Governance for Heterogeneous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04104)
*Zexun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运行时中立的治理模型PCAA（Proof-Carrying Agent Actions），通过动作证书而非厂商原生的会话记录来统一管理智能体系统中的高风险操作。PCAA围绕五个检查点组织控制流程，并支持可移植的证明结构。实验表明，该模型在异构运行时环境下能够保持路由质量并暴露不同故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统通过不同的运行时（如本地编码工具、框架SDK、托管平台、API网关等）执行操作，同一高风险动作在不同运行时中表现为不同形式（如shell命令、工具调用、会话转换），导致无法一致地回答基本的治理问题：什么动作被授权、由谁授权、采用何种批准语义、执行后有何证据。亟需一种运行时中立的治理模型来解决这一不一致性问题。

Method: PCAA模型围绕五个检查点组织控制：动作前可接纳性检查、动作开启、假设捕获、批准、以及结果关闭。将这些检查点绑定到可移植的动作信封、运行时和批准收据、以及可重放证明上。证书具有外部性感知能力（携带目标可见性、账户来源等边界事实），批准由明确的可执行性类别描述而非单一的已审查/未审查位。通过异构智能体控制平面的参考实现和披露受限的评估协议进行研究。

Result: 在从24个可执行种子扩展到96个追踪轨迹（覆盖四个运行时家族）的保护基准测试上，PCAA保持了路由质量，同时在消融实验中暴露了不同的故障模式。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了围绕证书承载动作的运行时治理的系统化表述，以及一个基于实现的说明：该表述如何在运行时变更下保持可移植性，而不退化为厂商特定的控制面。

Abstract: Agent systems execute through runtimes with very different control points: local coding tools, framework SDKs, managed agent platforms, API gateways, and observer-only integrations. A high-risk action such as publishing data externally may therefore appear as a shell command in one runtime, a tool call in another, and a hosted session transition in a third. This makes it difficult to answer a basic governance question consistently: what action was authorized, under whose authority, with what approval semantics, and with what evidence after execution?
  This paper presents Proof-Carrying Agent Actions (PCAA), a runtime-neutral governance model centered on an action certificate rather than on a vendor-native session record. PCAA organizes control around five checkpoints: pre-action admissibility, action open, assumption capture, approval, and outcome closure. It binds these checkpoints to a portable action envelope, runtime and approval receipts, and replay-ready proof. The model is extended in two practical ways: the certificate is externality-aware, carrying boundary facts such as destination visibility and account provenance, and approval is described by explicit enforceability classes rather than by a single reviewed or unreviewed bit.
  We study the model through a reference implementation in a heterogeneous agent control plane and a disclosure-bounded evaluation protocol. On a protected benchmark expanded from 24 executable seeds to 96 traces across four runtime families, PCAA preserves route quality while exposing distinct failure modes under ablation. The paper contributes a systems formulation of runtime governance around certificate-bearing actions and an implementation-grounded account of how that formulation can remain portable under runtime churn without collapsing into vendor-specific control surfaces.

</details>


### [152] [Extraction and Search in Rocq: Theorems, Definitions and Their dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04704)
*Jian Fang,Yingfei Xiong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为TheoremExtr的Rocq定理提取与分析工具，能够从Rocq项目中提取定理、依赖关系和定义，支持跨项目相似度搜索，并从32个开源项目中提取了71,795个定理和27,481个定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Rocq搜索命令局限于已导入模块环境，无法搜索外部定理；同时缺乏高效易用的工具来提取项目的完整定理信息（如名称、陈述、依赖关系），给用户和工具开发者带来不便。

Method: TheoremExtr工具通过解析阶段和运行时阶段分析定理组成，提取定理、依赖关系和定义及其类型，并构建跨项目相似度搜索网站。

Result: 从32个开源Rocq社区项目中提取了71,795个定理及其依赖关系，以及27,481个定义及其类型，并开发了支持跨项目相似度搜索的网站。

Conclusion: TheoremExtr有效解决了Rocq定理搜索范围受限和信息提取困难的问题，为定理证明提供了更便捷的跨项目搜索能力。

Abstract: Rocq (Coq) are now widely used in various fields, including software verification and mathematical proofs. When proving a new theorem, users often need to search and apply proven theorems to assist the current proof process. However, the current search command is limited to the environment of imported modules and cannot search for theorems outside of this scope. Furthermore, tool developers and researchers may want to obtain detailed information about theorems, such as theorem's names, statements, and dependencies. But there are currently no user-friendly and efficient tools available for extracting comprehensive information from Rocq projects. We introduce a Rocq theorem extraction and analysis tool, TheoremExtr, which is capable of analyzing theorem composition and extracting theorems, dependencies, and definitions from both parsing phase and runtime. We extracted 71,795 theorems and their dependencies from 32 open-source projects from the Rocq community. In addition, we extracted 27,481 definitions and their types among these projects. We also developed a website that supports cross-project similarity search for theorems and definitions. The tool is available at https://github.com/Rw1nd/TheoremExtr, and the search website is available at https://lemmasearch.com.

</details>


### [153] [Revisiting Vul-RAG: Reproducibility and Replicability of RAG-based Vulnerability Detection with Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04739)
*Sabrina Kaniewski,Fabian Schmidt,Tobias Heer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对基于RAG的漏洞检测框架Vul-RAG进行了可重复性研究，在本地开源环境中复现了其结果，并通过多种开源LLM扩展评估。研究发现Vul-RAG结果可复现但存在微小偏差，所有模型在成对准确率上存在约0.30的性能瓶颈，表明单纯提升模型能力无法显著改善检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的软件漏洞检测方法（尤其是RAG方法）多依赖专有模型和API，其可重复性和可复现性未得到充分探索，难以确定报告的结果是否具有泛化性还是主要依赖于特定的模型选择。

Method: 首先在完全本地和开源权重设置下使用报告中的开源基线模型复现Vul-RAG结果；然后将评估扩展到多种最新的开源LLM，包括代码专用、通用和推理模型，涵盖不同参数规模。

Result: Vul-RAG的结果在本地部署下是可复现的，但存在微小偏差。所有评估模型在成对准确率上均存在约0.30的性能瓶颈，即使更先进的新模型也未显著突破这一水平。

Conclusion: Vul-RAG发现可复现但存在性能天花板，仅凭提升模型容量无法大幅提升漏洞检测效果，需要从检测有效性、模型能力和模型规模之间综合考虑实际权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for automated software vulnerability detection, particularly in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) settings. However, for approaches relying on proprietary models and APIs, reproducibility and replicability remain largely unexplored, raising the question of whether reported results generalize or depend primarily on specific model choices. In this work, we present a reproducibility study of Vul-RAG, a RAG-based framework for source code vulnerability detection that enhances LLMs with high-level vulnerability knowledge. We first replicate the results in a fully local and open-weights setting using the reported open-weight baseline models. We then extend the evaluation to a diverse set of recent open-weight LLMs, including code-specialized, general-purpose, and reasoning models of varying parameter sizes. The results confirm that the findings of Vul-RAG are reproducible under local deployment, but with minor deviations. Across all evaluated models, we observe a performance plateau at approximately 0.30 pairwise accuracy (code pairs for which both the vulnerable and the patched function are correctly classified). Notably, this plateau persists even for more recent and advanced models, indicating that improvements in model capacity alone do not substantially enhance performance. Finally, we discuss practical implications and trade-offs between detection effectiveness, model capabilities, and model scale. Implementation and evaluation artifacts are publicly available at https://github.com/hs-esslingen-it-security/revisiting-Vul-RAG.

</details>


### [154] [UModel: An Agent-Ready Observability Data Modeling Method at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04799)
*Changhua Pei,Zheyuan Li,Zexin Wang,Hang Cui,Xiaohui Nie,Qi Zhou,Fang Situ,Cheng Zhang,Xin Zhang,Xidao Wen,Gaogang Xie,Jingjing Li,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出UModel，一个统一的本体框架，将可观测性从以数据为中心转向以对象为中心，通过语义图标准化异构遥测数据、实体和专家知识，并引入U-SPL查询接口使LLM代理能够自主探索系统拓扑和关联多模态数据。在AIOps 2025 Challenge数据集上，根因定位精度提升8%，并在阿里云部署一年，服务数万用户，支撑每秒百万级操作，亚秒级查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可观测性框架存在数据孤岛、模式不兼容、语义元数据不足等问题，导致基于LLM的代理无法建立根因分析所需的复杂关系，阻碍了自动化故障诊断的有效性。

Method: 提出UModel统一本体框架，构建虚拟本体层将异构遥测数据、实体和专家知识标准化为对象，通过语义图互连；同时引入U-SPL基于管线的查询接口，使代理能自主探索系统拓扑并关联多模态数据。

Result: 在AIOps 2025 Challenge数据集上，根因定位精度提升8%；在阿里云部署超一年，服务数万用户，支撑每秒百万级操作，亚秒级查询延迟。

Conclusion: 增强的数据组织方式能显著提升下游任务（如根因分析）的准确性，UModel提供了可扩展的建模框架，在实际大规模生产环境中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: When networked system failures occur, automatically performing Root Cause Analysis (RCA) using observability data is critical for ensuring networked system reliability. Recently, LLM-based agents have shown promise for automating this diagnosis process through advanced reasoning and autonomous exploration. However, existing observability frameworks remain archaic, characterized by fragmented data silos, incompatible schemas, and insufficient semantic metadata, preventing agents from establishing the complex relationships required for effective RCA. To address these challenges, we present UModel, a unified ontological framework that shifts observability from data-centric to object-centric modeling. UModel constructs a virtual ontological layer where heterogeneous telemetry, entities, and expert knowledge are standardized as objects and interconnected via semantic graphs. In addition, we introduce U-SPL, a pipeline-based query interface that enables agents to autonomously explore system topologies and correlate multimodal data. By re-modeling the "AIOps 2025 Challenge" dataset using UModel, the precision of root cause localization improved by 8%, demonstrating that enhanced data organization can significantly increase the accuracy of downstream tasks. UModel provides a scalable modeling framework that, in its deployment at Alibaba Cloud for more than one year, has served tens of thousands of users, sustained millions of operations per second, and delivered sub-second query latency.

</details>


### [155] [TeleSWEBench: A Commit-Driven Benchmark for Evaluating LLM-Powered Software Engineering in Telecommunications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05001)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Ali Mamaghani,Dinesh Bharadia,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeleSWEBench，首个面向电信领域的commit驱动基准测试，用于评估ASE工具和SWE Agent在5G无线协议栈（srsRAN）上的代码修改能力。该基准包含734个带可执行单元测试的问题，并设计了TeleJudge分层LLM评估框架。实验表明，最强ASE工具仅能达到25%的可交付修改率，模型在定位准确性和功能正确性上仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用编程基准无法捕捉电信领域特有的状态逻辑、数学严苛性和严格需求，导致ASE工具在该关键领域的评估存在空白。同时，O-RAN和AI-RAN等新兴框架使得电信网络代码库高度复杂化，亟需验证自动化工具能否处理专业化无线协议栈。

Method: 从srsRAN 5G仓库挖掘真实开发者commit，构建734个涵盖Easy/Medium/Difficult三档难度的测试问题，并配以可执行单元测试；提出TeleJudge分层LLM评估框架，结合上下文语义相似度评估与标准单元测试评估。

Result: 评估了AIDER、OpenHands和ClaudeCode框架，搭配Qwen3、GPT OSS、Gemma 4、Kimi和Qwencoder 2.5等推理LLM。两阶段评估显示最强ASE工具仅达成25%的可交付修改率，模型在代码定位准确性和功能正确性上均存在明显不足。

Conclusion: TeleSWEBench有效填补了电信领域ASE评估空白；当前最先进工具在5G协议栈上的表现远未达到生产可用标准，提示该领域仍需进一步研究以提升自动化代码修改能力。

Abstract: With the telecommunications field embracing zero touch management alongside novel O-RAN and AI-RAN frameworks, contemporary telecom networks now function as immensely intricate and heavily softwareized codebases. While automated software engineering (ASE) tools and Software Engineering (SWE) Agents hold the potential to alleviate the critical code generation bottleneck in this domain, their ability to navigate and modify specialized, mathematically rigorous wireless stacks like srsRAN 5G remains unverified. General-purpose coding benchmarks fail to capture the stateful logic and strict requirements of telecommunications, leaving a critical evaluation gap. In this paper, we introduce TeleSWEBench, the first commit-driven benchmark specifically designed to measure an agent's performance in the telecom domain. We mine real developer commits from the srsRAN 5G repository and distill them into structured test cases across three difficulty tiers (Easy, Medium, and Difficult). Our benchmark consists of 734 questions that are accompanied by executable unit tests. To avoid the rigidity of test cases, we further propose a hierarchical LLM as a Judge framework called TeleJudge that scores agent outputs at the file level and aggregates verdicts holistically. This follows an evaluation based on context and semantic similarity in parallel to a standard unit test-based evaluation. Using this benchmark, we evaluate AIDER, OpenHands, and the ClaudeCode frameworks, powered by state-of-the-art reasoning LLMs, including Qwen3, GPT OSS, Gemma 4, Kimi, and Qwencoder 2.5. Our two-stage evaluation reveals that models suffer from a lack of both localization accuracy and functional correctness, with the strongest ASE tools achieving up to 25% of shippable changes.

</details>


### [156] [Self-Reflective APIs: Structure Beats Verbosity for AI Agent Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05037)
*Arquimedes Canedo,Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种自反射API机制，在验证失败时返回机器可读的恢复建议（recovery_feedback.suggestions[]），帮助AI代理无需外部推理即可自动修复请求。在泄露审计后的实验中，结构化建议使Anthropic模型的任务完成率提升36.7-40.0个百分点，token效率提升1.8-2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理调用API遇到验证错误时，仅知道"哪里错了"不够，还需要知道"下一步怎么做"。现有方法要么依赖外部推理，要么仅提供纯文本诊断，效率低下。

Method: 设计自反射API，在验证失败时返回包含机器可读的recovery_feedback.suggestions[]负载，使代理能直接修复请求并重试。采用泄露审计后的基准测试（N=30/组，3种LLM，10个对抗性任务），并开发了audit_prompt_leakage.py作为可复用的CI基础设施。

Result: 在Anthropic模型上，结构化建议相比纯文本诊断提升任务完成率36.7-40.0个百分点（Fisher精确检验p≤0.0022），每个成功任务的token效率提升1.8-2.2倍。在gpt-4o-mini上提升不显著（p=0.435）。在计费API的二次领域复制实验证实了该模式。

Conclusion: 自反射API的结构化恢复建议能显著提升AI代理的API调用成功率，尤其在Anthropic模型上效果显著。研究还揭示并审计了LLM基准测试中两类未记录的答案泄露问题，相关审计工具已开源。

Abstract: When an AI agent calls an API and hits a validation error, it needs more than what went wrong -- it needs what to do next. A self-reflective API returns, on validation failure, a machine-readable recovery\_feedback.suggestions[] payload sufficient for the agent to repair the request and retry without external reasoning. On a leak-audited pilot ($N{=}30$ per cell, 3 LLMs, 10 adversarial tasks), structured suggestions lift task-completion rate by $+36.7$--$40.0$pp over plain-English diagnoses on Anthropic models (Fisher's exact $p \le 0.0022$), at $1.8$--$2.2\times$ better per-success token efficiency. The lift is not significant on gpt-4o-mini ($p{=}0.435$); a second-domain replication on a billing API confirms the pattern. The comparison only holds after auditing two undocumented classes of answer leakage in LLM benchmarks. We shipaudit\_prompt\_leakage.py as reusable CI infrastructure. Code and data: https://github.com/arquicanedo/self-reflective-apis.

</details>


### [157] [How Software Engineering Students Use LLMs to Write Research Papers: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05114)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Maria Teresa Baldassarre,Cleyton Magalhaes,Italo Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在软件工程教育中将反思性LLM使用整合到实证方法作业中的教育经验，通过分析146份学生披露声明，揭示了学生在头脑风暴、方法澄清、结果组织和写作润色中使用LLM的方式及其对内容准确性的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型日益融入软件工程教育，特别是在涉及实证软件工程和证据综合的活动中，有必要了解学生如何实际使用LLM并反思其影响。

Method: 作者在三年级软件架构课程中设计了一个实证方法作业，要求学生使用快速综述或灰色文献综述方法论撰写简短研究论文，并披露LLM使用情况。随后采用LLM辅助分类与人工验证相结合的交叉分析流程，分析了146份学生披露声明。

Result: 学生的反思描述了他们在头脑风暴、方法澄清、结果组织和写作润色等活动中使用LLM的方式，同时也报告了对生成内容不准确性和验证问题的担忧。

Conclusion: 本文讨论了将AI辅助技术整合到实证软件工程教育中的经验教训和教育启示。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly becoming part of software engineering education, including activities involving empirical software engineering and evidence synthesis. This paper reports an educational experience involving the integration of reflective LLM use into an empirical methods assignment in a third-year software architecture course. Students were asked to develop a short research paper using either a rapid review or a gray literature review methodology and to disclose how LLMs were used throughout the assignment. We analyzed 146 student disclosure statements using a cross-analysis process combining LLM-assisted categorization with manual verification and refinement by the researchers. The reflections describe how students incorporated LLMs during activities such as brainstorming, methodological clarification, organization of findings, and writing refinement, while also reporting concerns regarding inaccuracies and verification of generated content. This experience report discusses lessons learned and educational implications for integrating AI-assisted technologies into empirical software engineering education.

</details>


### [158] [The Invisible Lottery: How Subtle Cues Steer Algorithm Choice in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04057)
*Akanksha Narula,Mofasshara Binte Rafique,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过46,535次对照实验，系统研究了LLM在代码生成中受偶然提示线索影响而改变算法选择的现象，发现线索语义可导致算法族分布发生高达100个百分点的系统性偏移，并提出直接命名算法是最可靠的缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管提示敏感性已被广泛研究以提升输出质量，但偶然提示线索（任务规格之外的语境词或元数据）会无意中影响模型选择哪个算法，即使所有输出都能通过相同的测试。这种"算法引导"现象对代码的性能、安全性和可维护性有重要影响，但此前缺乏系统性的量化研究。

Method: 在11个任务、19种线索类型（18个通道加上一个保留语义但改变排版和标点的记忆化语义vs表面消融实验）、15个模型配置下进行46,535次受控实验，通过定义算法引导为线索引起的算法族分布偏移，量化分析偶然线索对LLM算法选择的影响。

Result: 发现算法族分布存在高达100个百分点的大规模系统性偏移，且与线索语义高度一致；该现象在限流等实际应用任务中同样存在；直接命名算法是最可靠的缓解措施。

Conclusion: 偶然上下文在性能、安全性和可维护性方面创造了一种"隐形抽奖"效应，即开发者在不经意间可能得到截然不同特性的算法实现，呼吁对LLM代码生成中的偶然引导给予更多关注。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now generate substantial production code, often for tasks with multiple valid algorithmic solutions. Incidental prompt cues, meaning contextual words or metadata outside the task specification, can steer which algorithm the model selects, even when all outputs pass the same tests. Prompt sensitivity is well studied as a tool to improve output quality. Here, output policy means algorithm choice under fixed correctness. We define algorithm steering as cue-induced shifts in algorithm-family distributions and run 46,535 controlled experiments across 11 tasks, 19 cue types (18 channels plus a memoization semantic-vs-surface ablation that preserves meaning while changing typography and punctuation), and 15 model configurations. We find large, systematic shifts in algorithm-family distributions (up to 100 pp), largely consistent with cue semantics, including in applied tasks such as rate limiting. Direct algorithm naming is the most reliable mitigation we tested. Accidental context therefore creates an "invisible lottery" over performance, security, and maintainability.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [CADET: A Modular Platform for Evaluating Distributed Cooperative Autonomy in Connected Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04072)
*Pragya Sharma,Brian Wang,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CADET是一个用于分布式协同自动驾驶系统评估的开源模块化平台，支持将感知、规划、控制模块灵活部署在车辆、路侧单元、边缘/云端，通过V2V/V2I实验揭示了分布式部署选择对安全性的根本影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动驾驶采用单一车载计算机的 monolithic 设计，忽视了通过V2X连接的协同自治范式（车辆与RSU、边缘服务器、云端智能协作）。协同感知与控制虽能提升安全性和效率，但引入了网络延迟、计算异构性、多租户争用等系统级挑战，且大模型规模要求云端部署。

Method: CADET将AV栈解耦为可组合模块，支持灵活部署在车辆、基础设施、边缘/云三层；集成SOTA模型，引入基于trace的网络和工作负载仿真，提供模型级、系统级、任务级同步检测；支持V2V和V2I实验。

Result: V2V意图包优于云端感知；RSU辅助感知在过载前可维持安全；分布式部署选择从根本上影响安全性。

Conclusion: CADET为系统和ML研究人员提供了独立于完整车辆仿真的分布式推理工作负载基准测试能力，是开源平台。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly central to autonomous vehicle (AV) pipelines, yet their integration has traditionally followed a monolithic design where perception, planning, and control execute on a single onboard computer. This design overlooks the emerging paradigm of cooperative autonomy, where vehicles interact with roadside units (RSUs), edge servers, and cloud-hosted intelligence through vehicle-to-everything (V2X) connectivity. Cooperative perception and control improve safety and efficiency, but also introduce systems-level challenges: network latency, compute heterogeneity, and multi-tenant contention, all critically affect real-time decision-making. These challenges are further amplified by the increasing reliance on large foundation models, whose scale necessitates cloud deployment.
  We present CADET (Cooperative Autonomy through Distributed Experimentation Toolkit), a modular platform for systematic and reproducible evaluation of distributed cooperative autonomy systems under realistic deployment conditions. CADET decouples the AV stack into composable modules that can be flexibly deployed across vehicles, infrastructure, and edge/cloud tiers. The framework integrates state-of-the-art models, incorporates trace-driven network and workload emulation, and provides synchronized model-, system-, and task-level instrumentation. Through V2V and V2I experiments, we show that distributed deployment choices fundamentally shape safety, with V2V intent packets outperforming cloud-based perception and RSU-assisted perception sustaining safety until overloaded by concurrent requests. Although designed for AV pipelines, CADET also supports dataset-driven experimentation, enabling systems and ML researchers to benchmark distributed inference workloads independently of full vehicle simulation. CADET is open source, with code and demo available at https://nesl.github.io/cadet-web.

</details>


### [160] [AgenticDiffusion: Agentic Diffusion-based Path Planning for Vision-Based UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04111)
*Faryal Batool,Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Fawad Mehboob,Valerii Serpiva,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AgenticDiffusion框架，结合多视图观测（FPV+俯视图）、语言引导推理、开放词汇目标定位、扩散规划器和NMPC，实现室内UAV高效导航，在40次真实世界试验中达到80%任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的导航框架通常依赖单视图观测，难以处理遮挡、目标可见性和全局场景结构推理，限制了室内UAV导航效率。

Method: 构建统一框架，融合自然语言指令与同步FPV/俯视图观测，通过开放词汇定位目标，利用扩散规划器生成视角特定导航轨迹，结合NMPC执行，支持多阶段任务和长时域导航。

Result: 在40次真实世界试验中总体任务成功率达80%，扩散规划器轨迹生成成功率100%，在自适应视角选择、多阶段任务、长时域导航和安全着陆点选择等场景中得到验证。

Conclusion: 多视图协同与扩散规划相结合能有效减少重复目标探索，提升复杂室内环境下的UAV导航效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Indoor UAV navigation requires efficient exploration, scene understanding, and reliable trajectory execution under limited field-of-view observations. Existing vision-based navigation frameworks typically rely on single-view observations, limiting their ability to reason about occlusions, target visibility, and global scene structure. In this work, we propose AgenticDiffusion, a multi-view UAV navigation framework that coordinates language-guided reasoning, open-vocabulary target grounding, vision-based diffusion planning, and NMPC within a unified aerial navigation pipeline. Given a natural language instruction and synchronized first-person-view (FPV) and top-view observations, the framework determines the most informative viewpoint for navigation and generates a mission plan prior to trajectory execution. The targets are localized using an open-vocabulary grounding model, after which viewpoint-specific diffusion planners generate navigation trajectories for UAV execution. Using complementary viewpoints, the proposed framework reduces repeated target exploration and improves navigation efficiency in cluttered indoor environments. The framework was validated in four real-world UAV navigation scenarios involving adaptive viewpoint selection, multi-stage mission execution, long-horizon navigation, and safe landing-site selection. The experimental results demonstrated an overall mission success rate of 80% in 40 real-world trials, while the diffusion planners achieved a trajectory generation success rate of 100%.

</details>


### [161] [CLAW: Learning Continuous Latent Action World Models via Adversarial Latent Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04130)
*Tewodros Ayalew,Matthew Jeung,Samuel Wheeler,Xiao Zhang,Andre de la Cruz Arce,Kaylene Stocking,Michael Maire,Matthew R. Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAW是一个完全端到端的自监督框架，无需动作标签即可从无动作视频中联合学习世界模型和连续潜在动作表示，支持模仿学习和目标导向规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要大量带动作标签的数据来训练世界模型和动作表示，而获取这类标注成本高昂且难以扩展。本文旨在开发一种无需任何动作标注的自监督方法，从纯视觉观测中学习有意义的动作表示和环境动态。

Method: 提出CLAW框架，通过对抗性潜在正则化和基于扩散的视频生成，联合训练潜在动作模型和世界模型。潜在动作模型从无动作视频中推断出连续的潜在动作表示，世界模型则利用这些表示建模环境动态和预测未来状态。

Result: 在多种任务和实体上的实验表明，CLAW能学习到语义上有意义的潜在动作表示，支持有效的动作迁移，并在从观测中模仿学习和目标导向规划任务中优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CLAW证明了在不依赖任何动作标签的情况下，通过自监督方式学习有意义的动作表示和世界模型是可行的，且学到的表示可用于下游的模仿学习和规划任务，为机器人学习和智能体研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce CLAW, a fully end-to-end self-supervised framework for learning a world model jointly with continuous latent action representations directly from action-free videos. Our approach leverages adversarial latent regularization and diffusion-based video generation to capture structured and semantically meaningful action representations while modeling rich, predictive environment dynamics, without relying on any action labels or annotations. By simultaneously training the Latent Action Model and world model, CLAW learns to reason about how inferred actions induce environment transitions from visual observations alone. We show that the resulting latent action world model supports both imitation learning from observation and goal-directed planning. In imitation learning, latent actions extracted from raw videos enable behavior cloning. For planning, CLAW generates sequences of latent actions and maps them to executable actions to reach desired goals. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks and embodiments demonstrate that CLAW produces semantically meaningful latent action representations, supports effective action transfer, and enables planning and imitation from observation, outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [162] [CoPark: Learning Reactive Parking via Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04149)
*Jiarong Wei,Yanxing Chen,Sinuo Song,Yin Wu,Anna Rehr,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoPark，一种基于残差策略架构的多智能体自博弈强化学习方法，通过“伙伴威胁调制的通道非对称先验释放”机制，在自动驾驶泊车场景中同时实现高精度几何可达与安全交互，在六个停车场训练并在新基准上取得70-85%成功率与3-6%碰撞率，远优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度几何规划与安全交互在拥挤环境中存在根本冲突：精度要求坚持固定几何路径，而交互要求即时避让偏离路径，导致针对单一目标优化的策略在另一目标上表现失败。

Method: 基于残差策略（residual-policy）架构：预计算的离线规划提供固定动作先验，残差头学习反应式修正。核心设计是“伙伴威胁调制的通道非对称先验释放”——连续威胁信号将纵向通道控制权转移给残差头以允许让行，而横向通道锚定预计算参考以保持亚米级车位对齐。另含闭环精化层修正动作网格离散化带来的残余终端误差。

Result: 在六个停车场训练，零样本评估于新基准（DLP和DSC3D）。CoPark实现约70-85%成功率，仅3-6%碰撞率，大幅优于经典方法、模仿学习和大规模RL基线。且涌现出倒车让行、中途让行、狭窄通道通行和排队等交互行为。

Conclusion: 通过通道非对称的先验释放机制，残差架构能够同时兼顾规划精度与交互安全性，并在零样本泛化中展现出超越训练数据覆盖范围的复杂多智能体协调行为。

Abstract: Learning a single policy that reaches a goal with high geometric precision while interacting safely with nearby agents poses conflicting objectives. Precision favors commitment to a fixed geometric plan, whereas interaction requires immediate deviation when another agent intrudes, causing policies optimized for one objective to often fail at the other. We study this problem in the context of reactive autonomous parking, where multiple vehicles must reach assigned slots with sub-meter terminal accuracy while remaining responsive to neighboring vehicles throughout the maneuver. We propose CoPark, a multi-agent self-play RL approach built on a residual-policy architecture. A precomputed offline plan provides a fixed action prior, while a residual head learns the reactive corrections. The residual policy learns behaviors under self-play, where data and scripting fall short, while the fixed prior holds the slot-frame geometry that pure policies struggle to reach reliably. The key design is a partner-threat-modulated, channel-asymmetric release of the prior. A continuous threat signal shifts authority of the longitudinal channel to the residual head to enable yielding, while the lateral channel remains anchored to the precomputed reference to preserve sub-meter slot alignment. A closed-loop refinement layer corrects residual terminal error from action-grid discretization. We train our policy on six parking lots and evaluate zero-shot on our new reactive-parking benchmark spanning Dragon Lake Parking (DLP) and DeepScenario Open 3D (DSC3D). CoPark achieves ~70-85% success with only 3-6% collision rate, substantially outperforming classical, imitation-learning, and large-scale RL baselines. Importantly, the results demonstrate emergent interaction behaviors such as reverse-yielding, mid-maneuver yielding, tight-corridor passing, and queuing.

</details>


### [163] [Selecting haptic guidance models in teleoperation: guidelines from a comparative user study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04157)
*Alexis Boulay,Margot Vulliez,David Daney*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了触觉引导遥操作中三种经典模型（弹簧-阻尼、势场、引导管）的统一公式化表达，通过用户实验比较了六种场景下的性能，发现无普适最优模型，并提供了基于环境和实时指标的选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉引导在遥操作中至关重要，但缺乏针对任务、环境和操作者特性的模型选择指南，现有模型（弹簧-阻尼、势场、引导管）各有优劣却无系统性比较与选择依据。

Method: 1) 定义统一公式化表达，将三种模型视为刚度-阻尼系统的变体；2) 进行用户实验，在垂直农场任务中设置六种不同环境条件场景；3) 提出新的客观交互评估指标（如引导力大小与舒适度/信任度的相关性分析）。

Result: 无普适最优模型：弹簧-阻尼模型在杂乱环境中表现最优，势场模型在自由空间中出色但靠近障碍物有风险，引导管模型提供平衡折衷；引导力大小与操作者舒适度及信任度评分相关。

Conclusion: 提供了基于环境特征和实时评估指标的实用模型选择指南，帮助根据具体任务条件选出最合适的触觉引导模型。

Abstract: Haptic guidance in teleoperation enhances operator performance through force feedback. This paper presents guidelines to select the most appropriate model considering the task, the environment and the operator. We define a unified formulation expressing most common models (spring-damper, potential field, and guiding tube) as variations of a stiffness-damping system with model-specific guiding functions. We conducted a user study comparing the three classical models across six scenarios with varying environmental conditions in a vertical farming task. Results show no universally superior model: spring-damper excels in cluttered environments, potential field in free spaces (but it shows risks near obstacles), and guiding tube offers a balanced compromise. We propose novel objective metrics to evaluate the interaction, and show that guiding force magnitude correlates with comfort and trust scores. These findings provide practical model selection guidelines through environmental characteristics and real-time evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [164] [Multi-Agent Next-Best-View Optimization for Risk-Averse Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04158)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Vivek Pandey,Guangyi Liu,Athanasios Cosse,Emrah Bayrak,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布式、风险感知的多智能体Next-Best-View框架，每个机器人维护私有局部3D高斯泼溅地图，通过Consensus ADMM优化分布式目标函数，仅交换候选视点、轨迹描述符和标量EIG贡献，在Gibson环境实验中以数量级更低的通信开销实现了接近集中式基线的地图质量和轨迹安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式多智能体NBV方法依赖共享原始传感器数据或大量通信开销，可扩展性有限，亟需一种通信高效、可扩展的分布式替代方案。

Method: 每个机器人维护私有局部3DGS地图；通过C-ADMM在通信图上优化分布式EIG目标，仅交换候选视点、轨迹描述符和标量EIG贡献；使用AV@R建模碰撞风险，用于掩码半径和路径评分。

Result: 在Gibson环境多团队规模实验中，分布式方法在制图质量和轨迹安全性上接近集中式基线，同时将通信开销降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式风险感知NBV框架能在显著减少通信开销的前提下，实现接近集中式方法的地图构建质量与路径安全性，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-agent Next-Best-View (NBV) selection for safe path planning in uncertain and unknown environments requires informative, safety-aware, and efficient coordination. Centralized approaches rely on sharing raw sensor data or significant communication overhead, resulting in limited scalability. We propose a distributed, risk-aware multi-agent NBV framework in which each robot maintains a private local 3D Gaussian Splatting map and the team jointly maximizes expected information gain (EIG) restricted to masked zones along planned trajectories. The resulting distributed objective is solved by Consensus ADMM (C-ADMM) over a communication graph, with each robot exchanging only candidate viewpoints, planned trajectory descriptors, and scalar EIG contributions. Collision risk along each trajectory is modeled via Average Value-at-Risk (AV@R) over the local 3DGS map and used both to shape the masking radius and to score planned paths. Experiments in Gibson environments at multiple team sizes show that the distributed formulation approaches the centralized baseline in mapping quality and trajectory safety while reducing communication by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [165] [Affordance2Action: Task-Conditioned Scene-level Affordance Grounding for Real-Time Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04172)
*Litao Liu,Yifan Han,Pengfei Yi,Wenbo Yu,Hanqing Wang,Haoran Du,Enze Yuan,Zilin Yuan,Ruiding Feng,Michael Liu,Qi Zhang,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Affordance2Action (A2A)框架，包含A2A-Bench基准和A2A-AffordGen标注流程，用于场景级、任务驱动的部件功能区域定位，支持单区域和多区域指令对应，揭示了现有方法在真实场景中的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能区域数据集与任务条件化操作场景不匹配：通常聚焦抓取或物体级功能、依赖合成场景、假设单一指令-区域对应关系，无法处理真实杂乱场景中同一物体不同任务对应不同功能区域、以及单任务可能对应多个功能区域的问题。

Method: 构建A2A框架：(1) A2A-AffordGen——智能体辅助标注流程，结合语言模型过滤、交互式部件分割、实例级掩码优化、任务推理指令生成和人工验证；(2) A2A-Bench——面向操作的场景级基准，覆盖单区域和多区域指令对应，支持实时功能区域定位和功能条件操作策略等下游应用。

Result: 实验表明A2A揭示了通用分割、基于VLM的定位和功能知识蒸馏等基线方法的显著差距，同时提升了任务级定位精度并为下游操作提供了有用的空间先验。

Conclusion: A2A框架有效弥合了现有功能区域数据与实际任务条件化操作需求之间的差距，数据集和代码将公开以推动开放研究。

Abstract: Task-conditioned manipulation requires grounding instructions to task-relevant functional parts rather than object categories. This setting is scene-dependent and often one-to-many in cluttered scenes: the same object may afford different interactions across tasks, while a single task may correspond to either one functional region or multiple valid functional regions, depending on the scene layout. Existing affordance datasets and benchmarks remain misaligned with this setting, as they typically focus on grasping or object-level affordances, rely on synthetic scenes, or assume a single instruction-region correspondence. We present Affordance2Action (A2A), a benchmark-centered learning framework for scene-level, task-conditioned part affordance grounding. At its core is A2A-Bench, a manipulation-oriented benchmark that covers both single-region and multi-region instruction correspondences in everyday scenes, with the latter highlighting the ambiguity and diversity of affordance grounding in realistic multi-object environments. To construct it at scale, we build A2A-AffordGen, an agent-assisted annotation pipeline that combines language-model filtering, interactive part segmentation, instance-level mask-out refinement, task-reasoning instruction generation, and human verification. A2A-Bench's supervision further supports diverse downstream applications, with real-time affordance grounding and affordance-conditioned manipulation policies as two representative examples. Experiments show that A2A exposes substantial gaps in generic segmentation, VLM-based grounding, and affordance distillation baselines, while improving task-level localization and providing useful spatial priors for downstream manipulation. All datasets and code will be publicly released to promote open research.

</details>


### [166] [Distribution-Free Risk-Aware Planning and Control Under Uncertainty Using Conformal Spectral Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04185)
*Junsik Eom,Tulga Ersal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险感知模型预测控制（RA-MPC）框架，通过集成预测集来保证风险控制在用户指定阈值以下，无需对底层不确定性分布做假设。该框架扩展了保形风险控制（CRC）到一般谱风险度量，并在仿真车辆避障场景中验证了其安全性和求解效率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态和不确定环境中，安全导航通常依赖于对真实不确定性的准确估计或假设，但由于数据有限或信息不完善，准确刻画不确定性分布非常困难。对不确定性及其相关风险的错误理解即使在高风险规避水平下也可能导致危险决策。

Method: 提出RA-MPC框架，首先开发了一种无分布的风险量化框架，将保形风险控制（CRC）扩展到一般谱风险度量以生成预测集；然后将预测集集成到MPC框架中，提供关于谱风险约束满足的统计安全保证。

Result: 在仿真车辆避障场景中，与基线RA-MPC框架相比，所提方法在提升安全性的同时减少了求解时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的RA-MPC框架能够在不对不确定性分布做假设的情况下，提供谱风险约束的统计安全保证，有效提升动态不确定环境下的导航安全性和计算效率。

Abstract: Safe navigation in dynamic and uncertain environments often relies on accurate estimation of, or assumptions about, the true underlying uncertainty. However, accurately characterizing the true uncertainty distribution is often difficult due to limited data or imperfect information. An incorrect understanding of the uncertainty and its associated risk may lead to dangerous decisions even under high levels of risk aversion. To address this issue, we propose a risk-aware model predictive control (RA-MPC) framework that incorporates prediction sets to guarantee risk control below a user-specified threshold without requiring assumptions about the underlying uncertainty distribution. To generate the prediction sets, we develop a distribution-free risk quantification framework that extends conformal risk control (CRC) to general spectral risk measures. We then show that incorporating the prediction sets into the MPC framework provides statistical safety guarantees in terms of spectral risk constraint satisfaction even under uncertainty misspecification. We validate the proposed framework in simulated vehicle obstacle avoidance scenarios, demonstrating improved safety and reduced solve time compared to a baseline RA-MPC framework.

</details>


### [167] [DLO-Lab: Benchmarking Deformable Linear Object Manipulations with Differentiable Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04206)
*Junyi Cao,Yian Wang,Ziyan Xiong,Chunru Lin,Zhehuan Chen,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为可变形线性物体（DLO）操作设计的可微仿真器，并基于该仿真器构建了基准测试套件和专用DLO智能体，通过策略性抓取点提议和长时域任务分解来应对拓扑复杂性和抓取敏感性挑战，最后通过策略学习算法评估和仿真到现实迁移实验验证了平台的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DLO操作研究局限于狭窄、任务特定的问题，依赖真实世界演示或手工设计的启发式方法，难以扩展到实践中多样的材料和任务；同时现有仿真环境对广谱材料行为的支持有限。

Method: 提出可微仿真器，支持多种材料属性建模（可扩展性、弹性、弯曲塑性、与其他物体的复杂交互）；基于此仿真器构建基准任务套件；引入专用DLO智能体，通过策略性抓取点提议和长时域任务分解来管理拓扑复杂性和抓取敏感性。

Result: 通过多种策略学习算法评估和仿真到现实迁移实验，验证了平台在推动DLO操作方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 该可微仿真器及配套基准和智能体为通用DLO操作的学习与评估提供了坚实基础，有效解决了现有方法在材料多样性和任务泛化方面的局限性。

Abstract: We address the challenge of enabling robots to manipulate deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as ropes, cables, and rubber bands. Prior work has primarily focused on narrow, task-specific problems, often relying on real-world demonstrations or handcrafted heuristics. Such approaches, however, struggle to scale to the wide variety of materials and tasks encountered in practice, and collecting sufficiently diverse real-world data is often impractical. Additionally, existing simulation environments offer limited support for the broad spectrum of material behaviors necessary for generalizable DLO manipulation. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a differentiable simulator explicitly designed for versatile DLO manipulation. Our simulator models a wide range of material properties-including (in)extensibility, elasticity, bending plasticity, and complex interactions with other objects-providing a robust foundation for learning and evaluating manipulation skills. Building on this simulator, we propose a benchmark suite of representative tasks that highlight the unique challenges of DLO manipulation. The successful execution of these tasks is often hindered by the topological complexity and grasp sensitivity inherent to DLOs. Therefore, we introduce a specialized DLO agent that explicitly manages these challenges by proposing strategic grasping points and decomposing long-horizon tasks to maximize control authority. Finally, we evaluate various policy-learning algorithms using our framework, alongside sim-to-real transfer experiments, demonstrating our platform's potential to advance DLO manipulation.

</details>


### [168] [Towards Estimating Normal and Shear Interface Pressures in Prosthetic Sockets via Least Squares and Mechanics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04222)
*Axel González Cornejo,Tianhao Yu,Chi Hwan Lee,Edgar Bolívar-Nieto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估假肢接受腔界面压力传感模型的测试平台，通过全局力/力矩和局部法向/剪切压力两种验证信号，分析了准静态弹簧-质量接触模型在稀疏传感条件下的表现，并引入偏差项改进模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假肢接受腔适配仍依赖手工迭代，缺乏客观指标。传统压力传感器存在漂移、仅能测量法向压力且无法解耦剪切分量等问题，亟需开发模型来增强有限测量数据。

Method: 构建测试平台，使用稀疏传感簇（四通道电容传感）采集局部界面载荷（法向和剪切），同时测量全局力/力矩。采用准静态弹簧-质量接触模型，通过两阶段凸最小二乘问题辨识参数，并引入常数偏差项进行校正。

Result: 静态加载验证表明，估计常数偏差项能减少全局力/力矩通道的稳态偏移，并改善与局部测量的一致性。帕累托前沿敏感性分析展示了包含偏差项时全局与局部目标之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该测试平台和验证流程为评估稀疏压力传感下的机械模型提供了有效手段，偏差项校正对提升模型精度具有重要作用。

Abstract: Prosthetic socket fitting remains largely manual and iterative, and objective fit metrics are still limited. Part of the challenge is the lack of long-term real-life pressure data at the residual limb--socket interface. Traditional pressure sensors are prone to drift over time, and capture only normal pressures at sparse locations within the socket, missing a critical component for biomechanical analysis: shear. Although some sensors can report both normal and shear interface stresses, these components are often difficult to decouple because of measurement crosstalk. One potential path forward is to develop models that can augment available measurements. This work introduces a testbed to evaluate model performance under sparse pressure sensing using two complementary validation signals: (i) the global wrench (\ie, total forces and moments expressed in an orthonormal frame) transmitted through the socket, by an artificial residual-limb, and (ii) local interface loads (\ie, decoupled normal and shear pressure components in a right-hand-rule orthogonal frame that lives in each instrumented location) measured by sparse sensing clusters, each composed of four capacitance-sensing channels. Rather than presenting full-field pressure estimates, the focus is on an analysis sequence that quantifies how well candidate mechanical models explain both global and local measurements under controlled conditions. A quasi-static spring--mass contact model is evaluated, and its parameters are identified via a two-stage convex least-squares problem. Validation under static loading shows that estimating constant bias terms reduces steady offsets in the wrench channels and improves agreement with local measurements. A Pareto-front sensitivity analysis further illustrates how the trade-off between global and local objectives changes when bias terms are included.

</details>


### [169] [PerceptTwin: Semantic Scene Reconstruction for Iterative LLM Planning and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04226)
*Charlie Gauthier,Sacha Morin,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PerceptTwin是一个全自动管道，能够直接从机器人感知栈生成的语义场景表示中构建交互式仿真环境，通过结合开放词汇对象映射、3D资产生成、可行性预测和常识条件检查，用于验证和优化机器人规划。实验表明，该方法将GPT系列规划器的规划成功率平均提升约39%，并将人类规划验证准确率提升高达18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仿真环境构建过程繁琐，为每个机器人操作环境创建定制仿真不可行，亟需一种自动化方法直接从机器人感知结果生成交互式仿真。

Method: 提出PerceptTwin全自动管道：(1) 从机器人感知栈获取语义场景表示；(2) 结合开放词汇对象映射与3D资产生成；(3) 引入可行性预测和常识条件检查；(4) 借鉴AI对齐文献，引入LLM评判器验证规划正确性与人类偏好一致性。

Result: 在测试任务套件中，PerceptTwin使GPT5、GPT5Mini、GPT5Nano规划器的规划成功率平均提升约39%；对于因技能前置条件未满足而失败的规划，人类规划验证准确率平均提升高达18%。

Conclusion: 从机器人感知出发的开放词汇场景仿真作为更安全、更可靠机器人规划的基础具有巨大潜力，能够有效增强规划安全并抵御有害的黑盒提示攻击。

Abstract: Simulation environments are useful for both robot policy learning and planning verification and validation. Traditionally, the process of creating a simulation was onerous. Creating a bespoke simulation environment for each individual environment that a robot would operate in was simply infeasible. In this work, we introduce PerceptTwin, a fully automatic pipeline that constructs interactive simulations directly from semantic scene representations produced by a robot's perception stack. PerceptTwin combines open-vocabulary object maps with 3D asset generation, affordance prediction, and commonsense condition checking. These interactive simulations can be used to validate and refine plans before they are executed on the robot hardware. Borrowing from the AI alignment literature, we also introduce an LLM judge that verifies plan correctness and alignment with human preferences. Experiments show that PerceptTwin feedback allows LLM planners to refine plans, enhance safety, and resist harmful black-box prompting attacks. In our suite of tasks, PerceptTwin improves plan success by an average of approximately 39% for GPT5, GPT5Mini, and GPT5Nano planners. Additionally, PerceptTwin also improves human plan verification by up to 18% on average for plans that fail due to unfilled skill preconditions. Our results demonstrate the potential of open-vocabulary scene simulation from robot perception as a foundation for safer, more reliable robot planning.

</details>


### [170] [What Are We Actually Benchmarking in Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04233)
*Tianchong Jiang,Xiangshan Tan,Samuel Wheeler,Luzhe Sun,Tewodros W. Ayalew,Matthew Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文识别了机器人操作基准测试的四种失败模式（捷径可解性、缺乏统计显著性、渐进过拟合、数据源依赖性），并针对每种模式提出诊断方法。对LIBERO、CALVIN、SimplerEnv、RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0进行审计发现，LIBERO和CALVIN多项诊断未通过，而RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0表现更好但鲜少被用于宣称进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准测试往往将单一评估设置下的分数视为通用操作能力的证据，但该做法存在根本性缺陷。研究者希望系统性地识别并量化这些缺陷，避免基准分数被过度解读为真正的能力进步。

Method: 识别四种失败模式并各提出一种诊断方法：捷径可解性诊断、统计显著性检验、过拟合检测、数据源依赖性分析。随后对五个代表性基准（LIBERO、CALVIN、SimplerEnv、RoboCasa、RoboTwin 2.0）进行实证审计。

Result: LIBERO上，0.09B参数的无语言编码器探针即可达到或接近报告的最优结果，且大多数报告的性能提升不具备统计显著性。CALVIN中，在训练范围内随机化积木位置会导致所有测试策略性能下降。RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0通过诊断的情况更好，但它们在近期进展声明中出现的频率远低于前两者。

Conclusion: 基准分数不应被直接视为通用操作能力的可靠证据，作者公开了四种诊断工具的实现，供研究者在使用基准分数宣称进展前进行自检。

Abstract: A robotics benchmark score measures success under one fixed evaluation setup, yet is routinely treated as evidence of general manipulation capability. We identify four failure modes, each of which weakens or invalidates a benchmark's role as a valid proxy for that capability: shortcut solvability, lack of statistical significance, creeping overfitting, and data-source dependence. We propose one diagnostic per failure mode. We audit LIBERO, CALVIN, SimplerEnv, RoboCasa, and RoboTwin 2.0 under these diagnostics. LIBERO and CALVIN fail multiple diagnostics. RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 fail fewer, despite appearing far less often in recent progress claims. On LIBERO, a 0.09B probe with no language encoder scores at or near reported SOTA, and most reported gains are not provably statistically significant. On CALVIN, randomizing block poses within the training range drops performance for every tested policy. We release the four diagnostics with reference implementations for authors and reviewers to apply before treating a benchmark score as evidence of progress. Code and artifacts are available at https://ripl.github.io/manipulation_benchmark_audit/.

</details>


### [171] [RSC: Decentralized Rigid Formation Flocking for Large-Scale Swarms via Hybrid Predictive Control and Online Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04248)
*Ganyu Zou,Linhan Wang,Chen Dai,Siji Chen,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RSC的分散式控制框架，通过有限时域轨迹预测与反应式APF安全控制器结合的混合架构，以及在线领导者-跟随者重配置机制，实现了大规模刚性编队集群在杂乱环境中的高成功率（83%）编队保持、避障与目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分散式刚体编队控制方法在杂乱环境中难以维持严格的智能体间距离约束，常陷入局部极小值死锁、产生高频控制振荡或灵活性不足，导致成功率低。

Method: 提出RSC框架：1）混合架构，集成有限时域轨迹预测（长期规划逃逸局部极小值）与反应式APF安全控制器（保证短期安全）；2）在线领导者-跟随者重配置机制，基于稳定角色交换加速编队穿越障碍后的重组。

Result: 在25架无人机杂乱环境测试中，RSC在严格标准（无碰撞、边误差<10%）下达到83%成功率，远超基线方法（<5%）。

Conclusion: RSC有效统一了刚性编队保持、避障和目标跟踪三个任务，显著提升了杂乱环境下的编队控制成功率。

Abstract: Decentralized rigid formation flocking requires a swarm of autonomous agents to maintain a predetermined geometric configuration while moving, relying solely on local sensing and communication. However, existing decentralized control methods struggle to maintain strict inter-agent distance constraints in cluttered environments, often suffering from local minima deadlocks, high frequency control oscillations, or limited flexibility during obstacle navigation, resulting in low success rate. To address these limitations, we propose Rigid Swarm Control (RSC), a decentralized control framework for large-scale rigid formation flocking. To escape local minima via robust long-term planning while ensuring short-term safety, RSC integrates finite-horizon trajectory predictions with a reactive artificial potential field (APF) safety controller within a hybrid architecture. Furthermore, to accelerate formation reassembly after obstacle traversal without interrupting task execution, RSC introduces an online leader-follower reconfiguration mechanism based on stable role exchange. Extensive evaluations in challenging cluttered environments with 25 UAVs demonstrate that RSC reliably unifies rigid formation maintenance, obstacle avoidance, and target tracking. Under strict success criteria - collision-free operation with a maximum relative edge-length error below 10%, RSC achieves an 83% success rate, significantly outperforming existing heuristic and learning-based baselines that fall below 5%.

</details>


### [172] [Instant-Fold: In-Context Imitation Learning for Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04269)
*Yilong Wang,Cheng Qian,Edward Johns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Instant-Fold是一个基于上下文模仿学习的可变形物体操作框架，通过单次人类演示即可推断并执行多种操作模式，无需梯度更新，且能从仿真零样本迁移到真实环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作（DOM）面临高维、部分可观测状态的挑战，这些状态通过长程、拓扑变化的交互演化，且存在多种有效的操作模式，现有方法难以处理此类复杂任务。

Method: 首先通过时间对比预训练学习形变感知的视觉表征，然后构建基于流匹配（flow-matching）的Transformer策略，以演示为条件预测动作，实现上下文模仿学习。

Result: 在仿真环境中训练后，Instant-Fold能够泛化到多种不同的折叠模式，并零样本迁移到真实世界场景，无需额外数据收集或微调。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需梯度更新的上下文模仿学习框架，有效解决了可变形物体操作中多模态、长程交互的难点，实现了仿真到真实的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Deformable object manipulation (DOM) is challenging due to high-dimensional, partially observable states that evolve through long-horizon, topology-changing interactions with multiple valid manipulation modes. We introduce Instant-Fold, an in-context imitation learning framework for DOM. Given a single human demonstration, our policy infers and executes diverse manipulation modes directly from the demonstration, including variations in spatial execution and ordering, without requiring gradient updates. Our approach first learns deformation-aware visual representations via temporal contrastive pretraining, after which a flow-matching transformer policy conditioned on the demonstration predicts actions to execute the intended manipulation mode. Trained entirely in simulation, Instant-Fold generalizes across diverse folding modes and transfers zero-shot to real-world settings without additional data collection or finetuning. Videos are available at https://instant-fold.github.io.

</details>


### [173] [Think Fast and Far: Long-Horizon Online POMDP Planning via Rapid State Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04355)
*Yuanchu Liang,Edward Kim,J. Arden Knoll,Wil Thomason,Zachary Kingston,Lydia E. Kavraki,Hanna Kurniawati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ROP-RAS3，一种基于参考的在线POMDP求解器，通过快速采样状态空间生成宏动作来偏置信念空间采样，在长时域POMDP（最多3000步前瞻、35维状态空间）中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域POMDP问题仍然难以求解，现有在线求解器受限于动作空间的穷举枚举，难以扩展到大规模、连续/混合动作空间的长时域问题。

Method: 提出ROP-RAS3，利用极快的基于采样的运动规划技术在线采样状态空间并生成多样化宏动作，利用这些宏动作偏置信念空间采样，从而避免对动作空间的穷举枚举。收敛速率依赖于采样动作数量而非动作空间大小。

Result: 在多种长时域POMDP（最多3000前瞻步、35维状态空间）上，ROP-RAS3在成功率上相比最先进方法提升数倍，且支持连续、离散及混合空间。还通过物理机器人平台验证了可行性。

Conclusion: ROP-RAS3通过基于参考的快速采样策略有效解决了长时域POMDP的求解困难，虽参考最优解不保证与全局最优解一致，但实证表明其在实际任务中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) are a general and principled framework for motion planning under uncertainty. Despite tremendous improvement in the scalability of POMDP solvers, long-horizon POMDPs remain difficult to solve. To alleviate the difficulty, this paper proposes a new approximate online POMDP solver, called Reference-Based Online POMDP Planning via Rapid State Space Sampling (ROP-RAS3). ROP-RAS3 uses novel extremely fast sampling-based motion planning techniques to sample the state space and generate a diverse set of macro actions online, which are then used to bias belief-space sampling and infer high-quality policies without requiring exhaustive enumeration of the action space -- a fundamental constraint for modern online POMDP solvers. ROP-RAS3 converges to a near-optimal reference-based solution at a rate that depends on the number of sampled actions, rather than the size of the action space. ROP-RAS3 is evaluated on various long-horizon POMDPs with up to 3000 lookahead steps and 35-dimensional state spaces, where the state, action and observation spaces can be continuous, discrete, or a hybrid of discrete and continuous. Although the reference-based optimal solution may not be the same as the optimal POMDP solution, empirical results indicate that in all of these problems, in terms of success rate, ROP-RAS3 outperforms other state-of-the-art methods by up to multiple folds. We also demonstrate the capability of our approach on a physical robot demonstration. This work extends the theory and empirical results of our ISRR24 paper. Code can be found at \texttt{https://github.com/RDLLab/ROPRAS3}.

</details>


### [174] [OSCAR: Omni-Embodiment Skeleton-Conditioned World Action Model for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04463)
*Zhuoyuan Wu,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OSCAR是一个精确的动作条件视频世界模型，通过标准化数据流水线和2D骨骼运动渲染作为统一条件表示，在单个GH200 GPU上微调Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B模型，实现了跨机器人本体的泛化和机器人策略的虚拟评估，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在真实机器人评估中面临三个主要挑战：训练数据集场景多样性有限、动作跟随不精确、跨本体泛化能力差。作者旨在通过统一的数据处理流程和条件表示来解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 构建大规模标准化数据流水线，对机器人数据和自我中心人类数据进行整理、过滤和去重，形成联合训练数据集；2) 采用2D运动骨骼渲染作为统一条件表示，实现跨不同机器人臂甚至人手的泛化；3) 在单个GH200 GPU上微调Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B模型。

Result: 在动作跟随、外观质量和运动一致性方面显著优于现有基线（尽管基线模型更大或使用更多GPU）。在RoboArena上的实验表明，OSCAR中的虚拟策略评估与真实世界评估之间存在显著相关性。

Conclusion: OSCAR为纯虚拟世界中评估机器人策略铺平了道路，证明了虚拟评估与真实评估之间的强相关性。

Abstract: We present OSCAR, a precise action-conditioned video world model that generalizes across different robot embodiments and enables robot policy evaluation. Existing video world models face three main challenges for real-world robot evaluation: limited scenario diversity in current robot training datasets, imprecise action following, and poor generalization across embodiments for broad adoption. We tackle these challenges from two perspectives. At its core is a large-scale standardized data pipeline that curates, filters, and deduplicates broad robotics and egocentric human datasets, yielding a clean joint-training dataset that spans diverse tasks, scenarios, actions, and robot embodiments. To condition the video model, we adopt 2D kinematic skeleton rendering as a unified conditioning representation that generalizes across different robot arms or even human hands. We finetune the Cosmos-Predict2.5-2B model on a single GH200 GPU. Our model achieves significant improvement on action following, appearance quality, and motion consistency, compared to existing baselines, which either have a much larger model size or require more GPUs. We further deploy OSCAR to evaluate robot policies from RoboArena. Extensive experiments demonstrate the significant correlation between our virtual policy evaluation in OSCAR and real-world evaluation, paving the way for the future where robot policies can be purely evaluated in virtual generated worlds.

</details>


### [175] [TransTac: Visuo-Tactile Modality Transition via Ultraviolet-Encoded Transparent Elastomers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04477)
*Lingyue Yang,Bin Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TransTac是一种透明紫外编码双目视觉触觉传感器，通过结合视觉观察和标记触觉重建，在单一紧凑设备中实现高分辨率接触几何重建，零样本识别准确率达83.3%，硬件成本约70美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉触觉传感器依赖不透明弹性层，无法实现视觉透明；RGB-D相机在近距离下深度感知严重退化。为克服这些局限性，需要一种既能保持视觉透明又能进行高精度触觉重建的紧凑传感器。

Method: 采用透明弹性体嵌入紫外反射标记，结合先验引导的Delaunay立体匹配算法进行鲁棒稀疏三角化；开发轻量级检测器实现半透明标记的稳定定位。

Result: 先验引导Delaunay匹配较全局分配基线提升约21%的匹配鲁棒性；零样本识别准确率达83.3%，超过不透明触觉基线约50个百分点；跨模态对齐类中心相似度从0.2提升至0.77以上。

Conclusion: TransTac有效解决了视觉透明与触觉重建的兼容性问题，在触觉图像语义理解和跨模态对齐方面显著优于现有方法，且硬件成本低廉。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTS) recover high-resolution contact geometry but typically rely on opaque elastomer layers that prevent visual transparency, while RGB-D cameras provide global depth perception yet degrade significantly at close range. To address this limitation, we present TransTac, a transparent ultraviolet (UV)-encoded binocular VBTS that integrates visual observation and marker-based tactile reconstruction within a single compact device. The system employs a transparent elastomer embedded with UV-reflective markers and a prior-guided Delaunay stereo matching algorithm for robust sparse triangulation.
  To reliably detect densely distributed semitransparent markers, we develop a lightweight detector that enables stable localization under contact and deformation. The proposed prior-guided Delaunay matching improves correspondence robustness by approximately 21% compared with global assignment baselines while maintaining high reconstruction accuracy. In semantic evaluation, TransTac achieves up to 83.3% zero-shot recognition accuracy on tactile images, exceeding opaque tactile baselines by approximately 50 percentage points. Embedding analysis further reveals substantially stronger cross-modal alignment with natural images, with class-center similarity increasing from around 0.2 to over 0.77. Controlled near-distance experiments quantify the degradation of RGB-D depth reliability and demonstrate extended geometric coverage enabled by visuo-tactile integration. Finally, a compact prototype is implemented with an approximate hardware cost of $70.

</details>


### [176] [Cooperative Circumnavigation for Multiple Unmanned Surface Vehicles Without External Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04518)
*Xueming Liu,Lin Li,Xiang Zhou,Tianjiang Hu,Qingrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需外部定位的多无人艇协同目标环绕框架，采用异构感知策略（主动感知与被动感知结合）、最大相关熵卡尔曼滤波和伪线性卡尔曼滤波进行相对位置估计，并设计基于耦合振荡器的编队控制器，通过保证持续激励条件确保滤波器可观测性，实现均匀圆形编队环绕目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多无人艇在缺乏外部定位（如GPS）环境下，仅依赖有限机载传感器实现协同目标环绕编队的问题。

Method: 1) 异构感知策略：主动感知（艇间通信获取相对距离和位移）与被动感知（被动传感器获取非合作目标的方位测量）相结合；2) 采用最大相关熵卡尔曼滤波（MCKF）估计艇间相对位置，伪线性卡尔曼滤波（PLKF）估计艇-目标相对位置；3) 基于耦合振荡器的编队控制器，确保系统持续激励条件以保证滤波器可观测性。

Result: 理论分析证明控制器能使艇间及艇-目标相对运动满足持续激励条件，保证卡尔曼滤波器的可观测性；数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够在无外部定位条件下，通过异构感知和滤波-控制协同设计，实现多USV对目标的均匀圆形环绕编队。

Abstract: This paper proposes a cooperative target circumnavigation framework for multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) operating without external localization. The objective is to maintain a uniform circular formation of a specified radius around a target using only limited onboard sensing. The framework adopts a heterogeneous perception strategy that distinguishes between the asymmetric sensing relationships with the target and among the USVs. Specifically, the USVs obtain relative range and displacement measurements through active perception and inter-vehicle communication, while bearing measurements to a non-cooperative target are acquired via passive sensors. To estimate relative positions--both among USVs and between each USV and the target--we employ a Maximum Correntropy Kalman Filter and a Pseudo-Linear Kalman Filter, respectively. A coupled oscillator-based formation controller is designed to ensure system observability while achieving circumnavigation. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the controller ensures the relative motions between the USVs, as well as that between each USV and the target, satisfy the persistent excitation condition, thereby guaranteeing observability of the Kalman-based filters. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is validated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [177] [MAD: Mapping-Aware World Models for Agile Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04534)
*Xinhong Zhang,Runqing Wang,Yunfan Ren,Ding Yu,Boyu Zhou,Jian Sun,Fang Deng,Jie Chen,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Mapping-Aware Dreamer (MAD)，一种几何感知世界模型用于视觉四旋翼飞行。MAD不直接重建原始图像，而是学习循环潜在动力学来重建机器人中心的占据和可见性网格地图及本体状态，迫使潜在状态编码与避障直接相关的局部几何、可见性历史和自运动。MAD在DiffAero中用GPU并行地图构建模块训练，支持三种策略学习模式。在视觉导航和竞速任务中，MAD优于纯视觉基线，实现模拟9.66 m/s和真实森林5.05 m/s的飞行速度，并成功部署于物理四旋翼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱场景中敏捷飞行的四旋翼需要记忆已观测区域、推断邻近占用空间，并在部分可见性和紧延迟下行动。现有方法在原始图像重建的自监督目标与避障直接需求之间存在差距，缺乏几何感知的世界模型。

Method: 提出MAD，一种几何感知世界模型，用循环潜在动力学重建占据网格图和可见性网格图而非原始图像；设计DiffAero训练框架和GPU并行地图构建模块提供高效监督；支持三种策略学习模式（MAD-Dreamer、PPO、SHAC的特征提取变体）。

Result: 在视觉导航和竞速任务中，MAD在成功率、飞行速度和跨任务迁移上均优于纯视觉基线；产生可解释的地图预测和准确的自运动估计；模拟飞行达9.66 m/s，真实森林实验达5.05 m/s。

Conclusion: 映射感知世界模型为模块化空中导航和端到端学习提供了实用的中间方案，证明几何感知表示对敏捷飞行至关重要。

Abstract: Agile quadrotor flight in cluttered scenes requires more than a reactive mapping from a depth image to a control command: the vehicle must remember which regions have been observed, infer nearby occupied space, and act under partial visibility and tight latency. In this paper, we present Mapping-Aware Dreamer (MAD), a geometry-aware world model for vision-based quadrotor flight. Instead of using raw-image reconstruction as the main self-supervised objective, MAD learns recurrent latent dynamics that reconstruct robocentric occupancy and visibility grid maps together with proprioceptive states. This design forces the latent state to encode local geometry, visibility history, and ego-motion in a form that is directly relevant to collision avoidance. MAD is trained in DiffAero using a GPU-parallel map-construction module that provides high-throughput supervision for occupancy and visibility. The learned representation is used in three policy-learning modes: imagination-based MAD-Dreamer and feature-extractor variants based on PPO and SHAC. Across visual navigation and racing tasks, MAD-based agents achieve higher success rates, faster flight, and better cross-task transfer than corresponding vision-only baselines. The model also produces interpretable map predictions and accurate ego-motion estimates from depth observations. We further deploy the learned policy on a physical quadrotor with an Intel RealSense D435i and demonstrate safe indoor and outdoor flight under limited sensing, reaching 9.66 m/s in simulation and 5.05 m/s in real-world forest experiments. These results show that mapping-aware world models provide a practical middle ground between modular aerial navigation and end-to-end learning.

</details>


### [178] [MineXplore: An Open-Source Reinforcement Learning Exploration Benchmark for GNSS-Denied Underground Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04569)
*Abhishek S,Badrikanath Praharaj,Sreeram MV*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MineXplore是一个基于智利地下铜矿数据集构建的开源MuJoCo导航基准，包含104,423平方米隧道网络，支持GPU加速强化学习管线，并通过PPO基线验证了稳定可复现的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下矿山环境（GPS拒止、光照退化、拓扑复杂）对自主机器人导航提出极端挑战，但现有开源生态缺乏基于真实矿山几何结构且兼容GPU加速学习管线的仿真基准。

Method: 基于Leung等2017年智利地下铜矿数据集，通过六阶段轮廓到MJCF的转换管线重建104,423平方米隧道网络，包含八边形墙壁横截面、LiDAR锯齿状墙几何、三种地形摩擦区域、全局5度倾斜和周期性点光源。几何保真度通过IoU=0.9538验证，纹理相似度达79.4%。

Result: 单智能体PPO基线在RLlib上经5个独立随机种子训练，最佳滚动覆盖率达88.89%（3/5种子达到90%覆盖目标），验证了平台在真实地下感知和拓扑条件下支持稳定可复现的策略学习。

Conclusion: MineXplore填补了地下矿山导航仿真基准的空白，提供了高几何保真度的开源平台，支持可复现的强化学习策略研究。

Abstract: Underground mines present extreme conditions for autonomous robot navigation: GPS is denied, lighting is degraded, and tunnel topology is loop-rich and non-convex. Simulation benchmarks grounded in real production-mine geometry and compatible with GPU-accelerated learning pipelines do not yet exist in the open-source ecosystem. We present MineXplore, an open-source MuJoCo-based navigation benchmark derived from the Leung et al. 2017 Chilean underground copper mine dataset. The environment reconstructs a 104,423 sq.m tunnel network through an six-stage contour-to-MJCF pipeline incorporating octagonal wall cross-sections, LiDAR-sourced jagged wall geometry, three terrain friction zones, a global 5 degree incline, and periodic spot lighting. Geometric fidelity is validated at an Intersection over Union (IoU) of 0.9538 against the source survey map, and surface texture similarity scores 79.4% across six structural dimensions. A single-agent PPO baseline trained via RLlib across five independent random seeds achieves a best rolling coverage of 88.89% (3 of 5 seeds reaching the 90% coverage target), confirming that MineXplore supports stable and reproducible policy learning under realistic underground sensing and topology.

</details>


### [179] [BPDA-GMM: Bayesian Probabilistic Data Association via Gaussian Mixture Models for Semantic SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04618)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Haolan Zhang,Xiem HoangVan,Antonio Sgorbissa,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BPDA-GMM 是一个基于贝叶斯概率数据关联的语义SLAM框架，利用狄利克雷过程先验和中国餐馆过程进行在线数据关联，支持动态增长的地图，并通过α散度退火和去耦后端优化提升了在感知混淆环境下的轨迹精度和语义建图质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率数据关联（PDA）方法在语义SLAM中存在以下局限：1）假设固定地标集；2）地图增长时重新计算关联权重；3）依赖手工调参的零假设权重。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出一个在线、自适应的贝叶斯PDA框架。

Method: 1）使用狄利克雷过程先验诱导中国餐馆过程（CRP）进行数据关联，累计证据偏好已有地标，浓度参数分配新地标概率；2）联合语义-几何门控筛选候选匹配；3）CRP加权的关联概率计算及语义高斯闭式更新；4）形成高斯混合模型，取主成分作为最大混合语义因子传入后端；5）引入α散度退火策略提升关联权重不明确时的判别能力；6）解耦后端将语义因子的位姿雅可比置零，使检测噪声只优化地标而不扰动轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实室内数据集上，BPDA-GMM在轨迹精度、语义建图质量以及对感知混淆和分类器误差的鲁棒性方面均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: BPDA-GMM通过贝叶斯概率数据关联和CRP模型，有效实现了语义SLAM中在线、可增长的数据关联，提升了系统在复杂环境下的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: Probabilistic data association (PDA) improves semantic SLAM in perceptually aliased scenes, but existing methods often assume a fixed landmark set, recompute association weights as the map grows, or rely on hand-tuned null-hypothesis weights. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{BPDA-GMM}, an online Bayesian PDA framework for semantic SLAM with a growing object-level map. BPDA-GMM uses a Dirichlet-process prior to induce a Chinese Restaurant Process (CRP) association model, where accumulated evidence favors existing landmarks, and the concentration parameter assigns probability mass to new landmarks. For each semantic detection, plausible candidates are selected by a joint semantic-geometric gate, CRP-weighted association probabilities are computed, and object landmarks are updated as semantic Gaussians in closed form. The resulting landmark set forms a Gaussian mixture model, and its dominant component is passed to the back-end as a max-mixture semantic factor. When association weights are inconclusive, an ambiguity-triggered $α$-divergence tempering step improves discrimination. Finally, a decoupled back-end zeroes the pose Jacobian of semantic factors, allowing noisy detections to refine landmarks without directly perturbing the trajectory. Experiments in simulation and on a real indoor dataset demonstrate improved trajectory accuracy, semantic mapping quality, and robustness to perceptual aliasing and classifier errors over state-of-the-art baselines. Code and video are publicly available at https://github.com/thanhnguyencanh/BPDA-SLAM.

</details>


### [180] [CoRe-MoE: Contrastive Reweighted Mixture of Experts for Multi-Terrain Humanoid Locomotion with Gait Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04718)
*Kailun Huang,Zikang Xie,Yanzhe Xie,Panpan Liao,Fanghai Zhang,Yanheng Mai,Wenhao Xu,Yunheng Wang,Renjing Xu,Haohui Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoRe-MoE两阶段强化学习框架，将步态生成与地形适应解耦，先在第一阶段学习稳定步态策略（含行走、奔跑及平滑过渡），再在第二阶段引入地形感知MoE分支并通过对比学习塑造门控网络，最终通过加权融合实现多地形适应。仿真和实物实验（Unitree G1机器人）均验证了其在成功率、稳定性、多地形适应性及抗干扰方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要实现行走与奔跑之间的平滑过渡，并在多种复杂地形上保持自然稳定的运动，但步态转换与多地形适应统一在一个策略中面临梯度干扰和视觉/动态变化导致的分布偏移问题。现有MoE架构虽能缓解多技能干扰，但朴素联合训练难以产生清晰的专家特化。

Method: 提出CoRe-MoE两阶段强化学习框架：第一阶段学习稳定运动策略（自然行走/奔跑及平滑过渡）；第二阶段引入地形感知MoE分支，使用对比学习目标训练门控网络，使其捕获结构化地形表征并促进专家特化；最终通过基策略与地形感知分支的加权融合输出动作。

Result: 仿真实验在成功率、运动稳定性和多地形适应性上优于基线方法；零样本部署在Unitree G1人形机器人上，实现了在楼梯、斜坡、台阶、障碍物及非结构化户外地形上的鲁棒行走和奔跑，并保持精确落脚点和动态稳定性。

Conclusion: CoRe-MoE通过解耦步态生成与地形适应，结合对比学习引导的MoE架构，有效解决了多任务干扰和专家特化问题，实现了人形机器人在多地形上的稳定自然运动与步态平滑过渡。

Abstract: Humans primarily rely on walking and running to traverse complex terrains, without resorting to unnecessarily complex motion patterns. Similarly, humanoid robots should achieve smooth transitions between walking and running while maintaining natural and stable locomotion. However, unifying gait transition and multi-terrain adaptation within a single policy remains challenging due to gradient interference and the distribution shift induced by terrain-dependent visual and dynamic variations. Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures can alleviate multi-skill interference, naive joint training often fails to yield clear expert specialization, limiting their effectiveness. To address these challenges, we propose CoRe-MoE, a two-stage reinforcement learning framework that decouples gait generation from terrain adaptation. In the first stage, a stable locomotion policy is learned to produce natural walking and running behaviors with smooth transitions. In the second stage, a terrain-aware MoE branch is introduced and trained with a contrastive objective to shape the gating network, enabling it to capture structured terrain representations and promote expert specialization. The final action is obtained via weighted fusion of the base gait policy and the terrain-aware branch, allowing the policy to preserve stable locomotion patterns while adapting to complex terrains. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms baseline approaches in terms of success rate, locomotion stability, and multi-terrain adaptability. Furthermore, zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot validates the effectiveness of our framework, achieving robust walking and running across stairs, slopes, steps, obstacles, and unstructured outdoor terrains, while maintaining accurate foothold placement and dynamic stability under external disturbances.

</details>


### [181] [CADENCE: Predicting Realized MAPF Execution Time Beyond Sum of Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04746)
*Abhishek S,Badrikanath Praharaj,Sreeram MV*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过硬件实验（480次试次）研究了MAPF规划指标与实际执行时间之间的差距，发现原始运动负担（如makespan、转弯、连续移动、启停转换）是除SoC之外预测执行完成时间最可靠的指标，可将MAE降低48.6%-59.8%，而交互感知协调特征带来的改进较小且不统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF算法评估指标（如SoC、makespan、规划器运行时间）可能无法准确反映实际执行性能，存在评估与实际执行之间的差距，因此需要系统研究哪些规划阶段可获得的特征最能预测实际执行时间。

Method: 在7×7固定工作单元上使用7台差分驱动机器人，生成120个规划（覆盖5个Empty、5个Medium Random、5个Bottleneck场景），每个规划执行4次，共480试次。使用场景留出岭回归模型和试次级混合效应模型，比较SoC、总规划行程成本、原始运动负担、交互感知协调结构四类特征对实际完成时间的预测能力。

Result: SoC单独具有信息性但不完整；原始运动负担带来最强改进，相比仅用SoC的模型，MAE降低48.6%-59.8%，RMSE降低44.2%-61.4%；交互感知协调特征在混合效应分析中显示出较小且不统一的增益。

Conclusion: 原始运动负担是除SoC之外最可靠的额外预测信号，表明执行时间差距的很大部分在机器人开始移动之前就已体现在离线规划中。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) algorithms are increasingly used to plan motion for robot teams in industrial warehouses and robotic shared workspaces, but standard MAPF algorithm evaluation metrics, such as Sum of Costs (SoC), makespan, and planner runtime, can obscure how planner choices translate into realistic execution performance. We present CADENCE (Coordination and Action-Driven Estimation for Networked Continuous Execution), a hardware study of this evaluation gap on a fixed 7 by 7 workcell with seven differential drive robots, asking which features available before execution can best predict final wall-clock completion time. We compare SoC, total planned travel cost, primitive motion burden (how much basic motion the plan requires, such as makespan, turns, consecutive moves, and start-stop transitions), and interaction aware coordination structure (how much inter-robot coordination the plan induces, such as dependency links, interacting robot pairs, dependency depth, and crowding exposure). To test this, we generate 120 plans across 15 scenarios -- 5 Empty, 5 Medium Random, and 5 Bottleneck and execute each plan four times, yielding a 480 trial hardware corpus. Using both a scenario-held -- out ridge model and a trial-level mixed-effects model, we find that SoC alone is informative but incomplete, while primitive motion burden gives the strongest improvement, reducing held out error by about 48.6%-59.8% in MAE and 44.2%-61.4% in RMSE relative to SoC-only models. Interaction-aware coordination features add smaller, less uniform gains, most clearly in the mixed-effects analysis. Across both models and uncertainty checks, primitive motion burden is the most reliable additional signal beyond SoC, suggesting that much of the execution time gap is already visible in the offline plan before any robot starts moving.

</details>


### [182] [COP-Q: Safety-First Reinforcement Learning for Robot Control via Cholesky-Ordered Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04749)
*Guopeng Li,Moritz A. Zanger,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Julian F. P. Kooij*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COP-Q方法，通过Cholesky分解在联合Q值空间中编码目标优先级，利用目标间协方差来平衡安全约束保守性与奖励样本效率，在机器人运动控制与安全导航中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线安全强化学习中，奖励和安全Q值由独立的评论家集成学习，每个目标独立处理不确定性，忽略了目标间的相关性，导致过于保守的价值估计，降低了样本效率。

Method: 提出Cholesky-Ordered Projection Q-learning (COP-Q)，在联合Q值空间中构建广义置信界，利用Cholesky分解将目标优先级编码为序列形式，在保持安全保守性的同时自适应减少奖励目标上的过度保守性。

Result: 在Brax机器人运动控制和安全导航任务上，覆盖硬安全与软安全设置，COP-Q实现了强安全性能以及有竞争力或更好的样本效率。

Conclusion: COP-Q计算开销小且易于与现有深度Q学习框架兼容，为安全强化学习中多目标价值估计提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Safe robot control requires maximizing return while satisfying safety constraints. In off-policy safe reinforcement learning, reward and safety Q-values are commonly learned by separate critic ensembles, with uncertainty handled independently for each objective. This objective-wise treatment neglects inter-objective correlation and can lead to overly conservative value estimates, thereby reducing sample efficiency. To address this issue, we propose Cholesky-Ordered Projection Q-learning (COP-Q), a safety-first method that incorporates inter-objective covariance into vector-valued Q-value estimation. COP-Q constructs a generalized confidence bound in the joint Q-value space and uses Cholesky factorization to encode objective priority in a sequential form. This preserves conservatism on safety while adaptively reducing excessive conservatism on the reward objective. The resulting estimate is used in both temporal-difference target computation and actor optimization. COP-Q incurs minimal computational overhead and is readily compatible with most existing deep Q-learning frameworks. Experiments on robot locomotion in Brax and safe navigation in Safety-Gymnasium, covering both hard- and soft-safety settings, demonstrate that COP-Q achieves strong safety performance together with competitive or improved sample efficiency relative to representative baselines.

</details>


### [183] [HapTile: A Haptic-Informed Vision-Tactile-Language-Action Dataset for Contact-Rich Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04825)
*Amirhosein Alian,Yongqiang Zhao,Shiyi Gu,Xuyang Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapTile是一个接触引导的视觉触觉操作数据集，通过在机器人末端执行器集成 fingertip 触觉传感、在遥操作端提供触觉反馈，并结合语言指令和动作轨迹，弥补了现有VLA数据集在触觉信息、任务多样性和语言条件方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）数据集大多仅依赖视觉信息，缺乏触觉传感；而包含触觉信息的数据集又往往缺少任务多样性、语言条件和动作轨迹的组合。同时，现有的遥操作管道很少为操作员提供触觉反馈，尽管触觉反馈在演示质量和操作稳定性中已被证明至关重要。

Method: 构建了HapTile数据集采集平台：1）在机器人末端执行器上设计定制fingertip触觉传感器，提供底层物理接触感知；2）将触觉反馈集成到遥操作控制器中，使操作员能够实时感知接触交互。数据集涵盖 pick-and-place、折叠、按压、堆叠等多种日常接触密集型操作任务，每个任务配有语言指令、同步的视觉触觉观测和动作轨迹。

Result: 提供了一个包含丰富接触感知的多任务操作数据集，并在接触密集型策略学习上使用两种基线模型进行了基准评测。

Conclusion: HapTile通过双层次触觉信息（机器人端触觉传感 + 操作员端触觉反馈）提升了接触密集型操作的数据质量，为基于触觉的策略学习提供了标准化基准数据集。

Abstract: Despite the importance of tactile sensing for reliable manipulation, most existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) datasets remain vision-only, and those that do incorporate tactile information typically lack the joint combination of task diversity, language conditioning, and action trajectories. Furthermore, existing teleoperation pipelines rarely provide haptic feedback to the operator, despite its established role in demonstration quality and manipulation stability. In this work, we present HapTile, a contact-grounded visuotactile manipulation dataset that advances beyond vision-only trajectory datasets by embedding physical interaction sensing at two levels: fingertip tactile feedback at the robot end-effector, and haptic-informed demonstrations at the teleoperator side. The data collection platform integrates haptic feedback directly into the teleoperation controller, enabling the operator to perceive contact interactions in real time. It is built around a standard and reproducible robotic system equipped with custom-designed fingertip tactile sensors. The dataset comprises everyday manipulation tasks spanning a broad range of contact-rich skills, including pick-and-place, folding, pressing, stacking, and other routine activities. Each task is paired with language instructions that condition the policy on the manipulation objective, together with synchronized visuotactile observations and action trajectories. In addition, we provide a benchmarking study on contact-rich policy learning using two baseline models to evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed contact-grounded dataset. The dataset and additional details are available on our website: haptile-dataset.github.io.

</details>


### [184] [M3imic: Learning a Versatile Whole-Body Controller for Multimodal Motion Mimicking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04829)
*Zuxing Lu,Ziang Zheng,Yao Lyu,Jingyu Liu,Feihong Zhang,Song Lu,Xin Yuan,Changyin Sun,Xingxing Zuo,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出M3imic框架，通过多模态编码器统一异构运动参考模态（机器人关节角、人体姿态轨迹、末端执行器位姿），在仿真中大规模强化学习训练单一策略，实现真实世界机器人无需重训即可适应多种运动参考模态，在Unitree G1上仿真测试集达到98.42%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽视了密集机器人关节角和稀疏末端执行器位姿之间的表征不匹配问题，缺乏能够统一异构运动参考模态的通用全身控制框架。

Method: 提出M3imic多模态全身控制框架，使用模态特定编码器将多种运动参考模态（关节角、人体姿态、末端位姿）映射到共享潜在空间，在仿真中通过大规模强化学习训练单一策略，实现sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人仿真测试集上达到98.42%峰值成功率，在仿真和真实世界实验中均展现出优异的泛化能力。

Conclusion: M3imic成功实现了无需模态特定重训即可跨多种运动参考模态工作的通用全身控制策略，有效解决了异构运动参考模态的统一问题。

Abstract: Building a general-purpose whole-body controller is essential for enabling diverse motion capabilities in humanoid robots across a wide range of downstream tasks, including locomotion and loco-manipulation. Different tasks rely on distinct motion reference modalities: locomotion primarily depends on coordinated robot joint trajectories, whereas manipulation requires precise end-effector trajectory tracking. Existing methods often overlook the representational mismatch between dense robot joint angles and sparse end-effector poses. To address this, we propose Multi-Modal Mimic (M3imic), a versatile multi-modal whole-body control framework that unifies heterogeneous motion reference modalities, including robot joint angles, human pose trajectories, and end-effector poses, using modality-specific encoders to map them into a shared latent space. Leveraging large-scale reinforcement learning in the simulator, we train a single policy that achieves sim-to-real transfer across multiple motion reference modalities without modality-specific retraining. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 robot are conducted to evaluate the proposed framework. In simulation, the policy achieves a peak success rate of 98.42\% on an unseen test dataset, demonstrating its exceptional generalization capability. The code is available at https://github.com/Renforce-Dynamics/MultiModalWBC

</details>


### [185] [Teaching Robots to Say 'I Don't Know' : SENTINEL for Uncertainty-Aware SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04853)
*Abhishek S,Badrikanath Praharaj,Sreeram MV*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SENTINEL是一个无需训练和标签的可靠性评估框架，通过几何扫描统计与跨模态深度一致性，为低成本2D LiDAR提供诊断信号，在检测到扫描损坏时回退到轮式里程计，防止SLAM损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本2D LiDAR缺乏高端传感器用于诊断测量失败的光强度通道，但被广泛用于教育和预算机器人平台，存在无声的SLAM损坏风险。

Method: 结合基于几何的扫描统计与LiDAR和RGB-D相机之间的跨模态深度一致性，计算每帧扫描的可靠性分数（0-1），低于阈值时拒绝损坏扫描并回退到标定轮式里程计。

Result: 在配备RPLidar A2M12和Intel RealSense D435i的GEFIER R1机器人上，于185cm×245cm场地中五种表面条件下（玻璃、镜子、光面纸、混合镜子和光面纸）的可靠性图谱显示干净与故障情况清晰分离。

Conclusion: SENTINEL能有效为仅测距LiDAR提供诊断信号，所有验证均在真实硬件上完成，因为仿真中无法复现这些故障模式。

Abstract: Low-cost 2D LiDARs lack the intensity channel that higher-end sensors use to diagnose measurement failures, yet they are widely used on educational and budget robotics platforms. We present SENTINEL, a training - free, label - free reliability estimation framework that gives range - only LiDAR an effective diagnostic signal. SENTINEL combines geometry-based scan statistics with cross - modal depth consistency between LiDAR and an RGB - D camera to compute a per - scan reliability score between 0 and 1. When the score falls below a threshold, corrupted scans are rejected and the robot falls back to calibrated wheel odometry, preventing silent SLAM corruption.
