We evaluate SENTINEL on a GEFIER R1 four - wheel skid-steer robot equipped with an RPLidar A2M12 and an Intel RealSense D435i in a 185 cm by 245 cm arena containing controlled transparent and reflective failure elements on a central obstacle. Spatial reliability maps across five surface conditions, including glass, mirror, shiny paper, and a mixed mirror and shiny-paper condition, show clear separation between clean and failure cases, allowing affected regions to be identified as reject or noise. Because these failure modes are absent in simulation, validation is performed entirely on real hardware.

</details>


### [186] [WAM-Nav: Asymmetric Latent World-Action Modeling for Unified Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04907)
*Ning Yang,Yan Huang,Kaiwen Peng,Ziheng He,Kai Wang,Cui Miao,Kailin Lyu,Guo Li,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了WAM-Nav，一种基于潜在世界-动作模型的视觉导航方法，通过共享Diffusion Transformer联合生成长时域动作和短时域视觉预演，实现更鲁棒、更有预见性的导航决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式策略缺乏前瞻性推理，无法主动避障；传统模块化方法将场景预测与策略学习分离，导致误差累积和推理效率低下。

Method: 采用共享Diffusion Transformer进行非对称联合扩散，同时生成长时域动作和短时域视觉预演；引入双流上下文条件机制融合里程计历史与视觉观测；设计统一的目标对齐模块支持图像目标、点目标和无目标探索。

Result: 在ClutterScenes和InternScenes基准上，Image-Goal和Point-Goal导航成功率分别提升15.7%和3.3%；真实世界零样本仿真到现实迁移平均任务成功率达85%。

Conclusion: WAM-Nav通过联合学习动作生成与视觉预演，有效减少了推理延迟和视觉误差累积，在多个导航任务上取得显著性能提升，并展现了强大的零样本仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Visual navigation requires generating smooth and collision-free trajectories under complex geometric and physical constraints. Existing reactive policies that directly map observations to actions lack anticipatory reasoning, limiting their ability to proactively avoid obstacles. While visual imagination offers predictive foresight, conventional modular approaches separate scene prediction from policy learning, often leading to error accumulation and inefficient inference. To address these limitations, we propose WAM-Nav, a Latent World-Action Model for embodied visual navigation that jointly learns action generation and latent visual foresight, enabling more robust and foresighted navigation decisions without compromising inference efficiency. Specifically, WAM-Nav utilizes a shared Diffusion Transformer for asymmetric joint diffusion to concurrently generate long-horizon actions and short-horizon visual foresight, reducing the inference latency and visual error accumulation inherent in multi-step autoregressive rollouts. To further encourage smooth and consistent trajectory generation, we introduce a dual-stream contextual conditioning mechanism that integrates episode-level ego-motion history with sequential visual observations. Combined with a unified goal alignment module that preserves balanced representations across goal types, WAM-Nav naturally supports Image-Goal, Point-Goal, and No-Goal exploration within a single policy. Extensive experiments on the challenging ClutterScenes and InternScenes benchmarks demonstrate strong generalization of WAM-Nav, particularly on Image-Goal and Point-Goal navigation, where it improves success rates by 15.7% and 3.3%, respectively. Real-world deployment further validates effective zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, achieving an average 85% task success rate across diverse indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [187] [HORIZON: Recoverability-Governed Curriculum for Physical-Domain Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05143)
*Chenhao Bai,Liqin Lu,Kaijun Wang,Hui Chen,Jin-Chuan Shi,Yuyang Liu,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HORIZON框架，通过检查点前沿课程（checkpointed frontier curriculum）在策略可恢复边界内逐步扩展物理域，解决机器人策略在策略上训练中的物理域扩展问题。实验揭示了物理域扩展的三个规律：域直接扩展不均匀且需要分阶段排序；域组合非单调，过多域稀释可恢复样本；离线专家蒸馏无法替代在策略课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习训练机器人策略时，简单地扩大物理随机化范围并不有效，因为物理域的体验需要在整个训练过程中保持有序和可学习。作者想要研究策略何时能从更困难的物理域中获益，并识别出可恢复性（recoverability）是在策略物理域扩展中的核心约束。

Method: 提出HORIZON框架，核心是一个检查点前沿课程（checkpointed frontier curriculum），仅在当前策略的可恢复边界内扩展物理域。使用回滚（rollback）和边界细化（boundary refinement）来管理每一步扩展，将固定随机化转变为连续的物理域增长过程。

Result: 实验发现三个规律：1）直接域扩展跨物理轴不均匀且无分阶段排序则不可学习；2）域组合非单调，添加过多域会稀释可恢复联合样本，降低整体鲁棒性；3）离线蒸馏独立专家无法替代在策略课程产生的联合交互。

Conclusion: 物理域泛化应被视为具身控制的持续增长问题，可恢复性是在策略扩展的组织原则。

Abstract: Scaling robust robot policies requires more than broader randomization, because physical-domain experience must remain organized and learnable throughout training. We study when a policy can benefit from harder physics and identify recoverability as a central constraint in on-policy physical-domain scaling. In on-policy training, new dynamics are useful only insofar as they remain close enough to the current policy to generate corrective on-policy data, rather than collapsing rollouts into unrecoverable failures. Using quadruped locomotion as a physically demanding benchmark for embodied generalization, we introduce HORIZON, a checkpointed frontier curriculum that expands physical domains only within the current policy's recoverable boundary. HORIZON uses rollback and boundary refinement to govern each expansion step, turning fixed randomization into a continual process of physical-domain growth. Experiments reveal three regularities of physical-domain expansion. First, direct domain widening is uneven across physical axes and often unlearnable without staged ordering. Second, domain composition is non-monotonic, and adding more domains beyond a compact core can dilute recoverable joint samples and reduce overall robustness. Third, offline distillation of isolated experts cannot substitute for the joint interaction generated by on-policy curriculum. Together, these results frame physical-domain generalization as a continual growth problem for embodied control, with recoverability as the organizing principle for on-policy expansion.

</details>


### [188] [X4Val: Learning Neural Surrogates for Variance-Reduced Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05159)
*Rachel Luo,Michael Watson,Apoorva Sharma,Heng Yang,Han Qi,Edward Schmerling,Sushant Veer,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出X4Val框架，利用非配对的异构数据（仿真、历史日志等）通过共享表示空间和学习迁移预测器，结合控制变量估计器，在自动驾驶和机器人操作任务上实现高达38.4%的方差缩减，提升机器人系统评估的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界测试数据昂贵且有限，而仿真、历史策略日志等辅助数据虽丰富但与真实域存在分布差异，难以直接用于高置信度性能估计。现有方法对非配对、多域数据的利用缺乏原则性框架。

Method: 提出X4Val框架，包含三个关键步骤：(1) 将真实域和辅助域样本嵌入共享表示空间；(2) 学习可迁移的真实指标预测器；(3) 将该预测器作为控制变量纳入估计器，在无配对样本条件下实现方差缩减。

Result: 在自动驾驶和真实机器人操作任务上，X4Val实现最高38.4%的方差缩减，且持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 非配对的异构数据可以被原则性地用于提升机器人系统验证的样本效率，为低成本、高置信度的真实世界性能估计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Rigorous evaluation of learning-based robotic systems is an essential prerequisite for deployment. However, real-world test data is expensive to gather; moreover, in a typical iterative development context, data gathered from the latest policy is necessarily limited in scale. This motivates evaluation methodologies that make use of heterogeneous data sources, including simulation, historical policy logs, and data collected from related platforms or environments. While such auxiliary data are abundant and inexpensive, they are generally not directly representative of real-world outcomes -- for example, performance in simulation may differ substantially from performance in the real world -- making their principled use for high-confidence performance estimation challenging. In this paper, we introduce X4Val, a general framework for variance-reduced real-world metric estimation in the presence of non-paired, multi-domain data. X4Val embeds samples from real and auxiliary domains into a shared representation space and learns a transferable predictor of real-world metrics; this learned predictor is then incorporated into a control-variates estimator, enabling variance reduction even when paired samples are unavailable. We provide theoretical analysis and empirical evaluations on autonomous driving and real-world robot manipulation tasks, domains across which X4Val achieves up to 38.4% variance reduction and demonstrates consistent improvements over strong baselines. These results show that non-paired, heterogeneous data can be leveraged to substantially improve the sample efficiency of rigorous robotic system validation.

</details>


### [189] [GRAIL: Generating Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from 3D Assets and Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05160)
*Tianyi Xie,Haotian Zhang,Jinhyung Park,Zi Wang,Bowen Wen,Jiefeng Li,Xueting Li,Qingwei Ben,Haoyang Weng,Yufei Ye,David Minor,Tingwu Wang,Chenfanfu Jiang,Sanja Fidler,Jan Kautz,Linxi Fan,Yuke Zhu,Zhengyi Luo,Umar Iqbal,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAIL是一个完全虚拟的数字生成管线，通过组合3D资产、仿真场景和视频基础模型先验来合成人机交互数据，无需物理环境搭建或遥操作，并成功在Unitree G1人形机器人上实现了84%的物体拾取成功率和90%的爬楼梯成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的全身操纵（locomanipulation）需要大量跨不同物体、全身运动和场景几何的机器人兼容演示数据，但遥操作和动作捕捉难以规模化，因为每次数据采集都依赖物理设备、穿戴式演员和机器人操作。

Method: GRAIL保持完全虚拟直到部署阶段：组合3D资产、仿真就绪场景和视频基础模型先验来合成交互数据；从完全指定的3D配置（已知物体几何、相机参数、公制尺度、环境深度和机器人比例角色）出发进行视频生成和重建；通过基于模型的物体跟踪、人体运动估计和交互感知优化重建公制4D人-物交互轨迹；将恢复的运动重定目标到人形机器人，并训练任务通用跟踪器（物体感知潜在适配器和场景感知跟踪器）。

Result: 生成了超过20,000个序列，涵盖拾取、物体操纵、坐姿和地形遍历。仅使用GRAIL生成的数据训练的视觉策略，在Unitree G1人形机器人上实现了84%的物体拾取成功率和90%的爬楼梯成功率。

Conclusion: GRAIL证明了完全虚拟的数据生成管线可以有效替代物理遥操作数据采集，为大规模人形机器人技能学习提供可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling humanoid loco-manipulation requires robot-compatible demonstrations across diverse objects, whole-body motions, and scene geometries, but teleoperation and motion capture are difficult to scale because each collection depends on physical setups, instrumented actors, and robot operation. We present GRAIL, a digital generation pipeline that remains fully virtual until deployment: it composes 3D assets, simulator-ready scenes, and priors from video foundation models (VFMs) to synthesize interactions without rebuilding physical environments or teleoperating the robot. Rather than reconstructing unconstrained in-the-wild videos, GRAIL starts from fully specified 3D configurations in which object geometry, camera parameters, metric scale, environment depth, and a robot-proportioned character are known before video generation and reused during reconstruction. This privileged setup better conditions 4D recovery, allowing model-based object tracking, human motion estimation, and interaction-aware optimization to reconstruct metric 4D human-object interaction (HOI) trajectories with reduced depth ambiguity and morphology mismatch. We retarget the recovered motions to a humanoid robot and train complementary task-general trackers: an object-aware latent adaptor for manipulation and a scene-aware tracker for terrain traversal. GRAIL produces over 20,000 sequences spanning pick-up, object manipulation, sitting, and terrain traversal. Using only GRAIL-generated data, we train egocentric visual policies through a sim-to-real pipeline and deploy them on a Unitree G1 humanoid, achieving 84\% real-world success on diverse object pick-up and 90\% success on stair-climbing.

</details>


### [190] [PointAction: 3D Points as Universal Action Representations for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03943)
*Mutian Tong,Han Jiang,Qiao Feng,Lingjie Liu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointAction通过联合预测未来RGB帧和动态3D点图，将视频生成模型桥接到机器人动作执行，利用显式点云4D建模减少RGB-only动作歧义，实现跨任务和跨本体的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频-动作模型利用预训练视频扩散模型的视觉动态，但RGB-only视频预测缺乏度量3D运动、接触几何和细粒度空间约束，导致动作基础不明确；同时跨任务和本体的动作监督成本高昂。

Method: PointAction微调基础视频生成模型，联合预测未来RGB帧和动态3D点图（点级4D建模），生成时序一致的3D场景几何运动；将点动力学作为结构化、本体无关的动作接口，再通过扩散动作解码器映射为可执行机器人动作。

Result: 在机器人场景的4D生成质量上达到SOTA；在仿真中优于现有基线；能泛化到预训练中未见过的两个真实机器人臂。

Conclusion: PointAction通过显式度量3D点动力学作为视频预测与控制之间的接口，有效降低了RGB-only动作基础的不确定性，在有限动作监督下实现了跨任务和跨本体的迁移能力。

Abstract: Video-Action Models (VAMs) leverage the broad visual dynamics captured by pre-trained video diffusion models, offering a promising path toward generalizable robot manipulation. However, RGB-only video rollouts are not directly actionable: they leave metric 3D motion, contact geometry, and fine-grained spatial constraints under-specified, making action grounding ambiguous. Meanwhile, scaling action supervision across diverse tasks and embodiments remains costly. We present PointAction, a framework that bridges video predictions to robot actions through explicit point-based 4D modeling. PointAction fine-tunes a foundation video generation model to jointly predict future RGB frames and dynamic 3D pointmaps, producing temporally consistent 3D motion of task-relevant scene geometry. These point dynamics serve as a structured, embodiment-agnostic action interface, which a diffusion-based action decoder maps to executable robot actions. By using metric 3D point dynamics as the interface between video prediction and control, PointAction reduces the ambiguity of RGB-only action grounding and supports transfer across tasks and embodiments with limited action supervision. Experiments show that PointAction achieves state-of-the-art 4D generation quality on robot scenes, outperforms existing baselines in simulation, and generalizes to two real robot arms unseen during pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Beyond Static Priors: Dynamic Neural Guidance for Large-Scale Ant Colony Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04039)
*Dat Thanh Tran,Van Khu Vu,Yining Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: DyNACO提出了一种动态神经引导的蚁群优化框架，通过周期性观察信息素分布和当前最优解来调整策略，解决了传统神经引导ACO中训练与推理不匹配的问题。在TSP上可扩展至10万节点，优于神经基线方法并减少运行时间；在CVRP上以不到1%的神经开销持续改进无引导基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经引导蚁群优化存在根本性的训练-推理不匹配问题：策略通常训练生成静态先验（如热图），但部署时却需要引导迭代的、长时程的搜索过程。

Method: 提出DyNACO框架，通过周期性观察信息素分布和当前最优解实现动态神经引导；结合基于扰动的ACO后端和范围受限的精炼机制，确保效果和稳定的信用分配。

Result: 在TSP上可扩展至10万节点实例，优于神经基线方法，且相比无引导求解器常能减少总运行时间；在CVRP上通过容量感知后端，以不到1%的神经开销持续改进无引导基线。

Conclusion: 验证了动态引导优于静态先验的结论，强调了在学习的优化中将神经训练与迭代搜索动态对齐的必要性。

Abstract: Neural-guided Ant Colony Optimization (ACO) suffers from a fundamental training-inference misalignment: policies are typically trained to generate static priors (e.g., heatmaps), yet deployed to guide iterative, long-horizon search processes. In this paper, we present DyNACO, a novel framework that achieves dynamic neural guidance by periodically observing the pheromone distribution and the incumbent solution. To make DyNACO tractable at scale, we pair the policy with a perturbation-based ACO backend and a scope-restricted refinement mechanism that jointly ensure efficacy and stable credit assignment. On TSP, DyNACO scales to 100,000-node instances and outperforms neural baselines while often reducing total runtime compared to the unguided solver. We extend DyNACO to CVRP via a capacity-aware backend, consistently improving the unguided baseline with less than 1% neural overhead. We further provide in-depth analysis validating the model's generalization capabilities and elucidating why dynamic guidance outperforms static priors. Our work underscores the necessity of aligning neural training with iterative search dynamics in learning-guided optimization. The code is available at https://github.com/shoraaa/DyNACO.

</details>


### [192] [U-Net-Accelerated Quality-Diversity Optimization for Climate-Adaptive Urban Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04658)
*Alexander Hagg,Tania Guerrero,Dirk Reith*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出用空间深度学习替代传统标量高斯过程作为MAP-Elites离线质量多样性算法的代理模型，用于城市布局的气候适应优化。U-Net空间代理只需随机采样训练即可达到高精度（R²=0.996），而标量GP在随机样本上完全失效。该流水线部署在OpenSKIZZE工具中，能在10分钟内生成数千个多样化的、经气候评估的建筑布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于物理的气候模拟计算成本高昂，规划者通常只能评估少于十种人工设计的布局，缺乏系统性探索设计空间的能力。需要一种高效的代理模型来替代慢速的物理模拟器，以便将质量多样性（QD）算法应用于城市布局优化。

Method: 将空间深度学习代理（U-Net）整合到离线MAP-Elites循环中，替代基于物理的模拟器。系统比较了U-Net与标量高斯过程（GP）代理在不同训练数据策略（准随机Sobol采样 vs. 主动QD引导采样）下的表现。

Result: U-Net空间代理无论训练数据来源如何，都能稳健学习底层物理映射（R²=0.996）；标量GP在随机样本上完全失效，需要昂贵的主动生成QD存档才能泛化。U-Net仅需一次性随机样本即可实现离线QD优化，达到高精度适应度排序（ρ=0.994）。

Conclusion: 空间归纳偏置（U-Net）比传统标量代理更适合物理模拟的替代任务，能够大幅降低离线QD优化的数据获取成本。该流水线在OpenSKIZZE中部署，使规划者能在10分钟内获得数千个多样化且经过气候评估的建筑布局方案。

Abstract: Optimizing urban layouts for climate adaptation requires balancing building density with cold-air ventilation. Because physics-based climate simulations are computationally expensive, planners typically evaluate fewer than ten manual designs. \gls{qd} algorithms offer a way to systematically illuminate the design space, but they require surrogate models to be practical.
  In this paper, we replace a slow, regulatory physics simulator with a spatial deep-learning surrogate (U-Net) inside an offline MAP-Elites loop. We systematically compare this spatial approach with a traditional \gls{gp} surrogate across different training-data strategies (quasi-random Sobol sampling vs.\ active \gls{qd} bootstrapping).
  Our results reveal that scalar \gls{gp} surrogates fail catastrophically when trained on random samples, requiring expensive, actively generated \gls{qd} archives to generalize. In contrast, the spatial inductive bias of the U-Net allows it to learn the underlying physics mapping robustly ($R^2 = 0.996$), completely independent of the training data source. This allows offline \gls{qd} optimization to achieve highly accurate fitness rankings ($ρ= 0.994$) using only a one-time batch of random training samples. The resulting pipeline, deployed in the open-source OpenSKIZZE tool, generates thousands of diverse, climate-evaluated building layouts in under ten minutes.

</details>
